# Epi briefing — where we are on the questions you asked

**One-line status:** We can answer **how to use the tables** for every Epi question today (methodology +
worked examples, grounded in Optum's own data dictionary). The **coverage numbers** — what fraction of
patients actually have each variable — are **one data-run away**: the profiling code is written and
schema-grounded, but no numbers exist until it runs against a slice.

**Lead with this:** *"For every question on the Epi-needs slide we have a recommended approach and the
tables/fields — now resolved against the deployed schema. The **direct-variable coverage** is near run-ready
(needs data access + the biomarker LOINC value-set). The **case-study attrition, six-source overlap, and
EHR-vs-claims discordance are not yet trusted** — the case notebooks are demos and the claims-union /
concordance work isn't built — so those need build + validation, not just a data run."*

---

## 1. The Epi-needs questions (deck p.1) — status

✅ = answerable now (we can walk Epi through it) · 🟡 = approach defined, **number needs a data run** · 🔴 = needs an external answer

| Epi-needs question | Status | Where / what's pending |
|---|:--:|---|
| Patient identification — ICD-10 + histological confirmation | ✅ | playbook + mapping; ≥2 dx + EO histology rule |
| Staging (TNM, FIGO) | ✅ | `onc_stage` / `onc_tnm`; FIGO = stage extent (not grade) |
| Outcomes — PFS, ORR, progression | ✅ | response + progression tables; PFS = curation |
| Outcomes — **OS / mortality** | 🟡 | source confirmed: `patient.date_of_death` (`YYYYMM`) + `deceased_indicator`; open = **completeness / fitness for OS** + month-only granularity |
| Working with response & progression data | ✅ | playbook (recall caveat ≈0.63 on progression) |
| Working with SDS / NLP-derived elements | ✅ | playbook; NLP recall caveats documented |
| Continuum of care — IDN flag, claims linkage | ✅ | method ready; IDN-flag specifics to confirm |
| LoT rules | ✅ | supplied LoT is **IV-only**; custom LoT = curation |
| Oral / maintenance / surgery / neoadj / adjuvant | ✅ | `Oncology_Medications` (oral+IV); timing-based derivations |
| Discordance — EHR vs claims, table vs table | ✅ 🟡 | method ready; **rates need the data run** |
| Tokenization — possible? which vendors? | 🔴 | **only Optum/vendor can answer** |

**Takeaway for the room:** the "how" column is essentially done. Every 🟡/🔴 is a *data* or *external*
dependency, not a design gap.

---

## 2. The use-case / coverage questions (Sample Query deck) — status

| Question | Status | Note |
|---|:--:|---|

| HPV testing coverage | 🟡 | expected **<1%** off-the-shelf — reproduce + show curation lift |
| Smoking / alcohol coverage | 🟡 | expected smoking **~50%**, alcohol much lower |

| Severity / staging — single source of truth, correlation? | 🟡 | staging source-coverage + true overlap query ready |

These are **the variable-availability matrix**. We have the matrix with *expected* ratings; the Databricks
`notebooks/` turn them into measured %s.

  examples: a query per concept + feasibility commentary. The full 6-cohort × attrition build is an

## 4. What we can show Epi tomorrow (already in the repo)

- **Best-practice playbook** — one section per Epi-needs question (approach, tables, codes, caveats).
- **Question→table mapping** — every variable → table/field → derivation → source → caveat.
- **Data-model reference** — the asset, keys, NLP performance, methodology rules.

  question. These are the Databricks notebooks that produce the numbers to show Epi, once pointed at a data slice.
- **Variable-availability matrix** — the GSK p.7 summary, off-the-shelf vs curation.
- **Two case-study worked examples.**

**Credibility point:** we know what "right" looks like — the coverage run is sanity-checked against known
anchors (HPV <1%, smoking ~50%, tumour size ~6%). If those land, the rest of the numbers are trustworthy.

---

## 5. What we need FROM Epi to produce the numbers (the ask)

1. **Data access + a test slice.** The schema is already known — `hive_metastore.clnprw_optum_mc_full_use`,
   cut `2025q4` (from `optum data location.pdf`); we just need the warehouse `HOST`/`HTTP_PATH` and a slice to run.
   This is the single biggest unblocker.
2. **Clinical sign-off** on the draft code lists (ICD/biomarker/drug value sets).
3. **Decisions:** OS completeness/fitness (source is confirmed) · tokenization vendors · 3P-claims identity ·
   the **biomarker LOINC value-set** (the `lab.LOINC` column itself is confirmed) · the a/b cohort-split
   definition · LoT line-gap thresholds (in `docs/optum-gsk-questions.md`).
4. **Priorities:** which tumours / variables matter most, so we run those denominators first.

  confirmed, and all referenced table names now resolve against the deployed schema (`optum data location.pdf`).
  What remains is column-level (a few `# TODO` predicates, caught by the notebook preflight) and the biomarker
  LOINC value-set.
- **Numbers: zero produced yet, by necessity — no data access.** The code is written and schema-mapped; the
  notebook preflight + a column check come before the first run.
- **Critical path:** test slice → preflight column checks → run coverage → validate against anchors → populate
  the matrix and case-study counts. Days of work once data lands, not weeks.

# How this was built — methodology & derivation (stakeholder explainer)

A plain-language account of **where every design choice comes from**, so the work can be explained and
defended. The short version: the *asks* come from the GSK decks, the *data model and rules* come from the
Optum data dictionary, and the *code lists* (ICD/CPT/biomarker terms) are illustrative starting points from
standard clinical coding that still need sign-off. Nothing here has been run against live data yet, so every
**number** is still pending; this doc is about the **method**.

---

## 1. The three things everything derives from

| Source | What we took from it |
|---|---|
| **GSK decks** — `Questions to Answer/optum mc.pdf`, `Sample Query.pdf` | The *questions*: the Epi-needs list, the two case studies, the priority-tumour list, the "key data variables" matrix, and the coverage findings to reproduce (HPV <1%, smoking ~50%). |
| **Optum data dictionary** — `Optum Data Dict/optum enriched oncology manual.pdf`, the column-level data dict (`optum data dict/`), `optum data location.pdf`, the entity-relationship PDF, + the NLP training deck | The *data model*: the tables, how they join (the `neoplasm_histology_key`), NLP precision/recall, and the documented methodology rules. The manual's worked SQL (§4.8.2) gave the join patterns; the **data location PDF** gave the real deployed schema/catalog/table names (so the notebooks point at actual tables, not placeholders); the **column-level dict** confirmed demographics/mortality live in `patient` and enrollment in `clm_derived_enrollment`. |
| **Standard clinical coding** — ICD-10, CPT/HCPCS, biomarker (HGNC) nomenclature | The *code lists* used to define each cohort and variable. These are illustrative and are the part that needs clinical sign-off. |

So when a stakeholder asks "where did X come from?", X is almost always traceable to one of these three.

---

## 2. How the tumour cohorts were derived

**Which tumours.** The priority list is taken directly from the GSK deck (`optum mc.pdf` p.6): ovarian,
endometrial, colorectal, prostate, multiple myeloma, NSCLC, HNSCC, sarcoma, bladder (+ a broader extension
list). Nine are active in `notebooks/_config_tumours.py` (the priority list with NSCLC/SCLC split off the shared
lung ICD); **sarcoma is held out** until it has a histology definition (the bone/soft-tissue ICD cohort alone would
pull in non-sarcoma malignancies).

**How a patient enters a cohort.** A patient is counted as having a cancer when they have **≥2 confirmed
diagnoses on separate dates** — the standard real-world-evidence rule for reducing rule-out/false-positive
coding. "Confirmed" uses the manual's own diagnosis fields (`diagnosis_status = 'Diagnosis of'`,
`diagnosis_cd_type LIKE 'ICD%'`).

**The ICD code lists** for each tumour come from that cancer's ICD-10 range (e.g. ovary `C56`/`C57`, colon
`C18–C20`, bladder `C67`, prostate `C61`). They are **illustrative** and flagged for clinical sign-off
throughout.

**Site-defined vs histology-defined tumours.** This distinction is derived from the manual's own NSCLC
example (§4.8.2). For most cancers the ICD code defines the cohort (ovarian, colorectal, …). But for some —
**NSCLC, SCLC, HNSCC** — the ICD code can't tell the subtype apart (e.g. `C34` is *both* NSCLC and SCLC), so
those cohorts additionally require a **histology** match (small-cell vs squamous vs adenocarcinoma) anchored
to the same tumour concept. In the config these are marked `histology_dependent=True`.

**Why counts are pinned to the target tumour.** Optum's Enriched Oncology links each tumour's stage, TNM,
grade, etc. to a concept key (`neoplasm_histology_key`); the manual (§2.1) says biomarker, lymph node and
performance-status tables are *stand-alone* instead. We follow that exactly: keyed variables are counted
only on the **target** tumour's concept, so a patient who also has another cancer can't lend that other
cancer's stage/TNM to the count. This is the single most important correctness rule in the build.

---

## 3. How the coverage approach was derived

**The matrix.** GSK's "Summary of the key data variables included in the database" slide (`optum mc.pdf`
p.7) asked for a captured / partially-captured / not-captured summary with **one row for off-the-shelf
oncology data and another for curation**. That slide *is* `docs/variable-availability-matrix.md`.

**The coverage number.** For each variable we count **distinct patients who actually have it populated**,
divided by the cohort — a simple, defensible profiling metric (`count(distinct ptid)`), not a model.

**Off-the-shelf vs curation.** The deck's two rows become two measurements: the variable from the Enriched
Oncology table *alone* (off-the-shelf) vs after triangulating with structured Labs + diagnosis codes
(curation). This quantifies the value of curation, e.g. HPV jumps from <1% off-the-shelf once Labs and
diagnosis codes are added.

**Sanity checks.** The Optum use-case document reports a few known figures — **tumour size ≈6%, HPV/p16
<1%, smoking ≈50%, alcohol much lower**. We use these as validation anchors: if a run lands far from them,
the table/column mapping is wrong.

**Small-cell suppression.** Counts of 1–10 patients are blanked (`MIN_CELL=11`) and shown as `<11`, with
true zeros kept distinct — standard output governance, matching the team's existing Databricks style.

---

## 4. How the case studies were derived

Both come straight from the GSK case-study slides (`optum mc.pdf` p.2–4):

- **Case A (colon dMMR/MSI-H)** — the 6-step cohort and the 6 data-source definitions are exactly as drawn
  on the slide. The clinical rules baked in (distant-metastasis exclusion using `C78–C79` and *keeping*
  regional `C77`; the peri-operative staging window) are derived from clinical convention for a surgical
  locally-advanced cohort and flagged for confirmation.
- **Case B (ovarian LoT / platinum)** — the strata (total, neoadjuvant/adjuvant, 1L, 2L+, platinum-sensitive
  vs -resistant) are the slide's ask. The decision to **rebuild line-of-therapy** rather than use Optum's
  supplied one is traced to a documented limitation in the manual: the supplied Line-of-Therapy table is
  built from structured Procedure data and is **IV-only**, so it misses oral therapy and has no
  neoadjuvant/adjuvant flag.

Both run as Databricks notebooks (`notebooks/caseA_…`, `notebooks/caseB_…`); the original parameterised SQL
is kept as the appendix `sql/reference_pipeline/`.

---

## 5. Provenance & confidence — at a glance

| Element | Derived from | Confidence |
|---|---|---|
| Priority-tumour list | GSK deck p.6 | **Given** by the client |
| Cohort entry (≥2 confirmed dx) | RWE convention + manual diagnosis fields | **High** (standard) |
| Confirmed table/column names (`onc_stage`, `onc_tnm`, `diag`, …) | Manual §4.8.2 worked SQL | **Confirmed** from Optum docs |
| Keyed vs stand-alone model | Manual §2.1 | **Confirmed** from Optum docs |
| Histology-dependent rule (NSCLC/SCLC/HNSCC) | Manual §4.8.2 NSCLC example | **Confirmed** approach; histology terms illustrative |
| Coverage metric + matrix structure | GSK deck p.7 | **High** |
| Validation anchors (6% / <1% / ~50%) | Optum use-case findings | **Reference** figures to check against |
| **ICD / CPT / biomarker code lists** | Standard coding (ICD-10, CPT/HCPCS, HGNC) | **Illustrative — needs clinical sign-off** |
| LoT gap / platinum-free thresholds | Clinical convention | **Illustrative — needs sign-off** |
| EO/MC table names | **Confirmed** against the deployed catalog (`optum data location.pdf`) | ✅ resolved — all `t_<name>_2025q4` |
| OS / mortality source | **Confirmed** — `patient.date_of_death` (`YYYYMM`) + `deceased_indicator` (data dict) | ✅ resolved (month-grained) |
| A few EO column predicates | Best-effort (not in §4.8.2) | **Confirm columns on the instance** |
| Tokenization, 3P-claims identity | — | **External** — only Optum/GSK can confirm |

---

## 6. What still needs confirmation before the numbers are trusted

1. **Clinical sign-off** on the code lists (ICD, histology/site terms, biomarker strings, drug lists).
2. **Schema mapping** — ✅ *table* names are resolved against the deployed catalog
   `hive_metastore.clnprw_optum_mc_full_use` (cut `2025q4`); what remains is a handful of *column-level*
   predicates flagged `# TODO` in the notebooks.
3. **Thresholds & definitions** with GSK (LoT line gaps, platinum-free interval, peri-operative/adjuvant
   windows, the a/b strict-cohort split).
4. **External answers** from Optum — see `docs/optum-gsk-questions.md` (mortality **completeness/fitness**
   (source confirmed), tokenization vendors, third-party-claims identity, the **biomarker LOINC value-set**
   (the `lab.LOINC` column itself is confirmed), etc.).
5. **A test-slice run** to validate against the anchors.

---

## 7. Where each piece lives (doc map)

- **The asset & rules** → `docs/data-model-reference.md` (tables, keys, NLP performance, methodology).
- **How to use each table** (per Epi question) → `docs/best-practice-playbook.md`.
- **Every variable → table/field → derivation** → `docs/question-to-table-mapping.md`.
- **What coverage means + how to read it** → `docs/coverage-assessment.md`.
- **The runnable code** → `notebooks/` (coverage, Epi counts, Case A/B).
- **The open external questions** → `docs/optum-gsk-questions.md`.
- **The full change history** (every fix, every round) → `docs/review-notes.md`.

# Questions for Optum / GSK (Phase-4 gate)

These are the items that **cannot be resolved from the data dictionary** and block execution or
final clinical correctness. Grouped by owner. Each maps to a flag in `docs/review-notes.md`.

## For Optum (data/licensing)

1. ~~**Production schema & version**~~ **RESOLVED** (`optum data location.pdf`): one schema
   `hive_metastore.clnprw_optum_mc_full_use`, cut `2025q4`, tables `t_<name>_2025q4`. *Remaining:* the
   **cut/versioning cadence** (when does `2025q4` → `2026q1`, and is the old cut retained?).
2. **Mortality / death** — death IS available: `patient.date_of_death` (**`YYYYMM`**, month-grained) +
   `deceased_indicator` (SS Death Index / provider-reported). *Remaining (the real question):* its
   **completeness / lag / fitness for OS**, given month-only granularity. Gates **OS** (`best-practice-playbook.md §7`).
3. **Tokenization** — does the licensed instance ship patient tokens, and via which vendor
   (Datavant or other)? Which token types/keys? (Not addressed anywhere in the materials.)
4. **Third-party claims** — identity and structure of the "third-party Medical & Rx claims" used in
   the Case Study A/B cohort definitions (3a/3b). Field/linkage differences vs Optum claims, and the
   **third-party enrollment / observable-time** table (the strict 3b gate must use *3P* enrollment, not Optum's).
4b. **`onc_medications` scope** — is the Oncology Medications table licensable/queryable for the
   **non-EO cohorts (1–3)**? If not, the structured-med source for those cohorts must come from the raw
   EHR/claims med tables rather than from `onc_medications.source` filtering. Also confirm the exact
   `source` and `action` value spellings.
5. ~~**LOINC availability**~~ **RESOLVED**: the structured `lab` table exposes a **`LOINC`** column (+ `TEST_NAME`)
   per the data dict (the *claims* `clm_lab_results` table uses `LOINC_CD`). *Remaining (the real ask):* the
   **biomarker LOINC value-set** to filter on — provide the appendix **§4.8.3 biomarker-lab list** / LOINC codes.
6. **Reference Guide & Data Dictionary** — full field lists/value sets and the Optum-mapped-concepts
   data dictionary (deck p.14), plus the **Cancer ICD-9/10 inclusion list** (appendix §4.8.4).
7. **Continuous enrollment / IDN** — tables identified (`clm_co_enrollment` is month-grained
   `ACTIVITY_YYYYMM` + `CLM_RX_ENROLL`/`CLM_MED_ENROLL`; `clm_derived_enrollment` has period `eligeff`/`eligend`;
   `pat_obs_time`). Open: the agreed **observable-window definition** (which table + washout→follow-up rule) and the
   `patient.idn_indicator` semantics for continuity-of-care gating. (Today the notebooks only check enrollment-row
   presence, labelled "has enrollment record".)
8. **AJCC edition** — confirmed not extracted by NLP; acceptable for the staging endpoints, or is an
   edition needed for any analysis?

## For GSK (study/clinical)

9. **Cohort a/b split** — define **2a vs 2b** and **3a vs 3b** in the Case Study A/B attrition tables
   (modeled here as broad vs strict-with-continuous-enrollment — confirm or replace).
10. **Clinical code-list sign-off** — review/extend the DRAFT value sets in `sql/reference_pipeline/code_lists.sql`:
    cancer ICD anchors, colectomy/cytoreductive CPT/PCS, IO/platinum/PARP/maintenance generics &
    J-codes, biosimilar map, dMMR/MSI/HPV lab set.
11. **LoT thresholds** — confirm the regimen window (30 d), discontinuation gap (90 d), and the
    **platinum-sensitivity threshold** (modeled as **>183 days**; some protocols use 6 calendar months).
12. **Endpoint definitions** — agree operational definitions for **PFS** (progression composite) and
    **response/ORR** (radiological response narrative vs RECIST), and the **maintenance** definition
    (modeled: a line whose regimen is wholly PARP/bevacizumab and which is *excluded* from the true
    2L+ count).
13. **Metastatic exclusion (Case A)** — confirm "no metastatic diagnosis" = **distant only** (C78–C79),
    retaining regional nodal (C77) disease for the locally advanced cohort.
14. **Platinum-sensitivity anchor (ovarian 2L+)** — confirm the clock starts at the last platinum dose of
    the platinum line **preceding the 2nd treatment line** (as modeled) vs the **latest platinum exposure**
    overall (which a later rechallenge could shift).
15. **Total OC definition (Case B)** — confirm Total OC is **ICD-identified for all six cohorts** with
    histology as an enrichment metric (as modeled), rather than histology-confirmed-only.
16. **Strict-cohort eligibility window (2b/3b)** — the current gate keeps each row whose date falls in an
    enrollment span (**event-level**). Define the **patient-level continuous-observable window** the strict
    cohorts must satisfy (e.g., enrolled from `dx − washout` through `last-followup` / progression
    assessment) so under-observed patients aren't admitted to "strict" counts.
17. **Peri-operative staging window (Case A)** — confirm the stage-eligibility window
    `[first_dx − 30 d, surgery + 90 d]` (chosen to include the post-op **pathological** stage); adjust the
    +90 d bound if a different restaging cut-off is preferred.
18. **Adjuvant window (Case B)** — confirm "adjuvant" = confirmed therapy within **≤180 d after primary
    cytoreduction** (provisional, to exclude late recurrence treatment); set the agreed cut-off.

# Question → Table → Field Mapping

> For each GSK variable/ask: where it lives, how to derive it, which layer is authoritative,
> and the capture caveat. Field names per **`docs/data-model-reference.md`**; rationale in
> **`docs/best-practice-playbook.md`**. Physical table names are **resolved** to the deployed catalog
> `hive_metastore.clnprw_optum_mc_full_use` (cut `2025q4`, tables `t_<name>_2025q4` — see data-model-reference §2.1);
> a few column spellings remain to confirm on a slice.
>
> **Layer legend:** `EHR` = structured EHR · `EO` = Enriched Oncology off-the-shelf · `EO-cur` =
> Enriched Oncology curation/derived (your business rule) · `CLM` = Market Clarity claims.
> **Capture legend:** ✅ high · 🟡 partial / recall-limited · 🟧 derivable-only · ⛔ not in asset.

**Phase-4 metric to populate (per variable, on data access):** for every row below, report
**capture %** (non-missing), **source-concordance %** (agreement across layers, e.g. NLP vs Labs/ICD),
**missing %**, and a **not-assessable** flag where the variable can't be evaluated for that cohort.
These become the quantitative fill for the availability matrix.

---

## 1. Cohort entry & identification

| Variable | Primary source (table.field) | Corroborate with | Derivation | Layer | Capture |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Cancer diagnosis (entry) | `Diagnosis.diagnosis_cd` (ICD-9/10), `diagnosis_cd_type LIKE 'ICD%'`, `diagnosis_status='Diagnosis of'` | Cancer ICD inclusion list (appendix §4.8.4); claims `Claims_Med_Diagnosis` | ≥2 codes on separate encounters; exclude rule-outs | EHR / CLM | ✅ |
| Histological confirmation | `onc_neoplasm_histology.histology`, `.histology_qualifier='actual'` | structured Labs / pathology | join on `PTID`; limit to target cancer | EO | 🟡 (recall ≈0.82) |
| Neoplasm type / primary site | `onc_neoplasm_histology.neoplasm_type`, `.direction`, `.neoplasm_qualifier` | Diagnosis ICD; Metastasis Location | limit to target; resolve multi-neoplasm (manual §4.2.2) | EO | 🟡 |
| Diagnosis date (index) | `onc_neoplasm_histology.neoplasm_dx_date` | earliest `Diagnosis` date | fallback to structured if null (manual §4.3.2) | EO / EHR | 🟡 |

---

## 2. Demographics & patient characteristics (ovarian case study)

| Variable | Primary source | Derivation | Layer | Capture |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Age at diagnosis | `Patient` (birth year) + index `neoplasm_dx_date` | dx_year − birth_year | EHR | ✅ |
| Sex | `patient.gender` | direct | EHR | ✅ |
| Race / ethnicity | `Patient` race/ethnicity | direct | EHR | 🟡 (varies) |
| BMI | `NLP_Measurement` (height/weight/BMI) + structured `Observations` | compute from height/weight if BMI absent | EHR / EO | 🟡 |
| ECOG / performance status | `Evaluation_System` (ECOG 0–5, Karnofsky) | latest pre-treatment value; QC range 0–5 | EO | 🟡 |
| Smoking status | `NLP_SDS` + `Observations` + dx codes `Z87.891/F17/Z72.0` | never/former/current; else ever/unknown | EHR / EO | 🟡 (~50%) |
| Alcohol status | `NLP_SDS` + `Observations` + dx `F10/Z72.1` | ever/unknown | EHR / EO | 🟧 (high missingness) |
| Continuous enrollment / observable time | `Claims_Continuous_Enrollment`, `Patient_Observable_Time` | gate longitudinal windows | CLM | ✅ |

---

## 3. Staging & severity (no single source — combine)

| Variable | Primary source | Corroborate with | Derivation | Layer | Capture |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Stage group (0–IV) | `onc_stage.stage`, `.stage_prefix` | TNM, Characteristics, mets ICD | prefer pathological; most-advanced corroborated at dx | EO | 🟡 |
| TNM | `onc_tnm.t/n/m` (+ prefix/suffix) | Stage table (may overlap) | parse prefix (c/p/y…); **AJCC edition N/A** | EO | 🟡 (TNM PPV 0.96/rec 0.81) |
| **FIGO stage** (gyn, extent I–IV) | `onc_stage.stage` near `neoplasm_dx_date` | histology + characteristic | gyn stage group; **not** the grade | EO | 🟡 |
| **FIGO grade** (gyn, differentiation) | grading-systems / `Evaluation_System` where `SYSTEM_NAME='FIGO'` | — | distinct from FIGO stage | EO | 🟡 |
| Grade | `Grade.grade`; grading-systems `SYSTEM_NAME` (Gleason/Nottingham/Oncotype Dx) | — | normalized 1–4 + narrative | EO | 🟡 |
| Tumor characteristic | `onc_characteristic` (benign/in situ/malignant/metastatic) | Stage, mets | advanced-disease corroboration | EO | 🟡 |
| Metastasis (distant) | `Metastasis_Location` + Diagnosis mets ICD | direction in neoplasm table | **distant only**; ICD confirms primary/secondary | EO / EHR | 🟡 (mets rec ≈0.60) |
| Lymph nodes | `Lymph_Node.lymph_numeric` (# +), `.lymph_numeric_scale` (# examined), `.lymph_narrative` | TNM N | N+ if positive nodes / narrative positive | EO | 🟡 |
| Tumor size | `onc_tumor_size.tumor_size_1/2/3` (+ units) | path/surgery context | up to 3 dims mm/cm | EO | 🟧 (~6% of pts) |
| Surgical margin | `Surgical_Margin.margin_narrative` (pos/neg), `.margin_distance` | Procedure (surgery) | positive = cells at edge (no distance) | EO | 🟡 |

---

## 4. Biomarkers

| Variable | Primary source | Corroborate with | Derivation | Layer | Capture |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Biomarker result (general) | `Biomarker.biomarker` (HGNC), `.narrative_result`, `.numeric_result`, `.test_name`, `.gene_characteristics` | **structured Labs** — `lab.LOINC` (confirmed) + lab name/result; value-set from appendix §4.8.3 list | read **both** tables; coalesce Labs dates | EO + EHR | 🟡 |
| dMMR / MSI-H | `Biomarker` rows for MLH1/MSH2/MSH6/PMS2 (mutation/loss) or MSI-H | Labs (MMR IHC / MSI PCR / NGS) | positive if MSI-H **or** any MMR-gene defect (manual §4.1.3) | EO + EHR | 🟡 |
| HPV / p16 status | `Biomarker` {HPV, p16/CDKN2A}.`narrative_result` | Labs (HPV DNA/RNA, p16 IHC — via `lab.LOINC`, confirmed); dx `B97.7` | positive-if-any hierarchy; report unknown fraction | EO + EHR | 🟧 (<1% NLP) |
| PD-L1 | `Biomarker` PD-L1 with assay clone in `test_name` (22C3/28-8/SP142) | Labs | per-panel row; record assay | EO + EHR | 🟡 |

---

## 5. Treatment, Line of Therapy, surgery

| Variable | Primary source | Corroborate with | Derivation | Layer | Capture |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Drug exposure (incl. **oral**) | `Oncology_Medications` (`drug_name`, `generic_desc`, `drug_class`, `action`, `action_date`, `date_from_note`, `route`, `source`) | Med Admin, Written Rx, Procedure J-codes, `Claims_Rx`/`Claims_Med_Services` | match generic/NDC/HCPCS; PAID claims only | EO + EHR + CLM | ✅/🟡 |
| Line of Therapy (supplied) | `Line_of_Therapy` (`regimen_name`, `lot`, dates, intervals) | NCCN regimens | **IV-only, no oral, no dx-match** | EO | 🟡 |
| **Custom LoT** (oral+IV) | `Oncology_Medications` → regimen build (30-day/90-day windows) | supplied LoT | curation per playbook §6 | EO-cur | 🟧 |
| Neoadjuvant vs adjuvant | systemic therapy dates vs **surgery date** (Procedure) | Surgical Margin / Tumor Size | before surgery = neoadj; after = adj | EO-cur / EHR | 🟧 |
| Maintenance therapy | regimen composition + duration after induction | — | single-agent continuation rule | EO-cur | 🟧 |
| Surgery (incl. cytoreductive) | `Procedure` (CPT/HCPCS/ICD-proc) | Surgical Margin, Tumor Size | cancer-directed resection codes | EHR | ✅/🟡 |
| Platinum sensitivity (ovarian 2L+) | **last platinum administration** date (custom LoT) + first progression after it | Progression table | sensitive **>183 d**; resistant **≤183 d** (2L+ patients only) | EO-cur | 🟧 |
| IO use (Case A step 6) | `Oncology_Medications` IO agents (J-codes/generic) | Procedure, claims | any IO after dx, before metastatic dx | EO + EHR + CLM | 🟡 |

---

## 6. Outcomes

| Variable | Primary source | Corroborate with | Derivation | Layer | Capture |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| ORR (response) | `Treatment_Response.response` | structured meds/procedures (no drug names in table) | stack + sort by PTID+date to attribute | EO | 🟡 |
| Progression event | `onc_progression.progression` IN ('progression','recurrence'), `progression_temporal_status='current'`; date = `note_date` | stage change, new mets, size trend, tx switch | composite signal | EO + EO-cur | 🟡 (rec ≈0.63) |
| PFS | progression event **or** death − treatment start | — | curation per playbook §7 | EO-cur | 🟧 |
| OS / mortality | `patient.date_of_death` (YYYYMM) + `deceased_indicator` | — (source confirmed) | from index to death | EHR/linked | 🟡 (completeness/fitness TBD) |
| HCRU | claims (`Claims_Med_Services`, `Claims_Inpatient_Confinement`, `Claims_Rx`) | EHR encounters | utilization/cost from claims | CLM | ✅ |
| Comorbidities / safety background rates | `Diagnosis` + `NLP_SDS` (+ `Problem`) | — | code lists + SDS, temporal/qualifier filtered | EHR + EO | 🟡 |

## 7. Continuity & discordance (analytic outputs, not single fields)

| Ask | Source / method | Layer | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| Continuum of care | `Claims_Continuous_Enrollment` + `Patient_Observable_Time` + **IDN flag** | CLM | gate longitudinality; IDN field to confirm |
| Claims ↔ EHR linkage | `PTID` (+ `PAT_PLANID`) | EHR+CLM | universal key |
| EHR-vs-claims discordance | concept presence/value compared per `PTID`+date window | EHR vs CLM | the 6-source Case A design |
| Table-vs-table discordance | Stage vs TNM vs Characteristic; NLP histology vs ICD; Biomarker vs Labs | EO vs EHR | report overlap/κ; no ground truth |
| Tokenization | **not in docs** | — | open question for Optum (Datavant?) |

# Review notes & Phase-4 readiness

Captures an external design review of the work products and how each item was dispositioned.
Use the three buckets as the gate before anyone runs the SQL against live data.

---

## ✅ Ready (reviewable as a feasibility/design package)

- Four data-layer framing (structured EHR · EO off-the-shelf · EO curation · claims).
- The **recall caveat** ("absence ≠ negative"; high precision, moderate recall) carried throughout.
- **Staging triangulation** concept (no single source of truth; precedence hierarchy).
- **Biomarker + Labs dual-source** strategy (incl. dMMR/MSI-H inference).
- **Custom-LoT rationale** (supplied LoT is IV-only; oral/neoadj/adj must be rebuilt).
- Variable-availability **matrix structure** (off-the-shelf vs curation).
- **Phase-4 gating** logic and open-questions list.

## ❓ Needs Optum / GSK confirmation (gates Phase 4 — see `PLAN.md §6`)

- **Mortality / death source** and OS linkage.
- **Tokenization** vendor & availability (Datavant?).
- **Third-party claims** identity and the **2a/2b / 3a/3b** (broad vs strict) definitions.
- **Production schema/table** names and version.
- **LOINC** availability on the structured Labs side (else use Optum lab name/result + appendix §4.8.3).
- **AJCC edition** handling (not extracted by NLP).
- **Clinical code lists** sign-off (colectomy/debulking CPT, IO & platinum generics, ICD anchors, IO J-codes).

## 🔧 Needs SQL hardening before execution

| # | Item | Status |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | LOINC language made **conditional** (playbook §3/§4, mapping) | ✅ done |
| 2 | **FIGO stage vs FIGO grade** separated (reference, playbook, mapping, Case B) | ✅ done |
| 3 | **Six cohort definitions** implemented via a source-layer switch (`src_diag/src_proc/src_med` + `use_onc`) | ✅ scaffolded (per-cohort config block); fill exact source tables on access |
| 4 | Shared lib **parameterized to ONE tumour** (examples moved to comments; `{{icd_regex}}`/`{{neoplasm_list}}`) | ✅ done |
| 5 | Flags made **cancer-specific**: mets/progression keyed via `neoplasm_histology_key`; **biomarker & lymph-node are stand-alone** → attributed by PTID+date+single-cancer | ✅ done |
| 6 | Case A **stage timing** (pre-surgery, near index dx; restaging ignored; pathological preferred) | ✅ done |
| 7 | Case A metastatic exclusion = **distant only (C78–C79)**, **C77 regional retained** | ✅ done |

## 🔧 Round 2 — second review (correctness bugs found in the round-1 SQL)

A second review caught that round 1 **overclaimed** "execution-ready" while real SQL bugs remained.
Fixed in this round:

| # | Item | Fix |
|---|---|---|
| R1 | **`use_onc` only gated `src_med`** — `onc_*` (histology/stage/biomarker/mets) leaked into cohorts 1-3, overstating EHR/claims-only feasibility | Gated **all** `onc_*` reads by `use_onc`; non-EO cohorts now correctly get empty NLP histology/stage/biomarker |
| R2 | **Six-cohort switch was a fixed block + comments** | Rewrote as a **real per-cohort UNION** of EHR / Optum-claims / 3P-claims sources filtered by `params.cohort` (placeholders to wire to real tables) |
| R3 | **dMMR/MSI-H not single-primary** | Added `flag_multiprimary` + single-primary restriction on the stand-alone biomarker attribution |
| R4 | **Platinum sensitivity used whole-line end & all platinum lines** | Now **2L+ only**, clock from **last platinum administration**, progression = **first ovarian progression after** that date |
| R5 | **Biosimilar collapse ineffective** (joined `vs_drug` before mapping) | Canonicalize via `vs_biosimilar` **first**, then join `vs_drug` on the canonical name (lib + Case B) |
| R6 | **`QUALIFY` + unbounded recursion** assumed Trino-runnable | Replaced `QUALIFY` with portable `row_number`; added a prominent **recursion-depth caveat** (Trino default ~10 truncates) + recommend procedural LoT for production |
| R7 | **Drug `first_action_date` could be an intent date** | Added `first_confirmed_date` (first date at strength ≥3); Case A IO timing now uses it |
| R8 | **`date_from_note='N'` wrongly excluded** (contradicted playbook) | Removed the exclusion — Optum already drops implied/relative dates; 'N'=encounter date is usable |
| R9 | **Priority counts confirmed via `vs_neoplasm` only** | Now uses neoplasm **and** histology terms; tumours lacking EO term mappings show **NULL + note**, not false 0 |
| R10 | **Stale doc wording** (matrix FIGO/C77-79/LOINC; mapping >6 mo) | Synced matrix + mapping to the corrected logic |

**Honest status:** **design-complete; execution SCAFFOLDED.** Beyond the external inputs below, two
**internal** items still require the analyst on data access: wiring the §0 per-cohort source placeholders
to real table names, and validating/porting the SQL dialect (recursion + array funcs).

External gates (`docs/optum-gsk-questions.md`): clinical **sign-off** of DRAFT code lists; Optum/GSK
**confirmations** (schema/table names, mortality source, tokenization, 3P-claims identity, LOINC, a/b
split, LoT thresholds). Then **run** the SQL and populate `docs/feasibility-report-template.md` + the matrix.

## 🔧 Round 3 — third review (remaining leaks & clinical-definition fixes)

| # | Item | Fix |
|---|---|---|
| B1 | **Case B Total OC was EO-gated** → cohorts 1-3 came out empty | `b_total_oc` now built from **`cohort_dx` (ICD)** for all cohorts; NLP histology is a separate `histology_confirmed` flag |
| B2 | **Maintenance counted as 2L+** (PARP/bev after 1L platinum became "line 2") | Added `b_tx_lines` = **non-maintenance** lines; 1L/2L+ and the platinum population now use those; maintenance counted separately |
| B3 | **Priority counts leaked EO** into non-EO cohort runs | `confirmed` CTE now `use_onc`-gated; non-EO cohorts return **NULL + "not assessable"** for histology-confirmed |
| B4 | **dMMR single-primary only on EO arm** | Same `flag_multiprimary` restriction now applied to the **structured-Labs arm** too (non-EO detection limitation documented) |
| B5 | **Enrollment gate only on `src_diag`** | `enrol_gate` now applied to **`src_proc`/`src_lab`/`src_med`** via a shared `src_enrol`; **3b uses third-party enrollment**, 2b uses Optum |
| B6 | **CSV matrix stale** (FIGO/C77-79/LOINC) while MD was fixed | CSV synced |
| B7 | **`histology_qualifier` not enforced** in the EO hub | `cohort_neo_hist` now requires `*_qualifier='actual'` on **both** the neoplasm and histology match arms |
| B8 | **Platinum anchor** could attach to a later rechallenge | Anchored to the platinum line **preceding the 2nd treatment line**; alternative flagged as GSK Q#14 |
| B9 | **N-stage direct cast** could drop `N1a`/`x` | Parse numeric via `regexp_extract(n,'[0-9]')`; `x`→null→nodes fallback |
| B10 | **`action` string case sensitivity** | `src_med.action` lower-cased once at source |

New external questions added (`optum-gsk-questions.md`): 3P enrollment table, `onc_medications` scope for
non-EO cohorts, platinum anchor (#14), Total-OC definition (#15).

## 🔧 Round 4 — fourth review (operational + next-layer correctness)

| # | Item | Fix |
|---|---|---|
| C1 | **No teardown → the six-cohort re-run errors** (every `create table` is plain) | Added `sql/00_reset.sql` (drop-if-exists for all per-run tables); documented the per-cohort loop |
| C2 | **Neoadjuvant/adjuvant mutually exclusive** (one phase per patient) | `b_neoadj_adj` now emits **non-exclusive** flags; a patient with chemo before *and* after surgery counts in both |
| C3 | **Case A stage window excluded the post-op pathological stage** | Widened to **[first_dx−30 d, surgery+90 d]** (peri-operative) to include path stage; window flagged as GSK Q#17 |
| C4 | **"Prefer pathological" claimed but not implemented** | Wording corrected (the flag is *reported*, not used to select) in SQL + Case A spec |
| C5 | **Priority `n_identified` misleading for histology-dependent tumours** | Added a `histology_dependent` column + warning; n_identified is explicitly the broad ICD count |
| C6 | **Priority histology-confirm didn't enforce `histology_qualifier='actual'`** | Now enforced on the histology arm (mirrors `cohort_neo_hist`) |
| C7 | **ICD regex assumed dotted codes** (C57.0/C90.0/C83.3) | Optional dot `\.?` → matches dotted and undotted storage |
| C8 | **Strict 2b/3b enrollment is event-level, not patient-level** | Caveat added at the code site; patient-level continuous-observable window is GSK Q#16 (not silently "fixed") |
| C9 | **Stale "two review rounds" status text** | README/PLAN → "multiple review rounds" |

Not changed (need GSK/Optum decision, flagged not guessed): the strict patient-level eligibility window
(#16), the peri-operative staging bound (#17), the platinum anchor (#14), and `onc_medications` fidelity
for non-EO cohorts (#4b). A duplicate round-2 review was re-submitted in round 4; its items were already
fixed in round 3 (no action).

## 🔧 Round 5 — fifth review (analytic-definition refinements)

| # | Item | Fix |
|---|---|---|
| D1 | **NSCLC/SCLC over-confirmed** (both map to `neoplasm_type='lung'`; neoplasm-OR-histology let lung confirm both) | Priority `confirmed` now **requires an actual histology match** for histology-dependent tumours; neoplasm-only confirm is disallowed there |
| D2 | **Case B `histology_confirmed=0` for non-EO read as "no histology"** | Now **NULL (not assessable)** for non-EO cohorts (matches priority-counts semantics); count emits NULL too |
| D3 | **Full top-to-bottom re-run failed on `vs_*`** (`code_lists` plain create; 00_reset leaves value sets) | `code_lists.sql` now drops `vs_*` first (idempotent) |
| D4 | **FIGO stage had no date bound** (could pick a stale old row) | Bounded to `[first_dx−30 d, first_dx+90 d]` |
| D5 | **Multi-primary over-excluded same-organ sites** (colon/rectum counted as 2) | Reframed: flag a patient only if they have an actual neoplasm **outside** the target tumour's set (avoids the colon/rectum and lung-nsclc/sclc traps) |
| D6 | **Biomarker lab-name match case-sensitive** | `lower(lab_name) like lower(pattern)` |
| D7 | **Adjuvant = any therapy after surgery** (recurrence years later mislabeled) | Bounded to **≤180 d post-surgery** (provisional; GSK Q#18) |
| D8 | **Static placeholder refs / continuation maintenance / status drift** | Documented: lib §0 parse-time note, Case B continuation-maintenance limitation, README `00_reset.sql`, PLAN "executable once wired" wording, review-notes bottom line "Rounds 2–5" |

Not changed (GSK/Optum decisions, flagged): adjuvant window (#18), strict patient-level eligibility (#16),
`onc_medications` non-EO scope (#4b), peri-op staging bound (#17). The round-5 message also re-pasted the
round-3/4 detailed review; the genuinely-new items above were actioned, the rest were already addressed.

## 🔧 Round 6 — sixth review (edge cases & reuse-safety)

| # | Item | Fix |
|---|---|---|
| E1 | **Intent-only drugs contaminated ovarian line summaries** — `b_line_summary` rejoined ALL `b_drug` (incl. strength-2 `prescribed`), polluting regimen/platinum/maintenance | Join now restricted to **`exposure_strength >= 3`** (matches `b_lines`) |
| E2 | **Case B `histology_confirmed` overstated** (true for neoplasm-only matches) | Now requires an **actual ovarian histology** match |
| E3 | **Shared `cohort_neo_hist` over-included histology-dependent tumours** (NSCLC/SCLC `lung`) | Histology-dependent rule now applied **in the shared hub** too (require actual histology) |
| E4 | **HNSCC had no histology term** → "not assessable" despite being a priority tumour | Added `hnscc`/`esophageal`/`cervical` squamous to `vs_histology` (DRAFT) |
| E5 | **Case A took earliest colectomy, not earliest *qualifying* surgery** | STEP 1 keeps all colectomies; STEP 2 selects the earliest surgery with the newly-dx window |
| E6 | **Case B surgery date unbounded vs dx** (old gyn surgery distorted neoadj/adj) | `b_surgery` bounded to `proc_date >= first_dx − 30 d` |
| E7 | **`action` exact-string fragile** | `trim(lower(action))` (+ `trim(lower(source))`) at source |
| E8 | **PLAN parenthetical still said "round 2"** | → "Rounds 2–5" |

Unchanged (known/flagged): `onc_medications` non-EO scope (#4b), recursive-LoT dialect validation,
static-placeholder parse-time requirement, continuation-maintenance. The round-6 message re-paste of the
detailed review was triaged; new items above were actioned, the rest already addressed.

## 🔧 Round 7 — seventh review (precision on histology-dependent tumours)

| # | Item | Fix |
|---|---|---|
| F1 | **Histology-dependent confirm joined by `ptid` only** — squamous histology from a *different* primary could confirm HNSCC/NSCLC | Now also requires an **actual target-SITE neoplasm** for the patient (site-anchored) in both priority counts and `cohort_neo_hist`; added head/neck/ESCC/cervix site terms to `vs_neoplasm` (DRAFT) |
| F2 | **Ovarian "primary" surgery could be a recurrent resection** (58957/58958) | `b_surgery` now excludes `descr like '%recurrent%'` and bounds to `[first_dx−30 d, first_dx+365 d]` (provisional primary window) |
| F3 | **`source` filtered on `lower(source)`, not `trim(lower(source))`** | `WHERE` now uses `trim(lower(source))` (matches the projection) |
| F4 | **Drug names not trimmed before biosimilar/class matching** | `upper(trim(generic_desc))` in lib + Case B |
| F5 | **"Rounds 2–5" stale after round 6** | → "Rounds 2–6" in PLAN + review-notes |

Still open (value-set sign-off / GSK decisions): squamous-histology site specificity & PDAC/glioblastoma
histology terms (DRAFT depth); neoadjuvant lower window & the +365 d primary-surgery bound; recursive-LoT
engine validation; `onc_medications` non-EO scope. These need data/clinical input, not more static review.

---

## 🔁 Round 8 — scope recalibration (worked examples, not a full pipeline)

The GSK ask ("enhanced Oncology **use-cases for training**") wanted **illustrative worked examples** of how
to derive each concept / build each cohort — not a hardened end-to-end pipeline. Over rounds 1–7 the
case-study SQL had drifted into a full production pipeline (driven by the review loop's production-correctness
pushes). Recalibrated:

- The case studies now **lead with worked-example query snippets + feasibility commentary**
  (`docs/case_studies/A-colon-dMMR-MSI.md §1`, `B-ovarian.md §0`).
- The full parameterized pipeline was **moved to `sql/reference_pipeline/`** (optional appendix, with its
  own README), not deleted — available for whoever eventually runs the analysis end-to-end.
- README/PLAN reframed: primary deliverable = best-practice guidance + worked examples; pipeline = appendix.

No SQL logic changed in this round — it was relocated and reframed.

---

## ➕ Round 9 — coverage assessment (the actual "what can we use" ask)

Clarified that the stakeholder primarily wants **how to use the tables + what coverage** (the "Summary of
key data variables" matrix; the HPV-<1% / smoking-~50% findings) — i.e. **runnable profiling code**, not
cohort building. Added:

- `sql/coverage/coverage_assessment.sql` — per-variable, per-source coverage (`count(distinct ptid)`) over
  a chosen denominator → fills the variables matrix.
- `sql/coverage/coverage_curation_and_sources.sql` — off-the-shelf vs **curation lift** (EO ∪ Labs ∪ codes;
  the matrix's two rows) + staging **single-source-of-truth** overlap.
- `docs/coverage-assessment.md` — how to run, how output maps to the matrix bands, validation anchors
  (tumour size ≈6%, HPV <1%, smoking ≈50%).

These are simple/runnable by design (the right tool for coverage); the cohort pipeline stays the optional
appendix.

---

## 🔧 Round 10 — coverage SQL hardening + key anchoring (two review passes)

Two read-only reviews of the round-9 coverage deliverable raised overlapping issues; all addressed:

- **Idempotency** — both coverage scripts now `drop table if exists` (denom/report/lift/staging) so a
  re-run with a changed denominator doesn't error.
- **"Any biomarker" curated overcount** — was `loinc is not null` (every routine lab). Now restricted to a
  biomarker/genomic LOINC value-set + lab-name regex (PD-L1, MSI/MMR, HER2, EGFR, ALK, BRAF, KRAS, BRCA, …).
- **Staging union/label** — added an "ANY staging signal incl. structured dx" union row (the EO-only union
  is kept and relabelled); changed the structured signal from broad `C7%` to secondary-neoplasm `C77–C79`
  (dotted/undotted regex); added a **true overlap view** (Stage∩TNM, Stage-only, TNM-only, triple).
- **Smoking/alcohol** — relabelled the assessment row **NLP-SDS-only**, and added curated
  **SDS ∪ Observations ∪ dx-code** arms in the curation-lift script.
- **HPV dx codes** — dotted/undotted-robust (`^B97\.?7`).
- **Expanded variables** — sex, age, BMI, continuous enrollment, death/mortality, HCRU, comorbidity dx.
- **Honesty** — scripts/doc now say "runnable **once schema-mapped**," with the logical-name and
  **keyed-EO** caveats explicit (route through `neoplasm_histology_key` if EO tables lack `ptid`).
- **Appendix key anchoring** — `lib_cancer_cohort.sql` and `priority_tumour_counts.sql` now require the
  matching histology and the target-site neoplasm on the **same `neoplasm_histology_key`** (was same
  `ptid`), so a multi-primary patient can't borrow histology from a different primary.
- **Sync** — README/PLAN/matrix now point to `sql/coverage/` as the matrix-population mechanism.

---

## 🔧 Round 11 — coverage trust pass + schema grounding from the PDFs

Prompted to re-mine the Optum data-dictionary PDFs (the schema **is** documented there) and a third coverage
review. Both converged:

- **`onc_trim` → `onc_tnm`** — the TNM table name was wrong; manual §4.8.2 worked SQL confirms `onc_tnm`.
  Fixed across all 10 files.
- **Schema grounded in the docs** — default schema placeholders now reference the documented Optum names
  (`oncology_<ver>_prod`, `market_clarity_panther_<ver>_prod`); data-model-reference records which physical
  table names are §4.8.2-**confirmed** vs best-effort.
- **Keyed-EO joins implemented (not just caveated)** — staging/progression concept tables are now reached
  through `onc_neoplasm_histology` on `neoplasm_histology_key` (the §4.8.2 pattern), not by `ptid` directly.
- **Shared denominator** — new `00_denominator.sql` defines `denom_pts`/`denom` once; both coverage scripts
  read it (can't drift to different denominators). Staging overlap is now denominator-restricted too.
- **Biomarker matching tightened** — dropped the broad tokens; word-boundary `\b` patterns (so `\balk\b`≠
  "alkaline", `\bret\b`≠"reticulocyte", `\bmet\b`≠"metabolic"), EGFR via LOINC only (collides with eGFR),
  LOINC value-set authoritative.
- **Diagnosis-code arms filtered** — all `diag` arms now require `diagnosis_status='Diagnosis of'` and
  `diagnosis_cd_type like 'ICD%'` (HPV, smoking/alcohol, C77-C79 staging signal, comorbidity).
- **Comorbidity relabelled** — "confirmed non-cancer dx present (dx-history breadth)", not "Charlson"
  (true Charlson needs the Quan value set).
- **Direct-vs-derived** made explicit — derived endpoints (neoadj/adj, maintenance, platinum sens., PFS,
  OS-as-TTE, concordance) are not coverage rows; they're appendix/Phase-4.
- **PLAN** de-staled ("run once schema-mapped", coverage-first §7).

## 🔧 Round 12 — lymph-node keying fix + Epi count queries

- **Lymph-node keying corrected (the flagged contradiction).** §2.1 says biomarker, lymph node and
  evaluation_system are **stand-alone (no `neoplasm_histology_key`)**. `coverage_assessment.sql` had wrongly
  routed `onc_lymph_node` through the hub; now joined on `ptid`. The §3.4 confirmed-vs-best-effort note (which
  itself had listed lymph_node as keyed) is fixed, so reference, coverage SQL and Case A now all agree.
  `onc_surgical_margin` keying is **not stated in §2.1** — treated as keyed pending confirmation, flagged in
  both the doc and the SQL header.
- **New `sql/coverage/epi_question_counts.sql`** — the presentation view: one labeled count per Epi-needs
  question (identification funnel + per-question coverage + EHR-vs-claims discordance template).
- **Matrix-truth fixes:** BMI coverage row relabelled "structured Obs only" (the EO NLP_Measurement arm is
  noted, not silently credited); race/ethnicity now counts both columns, not just race.
- **Epi briefing softened** — schema certainty now says core §4.8.2 names confirmed, ~9 EO spellings + the
  biomarker LOINC list still to confirm.

## 🔧 Round 13 — Databricks notebooks + target-concept key carry-forward

- **Databricks notebook layer** (`notebooks/`) in the team's house style (`run_sql`+pandas, Spark SQL,
  `MIN_CELL=11`, CSV/`display`): `epi_counts_*` (Epi counts) and `coverage_*` (full matrix), driven by a
  shared `_config_tumours` (tumours = config, not separate notebooks; `histology_dependent` toggle).
- **Target-concept key carry-forward (P1):** keyed-variable coverage (stage/TNM/grade/progression/…) now
  counts only on the **target tumour's** `neoplasm_histology_key` (from each tumour's `neoplasm`/`histology`
  config) — a multi-primary patient can no longer lend keyed values from another cancer.
- **Suppression preserves zeros:** only `1..MIN_CELL-1` is suppressed (`n_display`/`suppressed` cols); true 0
  ("not captured") stays distinct; console prints mask small Ns.
- **Case-insensitive gene regex** (`upper(...) RLIKE`), **`TUMORS`→`TUMOURS`** doc fix, **epi_question_counts.sql**
  cohort-sync guard + honest Q1 histology label, **PLAN/README** updated for the notebook layer.
- **Flagged (not changed):** `sample code.txt` carries real host/warehouse/schema metadata (no token) — left
  to the owner to sanitize (it's already in history).

---

## 🔧 Round 14 — SQL/notebook parity on target-key carry-forward

- **SQL coverage path brought to parity (P1):** `00_denominator.sql` now also builds **`denom_keys
  (ptid, neoplasm_histology_key)`**; `coverage_assessment.sql`, `coverage_curation_and_sources.sql`
  (staging) and `epi_question_counts.sql` count **keyed** variables (stage/TNM/grade/progression/…) on
  `denom_keys` instead of re-joining all of a patient's concepts — so the SQL path no longer lets a
  multi-primary patient lend keyed values from another cancer. For a tumour cohort the analyst sets
  `denom_keys` to the target concept (template in `00_denominator.sql`).
- **Notebooks fail fast (P2):** `epi_counts` / `run_coverage` raise if a tumour config has no
  `neoplasm`/`histology` EO terms (keyed coverage would otherwise silently revert to patient-level).
- **Spark regex escaping (P2):** the biomarker-lab `\b` word boundaries and `\.` in dx patterns are now
  **doubled** (`\\b`, `\\.`) so Spark SQL un-escapes them correctly.
- **Suppression (P3):** the Epi CSV's `denom_n` is masked (`<MIN_CELL`) when the cohort itself is small.
- `sample code.txt` env metadata still flagged (owner's call; already in history).

the branch merging to `main`. Two **new** presentation issues were valid and fixed:

- **Q1 funnel `%` blanked** — for histology-dependent tumours the denominator is the concept-confirmed
  cohort, so the broad-ICD funnel row could read >100%. Q1 rows are now **counts only** (pct blanked).
- **Funnel label** — header reworded "≥2 confirmed ICD dx → **EO target concept / histology-confirmed**"
  (histology-independent tumours confirm by neoplasm site, not necessarily histology).

## 🔧 Round 22 — re-point the notebooks at the real deployed schema (data-location/dict PDFs)

`optum data location.pdf` (Catalog Explorer screenshots of the live catalog), and the entity-relationship PDF.

- **One schema, one cut.** All four notebooks now build table names with
  `T(n)=hive_metastore.clnprw_optum_mc_full_use.t_{n}_2025q4` (was the `SCHEMA_EO`/`SCHEMA_MC` placeholder
  split). The location PDF shows a single schema of **52 tables**, all `t_<name>_2025q4`. **Every** table the
  notebooks reference resolves to a real one.
- **Table-name corrections** from the catalog: progression → `onc_progression` (not `onc_tumor_progression`),
  distant mets → `onc_metastatic_location`, margin → `onc_margin`; `onc_tumor_size` **confirmed** (dropped the
  "may be onc_tumor_other" TODO). Deployed names are the expanded forms (`t_diagnosis`/`t_procedure`/`t_visit`/
  `t_observation`, not `diag`/`proc`/`vis`/`obs`).
- **Demographics + mortality → `patient`** (the dict's demographics table): `birth_yr`, `gender`, `race`,
  `ethnicity`, `deceased_indicator`, `date_of_death` (**`YYYYMM`**). Was wrongly on `clm_member_detail`
  (which is per-eligibility-period). Fixed in coverage + epi_counts.
- **Enrollment columns** in Case A's strict gate → derived-enrollment `eligeff`/`eligend` (DATE), was
  `enrol_start`/`enrol_end`.
- **Finding — single claims layer.** The schema has **no separate third-party-claims asset**, so Case A's
  `3a`/`3b` cohorts reuse the same tables as `2a`/`2b` (documented in the notebook header + data-model ref).
- **Docs synced:** `data-model-reference.md` §2.1 (deployed location + 52-table list + naming-drift), the OS
  source; `notebooks/README.md` (only HOST/HTTP_PATH + code lists left); `methodology-and-derivation.md`
  (sources + confirmation table); top `README.md`.

What's left is now **column-level** (a few `# TODO` predicates) + clinical code-list sign-off, not table names.

## 🔧 Round 23 — deployed-column fixes the schema re-point exposed (external review)

A read-only review against the re-pointed notebooks caught real column-level mismatches now that the tables are
the live ones:

- **[P1] Structured-lab LOINC column.** `coverage_optum_databricks.py` curated-lift used `l.loinc_cd` on
  `t_lab` — but the **structured `lab`** table's column is **`LOINC`** (+ `TEST_NAME`); `LOINC_CD` belongs to
  the *claims* `clm_lab_results` table. Fixed both occurrences to `l.loinc` (would otherwise error on an
  unresolved column).
- **[P1] Case B progression.** Two bugs: the date column is **`note_date`** (not `progression_first_note`,
  which doesn't exist on `t_onc_progression` → would error), and the filter was `progression IS NOT NULL`,
  which counts `no progression`/`stable disease`/`remission` as events and distorts platinum strata. Now
  matches the SQL appendix: `progression IN ('progression','recurrence')` + `progression_temporal_status='current'`.
- **[P2] Case B empty-meds guard.** An empty `groupby().apply()` can drop the `line` column and KeyError on a
  cohort/test slice with no qualifying meds — now adds the column explicitly when empty.
- **[P2] Case B surgery upper bound.** Primary cytoreduction is now bounded BOTH sides — `[dx-30, dx+365]`
  (`SURG_WINDOW`) — so a late recurrence surgery can't become the "primary" date driving neoadj/adjuvant.
- **[P3] Doc staleness.** Top `README`, `coverage-assessment.md`, and the review-notes bottom line updated to
  say table names are resolved and only column-level checks remain (and to credit rounds 22–23).

A second, broader review then drove a **Case A clinical-logic pass + honesty/robustness fixes**:

- **[P1] Case A dMMR/MSI-H = positive disease**, not a bare marker mention: MSI-high OR an MMR row indicating
  loss/deficiency/mutation/positivity, excluding `planned`, dated near/after the colon dx (`onc_biomarker`
  cols `biomarker`/`narrative_result`/`gene_characteristics`/`note_date` confirmed). Was: any MSI/MMR name.
- **[P1] Case A IO = confirmed exposure** only (`action IN administered/filled/started/is on`), not prescribed
  intent or stopped/suspended.
- **[P1] Case A six cohorts** relabelled honestly as a **source-layer template**: this schema has one claims
  layer, so 1/2a/3a return the same pre-EO counts today. The `COH` config now names the Optum-claims tables to
  UNION for 2a/2b (`clm_med_diagnosis`/`clm_med_procedure`/`clm_med_services`/`clm_rx`) and marks 3a/3b
  not-assessable. (Claims unions not wired — they need different predicates and live-data validation.)
- **Case A Step 3 lymph-node fallback** (positive `onc_lymph_node` when TNM N is absent) + **stage timing uses
  the TNM row's own `note_date`** (not the concept-hub date) + **Step 4 also excludes EO `onc_metastatic_location`**
  (actual+current), not just structured C78-79. All columns verified against the data dict.
- **[P1] Stand-alone attribution** (biomarker/lymph/ECOG/response/LoT by `ptid`): Case A now adds date proximity;
  a full **single-primary restriction is still a documented TODO** for the coverage/Epi per-tumour rows.
- **[P1] Coverage is not the 5-layer matrix:** relabelled (notebook table, README, coverage-assessment) to
  "direct-variable coverage (EO + selected structured/claims)"; fixed overstated source labels (`visit`→EHR,
  `diagnosis`→Structured EHR); biomarker rows marked **(tested)/(any record)** = documentation, not result.
- **[P1/robustness] Preflight** added to coverage + Epi: `SHOW COLUMNS` checks every required table/column and
  names any gap **before** the big `UNION ALL`, so one renamed column no longer fails the whole report cryptically.
- **Output governance:** Case A/B console prints now mask small cells (`_msk`/`_m1`), matching the CSV suppression.
- **Sarcoma held out** of the active config (histology-defined, not site/ICD) → 9 active tumours, which also
  reconciles the notebooks/README "nine" list. **HPV anchor** note added: it's an oropharyngeal denominator,
  not broad H&N.
- **Docs reconciled to resolved facts:** OS/mortality source confirmed (`patient.date_of_death` YYYYMM +
  `deceased_indicator`) in `epi-briefing` (🔴→🟡) and `optum-gsk-questions` (schema + death marked RESOLVED, with
  the real open question = OS completeness/fitness + cut cadence); case notebooks called **simplified demo ports**,
  not "full builds," in README + both case-study docs.

**Still deferred (need live data or a bigger build, documented not hidden):** wiring the Optum-claims UNIONs for
2a/2b, a full single-primary attribution for stand-alone EO concepts, the third-party-claims column (no asset),
and a production Spark/window LoT for Case B (pandas driver-side filtering is fine only for a test slice).

## 🔧 Round 24 — post-merge review follow-ups (the four that survived re-fetch + a consistency fix)

The reviewer's long pass was against a stale checkout; after re-fetch they confirmed round 23 is on `main` and

- **[P1] Case A 3a/3b no longer emit numbers.** Flagged `assessable=False`; the output renders **`n/a`** (and the
  log prints "NOT ASSESSABLE") so the third-party-claims columns can't be misread as real six-source attrition.
- **[P2] Preflight now gates execution.** `STRICT_PREFLIGHT=True` (default) makes coverage + Epi **raise** if the
  `SHOW COLUMNS` check finds a missing table/column, instead of printing and ploughing into the big UNION.

  not the full five-layer matrix").

## 🔧 Round 25 — Case A count-logic precision + doc-drift the prior cleanup missed

Three real Case A count-changing fixes:
- **IO qualifying date** is now computed **within** the window (administration after first dx, before distant
  mets) instead of taking the patient's global-min IO date and filtering after - so a pre-dx IO for another
  cancer can no longer disqualify a valid later exposure (was an undercount).
- **dMMR/MSI-H result semantics are assay-specific:** MSI → high/unstable/positive; MMR-IHC genes →
  loss/absent/deficient or a gene mutation - **'positive' no longer counts for MMR** (positive staining =
  retained/proficient). Removes an overcount.
- **Node fallback** now fires **only when TNM N is unparseable** (matches the documented intent; a stated N0
  isn't overridden) and also accepts a positive node **count** (`lymph_numeric > 0`), not just the narrative.

Honesty/robustness:
- Coverage + Epi "Continuous enrollment" row relabelled **"Has enrollment record"** (presence only, not a
  window measure). Preflight error text corrected (the metrics share one UNION, so a missing column fails the
  whole report - "fail in place" was wrong).

Doc drift the round-24 cleanup didn't reach (reviewer's "Documentation alignment"):
- **PLAN.md** refreshed: added the data-location/dict/ERD PDFs, marked schema/mortality/LOINC-column resolved,
  updated rounds (→24) and the appendix-output expectations (attrition/overlap/discordance are a design, not a
  one-button output).
- **question-to-table-mapping.md**: intro says names are resolved to the deployed catalog; `Patient.sex` → `patient.gender`.
- **variable-availability-matrix.md**: sarcoma row marked **not assessable — held out**.
- **epi-briefing.md** lead no longer says attrition/overlap are "built and ready to run".
- **sql/reference_pipeline/README.md** de-contradicted (full design, but NOT runnable as-is); the appendix
  `caseB_ovarian.sql` uses the deployed `onc_progression` name; `code_lists.sql` LOINC comment updated.

Deferred (unchanged, documented): Optum-claims union for Case A 2a/2b, single-primary attribution for
stand-alone concepts, value-level concordance queries, and a Spark LoT for Case B.

---

## Bottom line

The package is a **best-practice guidance + worked-examples + runnable coverage-profiling deliverable**
(Databricks notebooks — coverage, Epi counts, and both case-study builds), with the original SQL kept as an
optional appendix.

- **Primary deliverables (rounds 8–26):** guidance (playbook, mapping, data-model reference), case-study
  **worked examples**, the variable matrix, and the **Databricks `notebooks/`** — the coverage profiling
  (`coverage_optum_databricks.py`) and one count per Epi question (`epi_counts_optum_databricks.py`), driven
  by the shared `_config_tumours`. Hardened across rounds 10–21: target-concept `neoplasm_histology_key`
  carry-forward, biomarker-specific Labs matching, the `onc_trim`→`onc_tnm` and lymph-node-stand-alone
  corrections, small-cell suppression (keeps true zeros), fail-fast on missing EO terms, and Q1 funnel
  counts-only. The redundant `sql/coverage/` SQL was removed in favour of the notebooks. **Rounds 22–26**
  re-wired the notebooks to the deployed schema (`clnprw_optum_mc_full_use`, `2025q4`), fixed the
  deployed-column mismatches (structured-lab `LOINC` not `LOINC_CD`; Case B progression via
  `onc_progression.note_date`), corrected Case A clinical logic (≥2-dx cohort applied from step 1,
  assay-specific dMMR, in-window IO, node fallback only when N unparseable), added the column **preflight**,
  made 3a/3b **not-assessable (`n/a`)**, and reconciled the stakeholder docs to the resolved schema/OS/LOINC facts.
- **Appendix pipeline (`sql/reference_pipeline/`):** rounds 2–7 fixed the internal correctness bugs (EO
  leakage, platinum strata, biosimilar order, exposure dating, maintenance/2L+, six-cohort gating,
  NSCLC/SCLC over-confirmation, multi-primary over-exclusion, surgery bounding); round-10 anchored
  histology+target-site neoplasm on the same `neoplasm_histology_key` (was patient-level).

**Status:** the coverage **notebooks** are **pointed at the deployed schema**
(`hive_metastore.clnprw_optum_mc_full_use`, cut `2025q4`) — all referenced table names resolve (round 22). What
remains before a run: the SQL-warehouse `HOST`/`HTTP_PATH`, a few **column-level** `# TODO` predicates,
`onc_margin` keying, the biomarker LOINC value-set, and clinical code-list sign-off. The appendix pipeline stays
**design-complete but execution-scaffolded**. Don't treat any output as client-facing truth until the notebooks
have been run on a slice and the anchors check out.

# Variable-Availability Matrix

> GSK's requested summary (`optum mc.pdf` p.7): *"a nice summary … one row for the off-the-shelf
> oncology data and another for the curation data."* Here that is captured as two adjacent columns
> — **EO off-the-shelf** vs **EO curation/derived** — alongside the structured EHR and claims layers.
>
> **This is a documentation-derived expectation matrix.** The ratings are the *expected* availability
> from the data dictionary. The **% cells are populated by running the Databricks `notebooks/`** (see
> `docs/coverage-assessment.md`) against live data — that profiling is the mechanism that turns these
> expected ratings into measured coverage. Machine-readable copy: `docs/variable-availability-matrix.csv`.

**Legend:** ✅ captured (high) · 🟡 partial / recall-limited · 🟧 derivable-only (curation/business rule) · ⛔ not in this layer
**Layers:** EHR = structured EHR · EO-OTS = Enriched Oncology off-the-shelf (`onc_*`) · EO-CUR = Enriched Oncology curation/derived · CLM-O = Optum claims · CLM-3P = third-party claims

---

## 1. Matrix

|---|---|:--:|:--:|:--:|:--:|:--:|---|
| | **Identification** | | | | | | |
| 1 | Cancer diagnosis (entry) | ✅ | 🟡 | — | ✅ | ✅ | EHR/claims ICD; ≥2 encounters |
| 2 | Histology confirmation | 🟡 | 🟡 | 🟧 | ⛔ | ⛔ | `onc_neoplasm_histology` + path/Labs |

| 39 | Comorbidities / safety background | ✅ | 🟡 | 🟧 | ✅ | ✅ | `Diagnosis` + SDS + `Problem` |

---

## 2. How to read it (headline takeaways)

- **Staging, FIGO, ECOG, response, progression are NLP-only (EO).** They are essentially absent from
  claims and largely absent from structured EHR — so any study needing them **requires Enriched Oncology**,
  and inherits its recall caveat. This is the clearest "value of enrichment" signal.
- **Treatment & HCRU are strongest in claims** (fills, cross-network, utilization); **oral therapy** is only
  complete once you use the `Oncology_Medications` table (not the supplied IV-only LoT).
- **Biomarkers need two layers** (EO Biomarker + structured Labs; `lab.LOINC` is confirmed — the open item is the biomarker LOINC value-set to match on); HPV is the weakest, derivable-only.
- **A whole column of GSK endpoints lives in EO-CUR** (custom LoT, neoadj/adj, maintenance, platinum status,
  PFS) — i.e., **not off-the-shelf**; they require the business rules in the playbook/case-studies.

---

## 3. Priority-tumour counts — template (populate in Phase 4)

Rows = GSK priority tumours; columns = the 6 case-study cohort definitions. Counts are filled on data access.

| Tumour | ICD anchor (illustrative) | C1 EHR | C2a | C2b | C3a | C3b | C4 MC-Enriched |
|---|---|--:|--:|--:|--:|--:|--:|
| Ovarian | C56.x | | | | | | |
| Endometrial | C54.x | | | | | | |
| Colorectal | C18–C20 | | | | | | |
| Prostate | C61 | | | | | | |
| Multiple myeloma | C90.0 | | | | | | |
| NSCLC | C34.x (+ histology) | | | | | | |
| HNSCC | C00–C14, C32 (+ histology) | | | | | | |
| Sarcoma *(not assessable — held out)* | C40–C41, C49 — **histology-defined, not site/ICD**; held out of the executable config pending a sarcoma histology definition | n/a | n/a | n/a | n/a | n/a | n/a |
| Bladder | C67.x | | | | | | |

> Broader GSK list to extend with: cervical, PDAC, ESCC, gastric, GIST, DLBCL, FL, MCL, MZL, SCLC,
> osteosarcoma, STS, unmethylated glioblastoma.
>>>>> OPTUM-MC FILE: notebooks/README.md
# Databricks notebooks (Optum MC + Enriched Oncology)

The runnable code in the house Databricks style (`run_sql` + pandas, Spark SQL, `MIN_CELL=11` small-cell
suppression, CSV export + `display`): the coverage profiling plus the two case-study cohort builds.

| Notebook | Answers | Output |
|---|---|---|
| `_config_tumours.py` | shared tumour config (`%run` from both coverage notebooks): the per-cancer code lists + `TUMOURS` dict | n/a |
| `epi_counts_optum_databricks.py` | one count per Epi question, per cancer | ID funnel + per-question coverage (`n_patients`, `% of cohort`); a CSV per tumour |
| `coverage_optum_databricks.py` | direct-variable coverage (EO + selected structured/claims inputs) — **not** the full 5-layer matrix (no third-party column, no EHR-vs-claims discordance) | (1) coverage by variable x source, (2) off-shelf vs curated lift, (3) staging source coverage + overlap; CSVs per tumour, plus an `all_eo` overview |
| `caseA_colon_attrition_databricks.py` | Case A: dMMR/MSI-H colon, 6-step attrition x 6 cohorts | the attrition table (patients left per step, per cohort) -> CSV |
| `caseB_ovarian_lot_databricks.py` | Case B: ovarian total / LoT / platinum sensitivity | strata counts (total, neoadj/adjuvant, 1L, 2L+, platinum-sens/resistant) -> CSV |

The case-study notebooks are Python ports of `../sql/reference_pipeline/`; they're scaffolded (wire the
per-cohort source tables) and the LoT/platinum thresholds need sign-off.

## Safeguards in the outputs
- **Small-cell suppression** (`MIN_CELL=11`): counts `1..10` are blanked and shown as `<11` (`n_display`,
  `suppressed` columns); true **0** is kept so "not captured" stays distinct from "small positive suppressed".
  Console prints also mask small Ns.
- **Target-concept keyed coverage**: keyed variables (stage/TNM/grade/progression) are counted only on the
  target tumour's `neoplasm_histology_key` (from each tumour's `neoplasm`/`histology` config), so a
  multi-primary patient can't lend stage from another cancer. Per-patient tables (biomarker, lymph node,
  ECOG, meds) join on `ptid`. A tumour added without `neoplasm`/`histology` terms fails fast rather than
  quietly reverting to patient-level counts.

## Adding / changing a tumour
The GSK priority list is active in `_config_tumours.py` (ovarian, endometrial, colorectal, prostate, bladder,
myeloma, NSCLC, SCLC, HNSCC) - code lists are illustrative and need sign-off. Tumours are config, not
separate notebooks: edit `_config_tumours.py` (one `TUMOURS` entry per cancer) and both coverage notebooks
`%run` it. Set `histology_dependent=True` where ICD can't define the cohort (NSCLC/SCLC/HNSCC) and add a
`histology` list (and ideally `neoplasm` site terms); identification then uses the histology+site anchoring
on the same `neoplasm_histology_key`, and the confirmed set becomes the denominator. Site-defined cancers
(colon/ovarian) just use the ICD cohort.

## Schema (already set) and what's left to fill in

The notebooks are pointed at the **deployed** location (from `Optum Data Dict/optum data location.pdf`):

```python
CATALOG = "hive_metastore"
SCHEMA  = "clnprw_optum_mc_full_use"   # ONE schema: claims/EHR + Enriched Oncology
CUT     = "2025q4"                     # data cut; tables are t_<name>_2025q4
def T(n): return f"{CATALOG}.{SCHEMA}.t_{n}_{CUT}"
```

There is one schema (not separate EO/MC schemas) and 52 tables, all named `t_<name>_2025q4`. Every table the
notebooks reference resolves to a real table in that catalog. Update `SCHEMA`/`CUT` here when a newer cut is
licensed (e.g. `2026q1`) — nothing else changes.

**Still to fill in before running (tagged `# TODO`):**

1. **Connection:** `HOST`, `HTTP_PATH` (your SQL warehouse — host is `adb-2026261073024158.18.azuredatabricks.net`
   for this workspace; set the warehouse `HTTP_PATH`).
2. **Per-study code lists:** the ICD lists per cancer in `_config_tumours.py` (+ optional HCPCS/NDC regimen codes).
3. **Biomarker LOINC list:** replace the `'--...LOINC...--'` placeholders from the Optum LOINC dictionary.
4. **A few column-level predicates** still want a column-name check on the real tables (table names are settled):
   `onc_grade.grade`, the `onc_tumor_size` size column, `onc_evaluation_system.system_name`, `nlp_sds.sds_term`,
   and `onc_treatment_response.response`. (The structured `lab` columns are confirmed: **`LOINC`** + `TEST_NAME` —
   the *claims* `clm_lab_results` table is the one that uses `LOINC_CD`.)

**Confirmed from the data dictionary (no action):** demographics + mortality come from `patient`
(`birth_yr`, `gender`, `race`, `ethnicity`, `deceased_indicator`, `date_of_death` as `YYYYMM`); enrollment from
`clm_derived_enrollment` (`eligeff`/`eligend` dates; `clm_co_enrollment` is the month-grained alternative);
the Enriched Oncology keyed/stand-alone split (via `neoplasm_histology_key`) is enforced in the queries.

> **Source layers:** this schema has a single claims layer (`clm_med_diag`/`proc`/`serv`, `clm_inp_conf`,
> `clm_lab`, `clm_rx`, `clm_der_enrl`, `clm_co_enrl`, `clm_mbr_dtl`) — there is no separate third-party-claims
> asset here, so Case A's `3a`/`3b` cohorts are flagged **not-assessable and render `n/a`** (not duplicate
> counts); they'd be wired only if a distinct third-party dataset is licensed. Cohorts `2a`/`2b` still read the
> same structured tables as cohort 1 until the Optum-claims UNION is wired (a TODO).

**Validation anchors** once they run: HPV/p16 `<1%`, smoking `~50%`, tumour size `~6%` (in the right denominator).
