# Databricks notebook source
# MAGIC %md
# MAGIC # Case A - dMMR/MSI-H locally advanced colon cancer (attrition)
# MAGIC **Simplified demo port** of `sql/reference_pipeline/caseA_colon.sql` - use the SQL appendix for trusted
# MAGIC numbers; this notebook is for showing the shape of the attrition across the 6 source definitions.
# MAGIC Spec: `docs/case_studies/A-colon-dMMR-MSI.md`.
# MAGIC
# MAGIC Tables point at the deployed schema `hive_metastore.clnprw_optum_mc_full_use` (cut `2025q4`). What is
# MAGIC still scaffolded is the **per-cohort source differentiation**: the Optum MC schema has one claims layer,
# MAGIC not separate EHR-only / Optum-claims / third-party-claims assets. Cohorts **3a/3b are not-assessable here
# MAGIC and render `n/a`** (no third-party asset); cohorts 1/2a (and 2b) currently read the same structured tables
# MAGIC (the Optum-claims UNION for 2a/2b is a TODO), so they coincide until that's wired. Treat the columns as the
# MAGIC *shape* of the source-layer split (see Notes). Steps 3 (NLP stage) and 5
# MAGIC (dMMR/MSI-H) only exist in the Enriched-Oncology cohort, so claims-only cohorts attrit to zero there - that
# MAGIC drop is the feasibility finding. Code lists are illustrative and need clinical sign-off.

# COMMAND ----------
import os, posixpath, pandas as pd
from databricks import sql

HOST      = "adb-XXXXXXXXXXXX.azuredatabricks.net"      # TODO
HTTP_PATH = "/sql/1.0/warehouses/XXXXXXXXXXXX"          # TODO
TOKEN     = dbutils.notebook.entry_point.getDbutils().notebook().getContext().apiToken().get()  # noqa: F821
CATALOG   = "hive_metastore"
SCHEMA    = "clnprw_optum_mc_full_use"   # one schema: claims/EHR + Enriched Oncology (optum data location.pdf)
CUT       = "2025q4"                      # data cut; tables are t_<name>_<CUT>
def T(n): return f"{CATALOG}.{SCHEMA}.t_{n}_{CUT}"
MIN_CELL = 11

def run_sql(q):
    with sql.connect(server_hostname=HOST, http_path=HTTP_PATH, access_token=TOKEN) as c, c.cursor() as cur:
        cur.execute(q); return pd.DataFrame(cur.fetchall(), columns=[d[0] for d in cur.description])
def _n(q): return int(run_sql(q).iloc[0, 0])      # scalar count
def _msk(v): return f"<{MIN_CELL}" if 0 < int(v) < MIN_CELL else int(v)   # mask small cells in console logs too
def _in(xs): return ", ".join("'" + str(x).replace("'", "''") + "'" for x in xs)
def _uin(xs): return _in([str(x).upper().strip() for x in xs])
def _code(xs): return ["".join(ch for ch in str(x).upper() if ch.isalnum()) for x in xs]
def _outdir():
    try:
        nb = dbutils.notebook.entry_point.getDbutils().notebook().getContext().notebookPath().get()  # noqa: F821
        d = posixpath.dirname(nb); return (d if d.startswith("/Workspace") else "/Workspace"+d) + "/casea_outputs"
    except Exception:
        return os.path.join(os.getcwd(), "casea_outputs")

# COMMAND ----------
# codes / EO terms (illustrative - sign-off needed)
COLON_ICD  = ["C18.0","C18.2","C18.3","C18.4","C18.5","C18.6","C18.7","C18.9","C19","C20"]
COLON_NEO  = ["COLON","RECTUM","RECTOSIGMOID","COLORECTAL"]          # EO neoplasm_type for the colon concept
COLECTOMY  = ["44140","44145","44150","44160","44204","44205","44206","44207","44210"]   # CPT
IO_DRUGS   = ["PEMBROLIZUMAB","NIVOLUMAB","DOSTARLIMAB","IPILIMUMAB"]
NEWDX_DAYS, PREOP_DAYS, POSTOP_DAYS = 183, 30, 90

# 6 data-source definitions. dx/proc/med point at the layer the cohort sees; use_onc = Enriched Oncology
# available; strict = continuous-enrollment variant (2b/3b).
#
# ⚠️ SOURCE-LAYER TEMPLATE (read before trusting the output). 3a/3b are not-assessable here and render `n/a`
# (no third-party asset). The remaining cohorts still read the SAME structured EHR tables (diagnosis/procedure) +
# onc_medications, so cohorts 1 and 2a return identical pre-EO counts and 2b differs only by the enrollment gate.
# To make them genuinely different:
#   - cohort 1 (EHR-only): keep structured `diagnosis`/`procedure` (these ARE the EHR layer);
#   - cohorts 2a/2b (EHR + Optum claims): UNION the Optum claims tables - `clm_med_diagnosis` (dx),
#     `clm_med_procedure`/`clm_med_services` (proc), `clm_rx` (meds) - which have their OWN columns/predicates
#     (not diagnosis_status='Diagnosis of'); wire + validate those before use;
#   - cohorts 3a/3b (third-party claims): NOT in this schema (no third-party asset) - leave as not-assessable
#     unless a separate dataset is licensed. 3b's enrollment is the Optum derived-enrollment stand-in.
# Until the claims unions are wired, treat the columns as an OUTPUT TEMPLATE: cohort 4 (enriched) and cohort 1
# (EHR) are the two that are actually distinct today. 3a/3b are flagged assessable=False and render as **n/a**
# (not a number) so they can't be mistaken for real third-party-claims attrition.
COH = {
  "1_ehr":      dict(dx=f"{T('diagnosis')}", proc=f"{T('procedure')}", med=f"{T('onc_medications')}", enrol=None,             use_onc=False, strict=False),  # EHR-only = structured diagnosis/procedure
  "2a_optum":   dict(dx=f"{T('diagnosis')}", proc=f"{T('procedure')}", med=f"{T('onc_medications')}", enrol=f"{T('clm_derived_enrollment')}", use_onc=False, strict=False),  # TODO: UNION clm_med_diagnosis/clm_med_procedure/clm_med_services/clm_rx
  "2b_optum":   dict(dx=f"{T('diagnosis')}", proc=f"{T('procedure')}", med=f"{T('onc_medications')}", enrol=f"{T('clm_derived_enrollment')}", use_onc=False, strict=True),   # TODO: same union + strict enrollment
  "3a_tpc":     dict(dx=f"{T('diagnosis')}", proc=f"{T('procedure')}", med=f"{T('onc_medications')}", enrol=None,             use_onc=False, strict=False, assessable=False),  # NOT-ASSESSABLE here (no third-party-claims asset)
  "3b_tpc":     dict(dx=f"{T('diagnosis')}", proc=f"{T('procedure')}", med=f"{T('onc_medications')}", enrol=f"{T('clm_derived_enrollment')}", use_onc=False, strict=True, assessable=False),   # NOT-ASSESSABLE here; enrollment is the Optum stand-in
  "4_enriched": dict(dx=f"{T('diagnosis')}", proc=f"{T('procedure')}", med=f"{T('onc_medications')}", enrol=f"{T('clm_derived_enrollment')}", use_onc=True,  strict=False),
}
STEPS = ["1 colon surgery", "2 newly dx <=183d", "3 stage T1-4N+/T4N0", "4 no distant mets", "5 dMMR/MSI-H", "6 IO use"]

def attrition(name, c):
    dx, proc, med = c["dx"], c["proc"], c["med"]
    ndx = "regexp_replace(upper(trim(diagnosis_cd)),'[^0-9A-Z]','')"
    npx = "regexp_replace(upper(trim(proc_cd)),'[^0-9A-Z]','')"
    colon = f"({ndx} IN ({_in(_code(COLON_ICD))}))"
    # cohort entry = the standard >=2-confirmed-dx-dates rule (matches Case B + the SQL appendix lib_cancer_cohort).
    # Defined first and applied from STEP 1 onward, so the surgery denominator uses the same cohort (not any single dx).
    fdx = f"SELECT ptid, min(to_date(diag_date)) first_dx FROM {dx} WHERE diagnosis_status='Diagnosis of' AND {colon} GROUP BY ptid HAVING count(DISTINCT diag_date) >= 2"
    # step 1: every colectomy event for a >=2-confirmed-dx colon-cancer patient (don't collapse to a global min yet)
    surg = (f"SELECT DISTINCT p.ptid, to_date(p.proc_date) sdt FROM {proc} p "
            f"WHERE {npx} IN ({_in(_code(COLECTOMY))}) AND p.ptid IN (SELECT ptid FROM ({fdx}) f)")
    # step 2: pick the earliest colectomy whose [-183d, 0] window contains the first colon dx (the QUALIFYING
    # cancer surgery - so an old non-qualifying colectomy can't wrongly exclude the patient)
    base = (f"SELECT e.ptid, min(e.sdt) surgery_date, d.first_dx FROM ({surg}) e JOIN ({fdx}) d ON d.ptid=e.ptid "
            f"WHERE d.first_dx BETWEEN date_sub(e.sdt,{NEWDX_DAYS}) AND e.sdt GROUP BY e.ptid, d.first_dx")
    if c["strict"] and c["enrol"]:   # 2b/3b: require continuous enrollment covering [first_dx, surgery]
        base = (f"SELECT b.* FROM ({base}) b WHERE EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM {c['enrol']} e WHERE e.ptid=b.ptid "
                f"AND e.eligeff <= b.first_dx AND e.eligend >= b.surgery_date)")  # derived-enrollment period: eligeff/eligend (DATE) per data dict
    n = {"1 colon surgery": _n(f"SELECT count(DISTINCT ptid) FROM ({surg}) t"),
         "2 newly dx <=183d": _n(f"SELECT count(*) FROM ({base}) t")}
    if c["use_onc"]:
        # colon target concept keys (by SITE - histology alone, e.g. 'adenocarcinoma', isn't colon-specific
        # and could match another primary). Carry the note date + surgery/first-dx for the peri-op window.
        ckeys = (f"SELECT h.ptid, h.neoplasm_histology_key, to_date(h.note_date) note_date, b.surgery_date, b.first_dx "
                 f"FROM {T('onc_neoplasm_histology')} h JOIN ({base}) b ON b.ptid=h.ptid "
                 f"WHERE h.neoplasm_qualifier='actual' AND upper(trim(h.neoplasm_type)) IN ({_uin(COLON_NEO)})")
        # N+ fallback: positive lymph nodes (stand-alone, ptid-level) in the peri-op window. Positive = a
        # 'positive' narrative OR a positive node count (lymph_numeric > 0). Used ONLY when TNM N is
        # missing/unparseable (an explicit N0 is left to the T4N0 branch - we don't override a stated N0).
        posnodes = (f"SELECT DISTINCT b.ptid FROM ({base}) b JOIN {T('onc_lymph_node')} ln ON ln.ptid=b.ptid "
                    f"AND (lower(coalesce(ln.lymph_narrative,'')) LIKE '%positive%' OR ln.lymph_numeric > 0) "
                    f"AND to_date(ln.note_date) BETWEEN date_sub(b.first_dx,{PREOP_DAYS}) AND date_add(b.surgery_date,{POSTOP_DAYS})")
        # step 3: T1-4 with N+ (TNM N>0) OR T4 N0, OR - only when TNM N is unparseable - the positive-node
        # fallback; on the colon concept, timed by the TNM row's own note_date in the peri-op window [dx-30, surgery+90]
        tnm = (f"SELECT DISTINCT k.ptid FROM ({ckeys}) k JOIN {T('onc_tnm')} t ON t.neoplasm_histology_key=k.neoplasm_histology_key "
               f"WHERE try_cast(regexp_extract(t.t,'[1-4]',0) AS int) BETWEEN 1 AND 4 "
               f"AND ( try_cast(regexp_extract(t.n,'[0-9]',0) AS int) > 0 "
               f"      OR (try_cast(regexp_extract(t.t,'[1-4]',0) AS int)=4 AND try_cast(regexp_extract(t.n,'[0-9]',0) AS int)=0) "
               f"      OR (regexp_extract(t.n,'[0-9]',0)='' AND k.ptid IN ({posnodes})) ) "
               f"AND to_date(t.note_date) BETWEEN date_sub(k.first_dx,{PREOP_DAYS}) AND date_add(k.surgery_date,{POSTOP_DAYS})")
        step3 = f"SELECT b.ptid, b.surgery_date, b.first_dx FROM ({base}) b WHERE b.ptid IN ({tnm})"
        # step 4: no DISTANT metastasis before surgery - structured C78-79 dx OR an actual+current EO
        # metastatic_location (keyed to the colon concept; EO mets = distant sites only). C77 regional kept in.
        mets_struct = f"SELECT ptid, to_date(diag_date) mdt FROM {dx} WHERE diagnosis_status='Diagnosis of' AND {ndx} RLIKE '^C7[89]'"
        mets_eo = (f"SELECT k.ptid, to_date(m.note_date) mdt FROM ({ckeys}) k JOIN {T('onc_metastatic_location')} m "
                   f"ON m.neoplasm_histology_key=k.neoplasm_histology_key "
                   f"AND lower(trim(m.mets_qualifier))='actual' AND lower(trim(m.mets_temporal_status))='current'")
        mets = f"SELECT ptid, min(mdt) mdt FROM (({mets_struct}) UNION ALL ({mets_eo})) z WHERE mdt IS NOT NULL GROUP BY ptid"
        step4 = f"SELECT s.ptid, s.surgery_date, s.first_dx FROM ({step3}) s LEFT JOIN ({mets}) m ON m.ptid=s.ptid WHERE m.mdt IS NULL OR m.mdt >= s.surgery_date"
        # step 5: dMMR / MSI-H POSITIVE disease - assay-specific result semantics (NOT a bare marker mention):
        #   * MSI testing: 'high'/'unstable'/'positive'/'MSI-H' = MSI-high.
        #   * MMR-IHC genes (MLH1/MSH2/MSH6/PMS2/MMR): 'loss'/'lost'/'absent'/'deficien' = dMMR. We deliberately
        #     do NOT count 'positive' here - positive IHC staining means RETAINED protein (proficient), not deficient.
        #   * a pathogenic-ish mutation in gene_characteristics also counts.
        # exclude 'planned'; dated near/after the colon dx. Biomarker has no concept key, so multi-primary
        # attribution is still ptid+date only (single-primary restriction is a TODO; the structured-Lab dMMR arm,
        # which needs the biomarker LOINC value-set, is also not unioned in yet - see notes).
        dmmr = (f"SELECT b.ptid, to_date(b.note_date) bio_dt FROM {T('onc_biomarker')} b "
                f"WHERE lower(coalesce(b.biomarker_temporal_status,'')) <> 'planned' AND ( "
                f"  ( upper(b.biomarker) LIKE '%MSI%' AND (lower(coalesce(b.narrative_result,'')) RLIKE 'high|unstable|positiv' "
                f"                                          OR upper(b.biomarker) LIKE '%MSI-H%' OR upper(b.biomarker) LIKE '%MSI-HIGH%') ) "
                f"  OR ( (upper(b.biomarker) LIKE '%MMR%' OR upper(b.biomarker) RLIKE 'MLH1|MSH2|MSH6|PMS2') "
                f"       AND ( lower(coalesce(b.narrative_result,'')) RLIKE 'loss|lost|absent|deficien' "
                f"             OR lower(coalesce(b.gene_characteristics,'')) RLIKE 'mutat|loss|deletion' ) ) "
                f") ")
        step5 = (f"SELECT DISTINCT s.ptid, s.surgery_date, s.first_dx FROM ({step4}) s JOIN ({dmmr}) d ON d.ptid=s.ptid "
                 f"AND d.bio_dt >= date_sub(s.first_dx,{PREOP_DAYS})")
        # step 6: IO EXPOSURE (confirmed action only: administered/filled/started/is on - not prescribed intent or
        # stopped/suspended). The qualifying date is the earliest administration WITHIN the window (after first dx,
        # before any distant mets), computed conditionally - so an earlier IO for another cancer before the colon dx
        # can't keep the global min and wrongly disqualify a valid later exposure.
        step6 = (f"SELECT a.ptid FROM {med} a JOIN ({step5}) s ON s.ptid=a.ptid LEFT JOIN ({mets}) m ON m.ptid=a.ptid "
                 f"WHERE upper(a.generic_desc) IN ({_in(IO_DRUGS)}) "
                 f"AND lower(trim(a.action)) IN ('administered','filled','started','is on') "
                 f"AND to_date(a.action_date) > s.first_dx AND (m.mdt IS NULL OR to_date(a.action_date) < m.mdt) "
                 f"GROUP BY a.ptid")
        n["3 stage T1-4N+/T4N0"] = _n(f"SELECT count(*) FROM ({step3}) t")
        n["4 no distant mets"]   = _n(f"SELECT count(*) FROM ({step4}) t")
        n["5 dMMR/MSI-H"]        = _n(f"SELECT count(*) FROM ({step5}) t")
        n["6 IO use"]            = _n(f"SELECT count(DISTINCT ptid) FROM ({step6}) t")
    else:
        # claims-only cohorts have no NLP stage or biomarker, so they can't pass step 3 onward
        for s in STEPS[2:]: n[s] = 0
    return n

# COMMAND ----------
rows = []
for name, c in COH.items():
    if not c.get("assessable", True):   # 3a/3b: no third-party-claims asset here - emit n/a, not a misleading count
        print(f"{name}: NOT ASSESSABLE (no third-party-claims asset in this schema)")
        for s in STEPS: rows.append((name, s, None))
        continue
    n = attrition(name, c)
    print(f"{name}: " + " | ".join(f"{s.split()[0]}={_msk(n[s])}" for s in STEPS))   # masked log
    for s in STEPS: rows.append((name, s, n[s]))
att = pd.DataFrame(rows, columns=["cohort","step","n_patients"])
# display: n/a for not-assessable cohorts, <MIN_CELL for small positive cells, true 0 kept
def _disp(v):
    if v is None or (isinstance(v, float) and pd.isna(v)): return "n/a"
    v = int(v); return f"<{MIN_CELL}" if 0 < v < MIN_CELL else str(v)
att["n_display"] = att["n_patients"].map(_disp)
table = att.pivot(index="step", columns="cohort", values="n_display").reindex(STEPS)
out = _outdir(); os.makedirs(out, exist_ok=True)
table.to_csv(f"{out}/caseA_attrition.csv"); print(f"wrote {out}/caseA_attrition.csv")
display(table)   # noqa: F821

# COMMAND ----------
# MAGIC %md
# MAGIC ### Notes
# MAGIC Implemented vs the spec: stage (TNM) is restricted to the colon concept and timed by the **TNM row's own
# MAGIC note_date** in the peri-op window; **N+ falls back to positive lymph nodes (narrative or `lymph_numeric>0`)
# MAGIC ONLY when TNM N is unparseable** (a stated N0 isn't overridden); step 4 excludes distant mets from
# MAGIC **structured C78-79 OR the EO metastatic_location** table; step 5 uses **assay-specific dMMR/MSI-H result
# MAGIC logic** (MSI: high/unstable/positive; MMR-IHC: loss/absent/deficient or a gene mutation - "positive" does
# MAGIC NOT count for MMR, since positive staining = retained/proficient), dated near/after dx; step 6 takes the
# MAGIC **earliest confirmed IO exposure WITHIN the window** (after dx, before distant mets) so a pre-dx IO for
# MAGIC another cancer can't disqualify a valid later one; 2b/3b apply a continuous-enrollment gate.
# MAGIC Still simplified: dMMR/biomarker and lymph nodes are stand-alone (no concept key) so for a multi-primary
# MAGIC patient they're attributed by `ptid` + date proximity only - a **single-primary restriction is still a
# MAGIC TODO**; the **structured-Lab dMMR arm** (needs the biomarker LOINC value-set) isn't unioned in yet; MMR-IHC
# MAGIC result wording still needs lab sign-off; the run doesn't yet persist cohort-tagged PTID sets, so the
# MAGIC cross-cohort overlap (Venn) is a downstream step. For trusted numbers use the SQL appendix.
# MAGIC - Source layers: the data dictionary (`optum data location.pdf`) shows ONE claims layer in this schema
# MAGIC   (clm_med_diag/proc/serv, clm_inp_conf, clm_lab, clm_rx, clm_der_enrl, clm_co_enrl, clm_mbr_dtl) - there is
# MAGIC   no separate third-party-claims asset here, so 3a/3b are flagged `assessable=False` and render **n/a**
# MAGIC   (they do NOT run). Wire them to a real third-party-claims dataset, and flip the flag, only if one is licensed.
# MAGIC - Confirmed against the data dict: `procedure`, `onc_medications.generic_desc`, and derived enrollment
# MAGIC   `eligeff`/`eligend` (DATE). Remaining sign-off is on the code lists, not the table/column names.
>>>>> OPTUM-MC FILE: notebooks/caseB_ovarian_lot_databricks.py
# Databricks notebook source
# MAGIC %md
# MAGIC # Case B - ovarian cancer: line of therapy & platinum sensitivity
# MAGIC **Simplified demo port** of `sql/reference_pipeline/caseB_ovarian.sql` - use the SQL appendix for trusted
# MAGIC numbers. Total OC, treatment phase (neoadjuvant/adjuvant), 1L / 2L+, platinum-sensitive vs -resistant.
# MAGIC Spec: `docs/case_studies/B-ovarian.md`. Line of therapy is sessionised in pandas, which fits the
# MAGIC platform style.
# MAGIC
# MAGIC Simplified vs the spec: LoT is a **gap-based** split only (not the regimen-change / 30-90-day rules);
# MAGIC maintenance isn't separated; this runs the **enriched cohort only** (the other 5 source cohorts follow
# MAGIC Case A's source-swap), and the patient-characteristics table isn't produced here. The thresholds and
# MAGIC code lists are illustrative and need sign-off.

# COMMAND ----------
import os, posixpath, pandas as pd
from databricks import sql

HOST      = "adb-XXXXXXXXXXXX.azuredatabricks.net"      # TODO
HTTP_PATH = "/sql/1.0/warehouses/XXXXXXXXXXXX"          # TODO
TOKEN     = dbutils.notebook.entry_point.getDbutils().notebook().getContext().apiToken().get()  # noqa: F821
CATALOG   = "hive_metastore"
SCHEMA    = "clnprw_optum_mc_full_use"   # one schema: claims/EHR + Enriched Oncology (optum data location.pdf)
CUT       = "2025q4"                      # data cut; tables are t_<name>_<CUT>
def T(n): return f"{CATALOG}.{SCHEMA}.t_{n}_{CUT}"
MIN_CELL = 11
LINE_GAP, PLATINUM_FREE, ADJ_WINDOW = 120, 183, 180     # days: new-line gap, platinum-sensitive cutoff, adjuvant window
SURG_WINDOW = 365                                       # days post-dx: primary cytoreduction is near diagnosis (a later one is likely recurrence)

def run_sql(q):
    with sql.connect(server_hostname=HOST, http_path=HTTP_PATH, access_token=TOKEN) as c, c.cursor() as cur:
        cur.execute(q); return pd.DataFrame(cur.fetchall(), columns=[d[0] for d in cur.description])
def _in(xs): return ", ".join("'" + str(x).replace("'", "''") + "'" for x in xs)
def _uin(xs): return _in([str(x).upper().strip() for x in xs])
def _code(xs): return ["".join(ch for ch in str(x).upper() if ch.isalnum()) for x in xs]
def _outdir():
    try:
        nb = dbutils.notebook.entry_point.getDbutils().notebook().getContext().notebookPath().get()  # noqa: F821
        d = posixpath.dirname(nb); return (d if d.startswith("/Workspace") else "/Workspace"+d) + "/caseb_outputs"
    except Exception:
        return os.path.join(os.getcwd(), "caseb_outputs")
def _mask(d):   # small-cell suppression on a {label: count} dict
    return {k: (f"<{MIN_CELL}" if 0 < v < MIN_CELL else int(v)) for k, v in d.items()}
def _m1(v):     # scalar small-cell mask, so console logs don't leak small cells either
    return f"<{MIN_CELL}" if 0 < int(v) < MIN_CELL else int(v)

# COMMAND ----------
# codes (illustrative - sign-off needed)
OVARIAN_ICD = ["C56.1","C56.2","C56.3","C56.9","C57.00","C57.01","C57.02","C48.1","C48.2","C48.8"]
OVARIAN_NEO = ["OVARY","FALLOPIAN TUBE","FALLOPIAN","PERITONEUM","PERITONEAL"]    # EO neoplasm_type for the ovarian concept
PLATINUM    = ["CARBOPLATIN","CISPLATIN","OXALIPLATIN"]
REGIMEN     = PLATINUM + ["PACLITAXEL","DOCETAXEL","GEMCITABINE","DOXORUBICIN","LIPOSOMAL DOXORUBICIN",
                          "TOPOTECAN","BEVACIZUMAB","OLAPARIB","NIRAPARIB","RUCAPARIB"]
SURGERY_CPT = ["58950","58951","58952","58953","58954","58956","58200","58210"]   # PRIMARY cytoreduction (58957/58958 are recurrent - excluded)
ndx = "regexp_replace(upper(trim(diagnosis_cd)),'[^0-9A-Z]','')"

# total OC = >=2 confirmed ovarian dx dates; carry the first ovarian dx to bound treatment/surgery
oc = (f"SELECT ptid, min(to_date(diag_date)) first_dx FROM {T('diagnosis')} WHERE diagnosis_status='Diagnosis of' "
      f"AND diagnosis_cd_type LIKE 'ICD%' AND {ndx} IN ({_in(_code(OVARIAN_ICD))}) "
      f"GROUP BY ptid HAVING count(DISTINCT diag_date) >= 2")
n_total = int(run_sql(f"SELECT count(*) FROM ({oc}) t").iloc[0, 0])
print("total ovarian:", _m1(n_total))

# COMMAND ----------
# antineoplastic administrations ON/AFTER the ovarian diagnosis (so a prior cancer's therapy isn't counted
# as ovarian 1L/2L/platinum). oral + IV, via Oncology Medications.
meds = run_sql(
    f"SELECT mm.ptid, to_date(mm.action_date) dt, upper(mm.generic_desc) drug "
    f"FROM {T('onc_medications')} mm JOIN ({oc}) o ON o.ptid=mm.ptid "
    f"WHERE upper(mm.generic_desc) IN ({_in(REGIMEN)}) AND lower(trim(mm.action)) IN ('administered','filled','started','is on') "
    f"AND to_date(mm.action_date) >= o.first_dx")
meds["dt"] = pd.to_datetime(meds["dt"])
meds = meds.dropna(subset=["dt"]).sort_values(["ptid", "dt"])

# line of therapy: a new line starts after a gap > LINE_GAP days (simplified; maintenance not separated)
def assign_lines(g):
    line, last, out = 1, None, []
    for dt in g["dt"]:
        if last is not None and (dt - last).days > LINE_GAP:
            line += 1
        out.append(line); last = dt
    g = g.copy(); g["line"] = out; return g
# guard the empty case (no qualifying meds in a cohort / test slice): an empty groupby.apply can drop the
# 'line' column and KeyError downstream, so add it explicitly.
if meds.empty:
    meds = meds.assign(line=pd.Series(dtype="int64"))
else:
    meds = meds.groupby("ptid", group_keys=False).apply(assign_lines)

lot = meds.groupby("ptid")["line"].max().rename("n_lines").reset_index()    # lines per patient
n_1L  = int((lot["n_lines"] >= 1).sum())
n_2Lp = int((lot["n_lines"] >= 2).sum())
print(f"on therapy: {_m1(n_1L)} (>=1L), {_m1(n_2Lp)} (>=2L+)")

# COMMAND ----------
# platinum sensitivity for 2L+: platinum-free interval from the LAST 1L platinum dose to the FIRST
# progression after that platinum (the spec's definition), >183 days = sensitive. Every 2L+ patient gets
# one label so the categories reconcile to total 2L+ (incl. the not-assessable ones).
progdf = run_sql(
    f"SELECT h.ptid, to_date(p.note_date) prog_dt FROM {T('onc_neoplasm_histology')} h "
    f"JOIN {T('onc_progression')} p ON p.neoplasm_histology_key=h.neoplasm_histology_key "
    # a progression EVENT = disease worsening only ('progression'/'recurrence'), current to the note - NOT
    # remission/stable/no-progression (matches sql/reference_pipeline/caseB_ovarian.sql). note_date is the date col.
    f"WHERE lower(trim(p.progression)) IN ('progression','recurrence') AND lower(trim(p.progression_temporal_status))='current' "
    f"AND h.neoplasm_qualifier='actual' AND upper(trim(h.neoplasm_type)) IN ({_uin(OVARIAN_NEO)})")  # ovarian concept only
progdf["prog_dt"] = pd.to_datetime(progdf["prog_dt"], errors="coerce")
prog = progdf.dropna().groupby("ptid")["prog_dt"].apply(lambda s: sorted(s)).to_dict()

def platinum_status(ptid):
    g = meds[meds.ptid == ptid]
    last_plat = g[(g.line == 1) & (g.drug.isin(PLATINUM))]["dt"].max()
    if pd.isna(last_plat):
        return "no prior platinum line"
    after = next((d for d in prog.get(ptid, []) if d > last_plat), None)   # first progression after platinum
    if after is None:
        return "no progression observed"
    return "platinum-sensitive" if (after - last_plat).days > PLATINUM_FREE else "platinum-resistant"

twoLp = lot[lot.n_lines >= 2]["ptid"]
plat = pd.Series([platinum_status(p) for p in twoLp]).value_counts().to_dict()
n_sens = int(plat.get("platinum-sensitive", 0)); n_res = int(plat.get("platinum-resistant", 0))
n_noprog = int(plat.get("no progression observed", 0)); n_noplat = int(plat.get("no prior platinum line", 0))

# COMMAND ----------
# neoadjuvant vs adjuvant - NON-EXCLUSIVE: a patient with therapy both before and soon after surgery counts
# in both. neoadjuvant = any therapy before primary cytoreductive surgery; adjuvant = any within ADJ_WINDOW after.
surg = run_sql(
    f"SELECT pr.ptid, min(to_date(pr.proc_date)) surgery_date FROM {T('procedure')} pr JOIN ({oc}) o ON o.ptid=pr.ptid "
    f"WHERE regexp_replace(upper(trim(pr.proc_cd)),'[^0-9A-Z]','') IN ({_in(_code(SURGERY_CPT))}) "
    # bound BOTH sides to the diagnosis: from dx-30 (not an old primary) to dx+SURG_WINDOW (a later cytoreduction
    # is likely recurrence, not the primary - so it shouldn't drive neoadjuvant/adjuvant classification)
    f"AND to_date(pr.proc_date) BETWEEN date_sub(o.first_dx,30) AND date_add(o.first_dx,{SURG_WINDOW}) GROUP BY pr.ptid")
surg_dt = dict(zip(surg["ptid"], pd.to_datetime(surg["surgery_date"], errors="coerce")))
n_neoadj = n_adj = 0
for ptid, sd in surg_dt.items():
    if pd.isna(sd): continue
    tx = meds[meds.ptid == ptid]["dt"]
    if (tx < sd).any(): n_neoadj += 1
    if ((tx >= sd) & (tx <= sd + pd.Timedelta(days=ADJ_WINDOW))).any(): n_adj += 1

# COMMAND ----------
strata = _mask({
  "total OC": n_total,
  "neoadjuvant (any tx before surgery)": n_neoadj,
  "adjuvant (any tx <=180d after surgery)": n_adj,
  "1L (>=1 line)": n_1L,
  "2L+ (>=2 lines)": n_2Lp,
  "2L+ platinum-sensitive": n_sens,
  "2L+ platinum-resistant": n_res,
  "2L+ no progression observed": n_noprog,
  "2L+ no prior platinum line": n_noplat,
})
out = _outdir(); os.makedirs(out, exist_ok=True)
df = pd.DataFrame([(k, v) for k, v in strata.items()], columns=["stratum", "n_patients"])
df.to_csv(f"{out}/caseB_strata.csv", index=False); print(f"wrote {out}/caseB_strata.csv")
display(df)   # noqa: F821

# COMMAND ----------
# MAGIC %md
# MAGIC ### Notes
# MAGIC - LoT is a simplified gap-based sessionisation (new line after a >120-day break); it does not yet
# MAGIC   separate maintenance (olaparib/niraparib/bevacizumab) from a new line, or detect regimen changes
# MAGIC   within a window. The full rules + thresholds need clinical sign-off.
# MAGIC - Platinum sensitivity uses the platinum-free interval from the last 1L platinum to the first ovarian
# MAGIC   progression after it; >183 days = sensitive. A progression EVENT counts only `progression`/`recurrence`
# MAGIC   that is `current` (not remission/stable/no-progression), restricted to the ovarian concept.
# MAGIC - Treatment and surgery are bounded to the ovarian diagnosis on BOTH sides: meds on/after first OC dx;
# MAGIC   primary cytoreduction within [dx-30, dx+SURG_WINDOW(365)] so a prior cancer's therapy, an old surgery,
# MAGIC   or a much later recurrence surgery doesn't get counted as the primary. SURG_WINDOW needs sign-off.
# MAGIC - To run the other source cohorts, swap the med/dx/proc sources the way Case A's COH config does;
# MAGIC   claims-only cohorts lose the oral-therapy and progression signal.
# MAGIC - Confirmed against the data dict: `onc_progression` cols are `note_date` + `progression` +
# MAGIC   `progression_temporal_status`. Still confirm onc_medications fields and the ovarian surgery CPT set.
>>>>> OPTUM-MC FILE: notebooks/coverage_optum_databricks.py
# Databricks notebook source
# MAGIC %md
# MAGIC # Coverage matrix (Optum MC + Enriched Oncology)
# MAGIC Companion to epi_counts_optum_databricks. For a chosen denominator, what fraction of patients have
# MAGIC each variable, by source. Three parts: coverage by variable, off-the-shelf vs curated lift, and
# MAGIC staging source coverage + overlap. Tumours come from _config_tumours.
# MAGIC Schema is set to the deployed catalog; fill in HOST/HTTP_PATH, a few `# TODO` column spellings and the
# MAGIC biomarker LOINC list. The preflight (below) reports any missing table/column before the big UNION runs.

# COMMAND ----------
# connection + settings (same as the epi-counts notebook)
import os, posixpath, pandas as pd
from databricks import sql

HOST      = "adb-XXXXXXXXXXXX.azuredatabricks.net"      # TODO
HTTP_PATH = "/sql/1.0/warehouses/XXXXXXXXXXXX"          # TODO
TOKEN     = dbutils.notebook.entry_point.getDbutils().notebook().getContext().apiToken().get()  # noqa: F821

CATALOG   = "hive_metastore"
SCHEMA    = "clnprw_optum_mc_full_use"   # one schema: claims/EHR + Enriched Oncology (optum data location.pdf)
CUT       = "2025q4"                      # data cut; tables are t_<name>_<CUT>
def T(n): return f"{CATALOG}.{SCHEMA}.t_{n}_{CUT}"

STUDY_START = None   # cohort dx window; None = all history
STUDY_END   = None
MIN_CELL    = 11     # blank counts below this
STRICT_PREFLIGHT = True   # stop the run if the preflight finds a missing table/column (set False to run anyway)

def run_sql(q):
    with sql.connect(server_hostname=HOST, http_path=HTTP_PATH, access_token=TOKEN) as c, c.cursor() as cur:
        cur.execute(q)
        return pd.DataFrame(cur.fetchall(), columns=[d[0] for d in cur.description])

def preflight(required):
    """Report any missing table/column BEFORE the big UNION ALL runs, so one renamed column is named precisely
    instead of failing the whole report with a cryptic error. Best-effort: never raises. required = list of
    (table_fqn, [cols])."""
    gaps = []
    for tbl, cols in required:
        try:
            have = set(run_sql(f"SHOW COLUMNS IN {tbl}").iloc[:, 0].str.lower())
        except Exception as e:
            gaps.append(f"{tbl}: TABLE NOT FOUND ({str(e)[:80]})"); continue
        miss = [c for c in cols if c.lower() not in have]
        if miss: gaps.append(f"{tbl}: missing column(s) {miss}")
    if gaps:
        print("PREFLIGHT GAPS - fix these before trusting the report:")
        for g in gaps: print("  -", g)
    else:
        print("preflight OK: all required tables/columns present")
    return not gaps

def _outdir():
    try:
        nb = dbutils.notebook.entry_point.getDbutils().notebook().getContext().notebookPath().get()  # noqa: F821
        d  = posixpath.dirname(nb)
        return (d if d.startswith("/Workspace") else "/Workspace" + d) + "/coverage_outputs"
    except Exception:
        return os.path.join(os.getcwd(), "coverage_outputs")

def _in(xs):   return ", ".join("'" + str(x).replace("'", "''") + "'" for x in xs)
def _code(xs): return ["".join(ch for ch in str(x).upper() if ch.isalnum()) for x in xs]
def _uin(xs):  return _in([str(x).upper().strip() for x in xs])
def _window(col):
    parts = []
    if STUDY_START: parts.append(f"to_date({col}) >= date'{STUDY_START}'")
    if STUDY_END:   parts.append(f"to_date({col}) <= date'{STUDY_END}'")
    return (" AND " + " AND ".join(parts)) if parts else ""
_norm_dx = "regexp_replace(upper(trim(diagnosis_cd)),'[^0-9A-Z]','')"

def _finalize(df, denom_n, name, tag):
    raw = df["n_patients"].fillna(0).astype(int)
    sup = (raw > 0) & (raw < MIN_CELL)                       # hide small counts; keep real zeros
    df["n_patients"]   = raw.where(~sup)
    df["pct_of_denom"] = (100.0 * raw / denom_n).round(2).where(~sup)
    df["n_display"]    = [f"<{MIN_CELL}" if s else str(int(v)) for s, v in zip(sup, raw)]
    df["suppressed"]   = sup
    out = _outdir(); os.makedirs(out, exist_ok=True)
    df.to_csv(f"{out}/{name}_{tag}.csv", index=False); print(f"  wrote {out}/{name}_{tag}.csv")
    display(df)   # noqa: F821
    return df

print("schema:", f"{CATALOG}.{SCHEMA}", "| cut:", CUT)

# COMMAND ----------
# denominator: all EO patients, or a tumour cohort
def _broad_sql(icd):
    return (f"SELECT ptid FROM {T('diagnosis')} WHERE diagnosis_status='Diagnosis of' AND diagnosis_cd_type LIKE 'ICD%' "
            f"AND {_norm_dx} IN ({_in(_code(icd))}){_window('diag_date')} "
            f"GROUP BY ptid HAVING count(DISTINCT diag_date) >= 2")

# (ptid, neoplasm_histology_key) for the target tumour. Keyed coverage joins on these so a patient's
# other primary can't contribute its stage/TNM/etc. histology-dependent cancers need the histology on
# the same key as the target site; site-defined ones just need site or histology to match.
def cohort_keys(cfg):
    if not cfg:
        return f"SELECT DISTINCT ptid, neoplasm_histology_key FROM {T('onc_neoplasm_histology')}"
    broad = _broad_sql(cfg["icd"]); hist = cfg.get("histology"); neo = cfg.get("neoplasm")
    base  = f"FROM {T('onc_neoplasm_histology')} h JOIN ({broad}) c ON c.ptid=h.ptid"
    if cfg.get("histology_dependent"):
        sel = (f"SELECT DISTINCT h.ptid, h.neoplasm_histology_key {base} "
               f"WHERE h.histology_qualifier='actual' AND upper(trim(h.histology)) IN ({_uin(hist)})")
        if neo:
            sel += (f" AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM {T('onc_neoplasm_histology')} a2 WHERE a2.neoplasm_histology_key=h.neoplasm_histology_key "
                    f"AND a2.neoplasm_qualifier='actual' AND upper(trim(a2.neoplasm_type)) IN ({_uin(neo)}))")
        return sel
    conds = []
    if neo:  conds.append(f"(h.neoplasm_qualifier='actual' AND upper(trim(h.neoplasm_type)) IN ({_uin(neo)}))")
    if hist: conds.append(f"(h.histology_qualifier='actual' AND upper(trim(h.histology)) IN ({_uin(hist)}))")
    return f"SELECT DISTINCT h.ptid, h.neoplasm_histology_key {base}" + ((" WHERE " + " OR ".join(conds)) if conds else "")

def cohort_pts(cfg):   # distinct-ptid denominator
    if not cfg:                              return f"SELECT DISTINCT ptid FROM {T('onc_neoplasm_histology')}"
    if cfg.get("histology_dependent"):       return f"SELECT DISTINCT ptid FROM ({cohort_keys(cfg)}) k"
    return _broad_sql(cfg["icd"])            # site-defined cancer: the ICD cohort is the denominator

# COMMAND ----------
# (1) coverage by variable and source
def coverage_report(pts, keys, denom_n, tag):
    # concept-key tables: count through the target keys
    keyed = [  # (domain, variable, source, join)
      ("Histology","Histology (actual)","EO off-shelf",   f"JOIN {T('onc_neoplasm_histology')} a ON a.neoplasm_histology_key=k.neoplasm_histology_key AND a.histology_qualifier='actual' AND a.histology IS NOT NULL"),
      ("Staging","Stage group (0-IV)","EO off-shelf",     f"JOIN {T('onc_stage')} s ON s.neoplasm_histology_key=k.neoplasm_histology_key AND s.stage IS NOT NULL"),
      ("Staging","TNM","EO off-shelf",                    f"JOIN {T('onc_tnm')} t ON t.neoplasm_histology_key=k.neoplasm_histology_key AND (t.t IS NOT NULL OR t.n IS NOT NULL OR t.m IS NOT NULL)"),
      ("Staging","Tumour characteristic","EO off-shelf",  f"JOIN {T('onc_characteristic')} c ON c.neoplasm_histology_key=k.neoplasm_histology_key AND c.neoplasm_characteristic IS NOT NULL"),
      ("Staging","Grade","EO off-shelf",                  f"JOIN {T('onc_grade')} g ON g.neoplasm_histology_key=k.neoplasm_histology_key AND g.grade IS NOT NULL"),                # TODO: confirm
      ("Staging","Metastasis (distant)","EO off-shelf",   f"JOIN {T('onc_metastatic_location')} m ON m.neoplasm_histology_key=k.neoplasm_histology_key AND m.mets_qualifier='actual'"),  # TODO: confirm
      ("Progression","Tumour size","EO off-shelf",        f"JOIN {T('onc_tumor_size')} z ON z.neoplasm_histology_key=k.neoplasm_histology_key AND z.tumor_size_1 IS NOT NULL"),  # table confirmed (data dict); TODO confirm size column
      ("Progression","Tumour progression","EO off-shelf", f"JOIN {T('onc_progression')} p ON p.neoplasm_histology_key=k.neoplasm_histology_key AND p.progression IS NOT NULL"),
      ("Progression","Surgical margin","EO off-shelf",    f"JOIN {T('onc_margin')} sm ON sm.neoplasm_histology_key=k.neoplasm_histology_key AND sm.margin_narrative IS NOT NULL"),  # TODO: confirm name + keying
    ]
    # per-patient tables: just join on ptid
    ptid = [
      ("Demographics","Sex","Structured EHR",                f"JOIN {T('patient')} p ON p.ptid=d.ptid AND p.gender IS NOT NULL"),                 # patient table = demographics (data dict)
      ("Demographics","Age / birth year","Structured EHR",   f"JOIN {T('patient')} p ON p.ptid=d.ptid AND p.birth_yr IS NOT NULL"),
      ("Demographics","Race / ethnicity","Structured EHR",   f"JOIN {T('patient')} p ON p.ptid=d.ptid AND (p.race IS NOT NULL OR p.ethnicity IS NOT NULL)"),
      ("Demographics","BMI (structured Obs only)","Struct.",  f"JOIN {T('observation')} o ON o.ptid=d.ptid AND (upper(o.observation_type) LIKE '%BMI%' OR upper(o.observation_type) LIKE '%BODY MASS%')"),  # TODO; could add nlp_measure
      ("Observability","Has enrollment record","Claims",     f"JOIN {T('clm_derived_enrollment')} e ON e.ptid=d.ptid"),                                       # presence only, NOT continuous coverage over a window - a window measure (eligeff/eligend spanning index->follow-up) is a TODO
      ("Outcomes","Death / mortality (OS)","Linked mort.",   f"JOIN {T('patient')} p ON p.ptid=d.ptid AND p.date_of_death IS NOT NULL"),          # date_of_death = YYYYMM; deceased_indicator='1' is the flag alternative
      ("HCRU","Any encounter / visit","EHR (visit)",         f"JOIN {T('visit')} v ON v.ptid=d.ptid"),                                            # visit = structured EHR (claims HCRU is in clm_med_services)
      ("Comorbidity","Confirmed non-cancer dx (breadth)","Structured EHR", f"JOIN {T('diagnosis')} dx ON dx.ptid=d.ptid AND dx.diagnosis_status='Diagnosis of' AND dx.diagnosis_cd_type LIKE 'ICD%' AND dx.diagnosis_cd NOT LIKE 'C%'"),  # EHR diagnosis only (claims dx is clm_med_diagnosis)
      ("Staging","Lymph nodes","EO off-shelf",               f"JOIN {T('onc_lymph_node')} l ON l.ptid=d.ptid"),                                   # stand-alone, no concept key
      # NOTE: the biomarker rows measure TEST/MARKER DOCUMENTATION (a record exists), not a populated or positive
      # result - positive-result coverage is a separate, lower number (see Case A's dMMR positive logic).
      ("Biomarkers","Any biomarker (any record)","EO off-shelf", f"JOIN {T('onc_biomarker')} b ON b.ptid=d.ptid AND b.biomarker IS NOT NULL"),
      ("Biomarkers","PD-L1 (tested)","EO off-shelf",         f"JOIN {T('onc_biomarker')} b ON b.ptid=d.ptid AND upper(b.biomarker) LIKE '%PD-L1%'"),
      ("Biomarkers","dMMR/MSI-H (tested)","EO off-shelf",    f"JOIN {T('onc_biomarker')} b ON b.ptid=d.ptid AND (upper(b.biomarker) LIKE '%MSI%' OR upper(b.biomarker) LIKE '%MMR%' OR upper(b.biomarker) RLIKE 'MLH1|MSH2|MSH6|PMS2')"),
      ("Biomarkers","HPV/p16 (tested)","EO off-shelf",       f"JOIN {T('onc_biomarker')} b ON b.ptid=d.ptid AND (upper(b.biomarker) LIKE '%HPV%' OR upper(b.biomarker) LIKE '%P16%' OR upper(b.biomarker) LIKE '%CDKN2A%')"),
      ("Outcomes","Treatment response (ORR)","EO off-shelf", f"JOIN {T('onc_treatment_response')} r ON r.ptid=d.ptid AND r.response IS NOT NULL"), # TODO: confirm
      ("Treatment","Line of Therapy (IV, supplied)","EO",    f"JOIN {T('onc_line_of_therapy')} lt ON lt.ptid=d.ptid"),                            # TODO: confirm
      ("Treatment","Oncology medications (oral+IV)","EO",    f"JOIN {T('onc_medications')} mm ON mm.ptid=d.ptid"),
      ("Performance","ECOG / performance status","EO",       f"JOIN {T('onc_evaluation_system')} e ON e.ptid=d.ptid AND (upper(e.system_name) LIKE '%ECOG%' OR upper(e.system_name) LIKE '%KARNOFSKY%')"),  # TODO: confirm
      ("Social history","Smoking (NLP SDS only)","SDS",      f"JOIN {T('nlp_sds')} s ON s.ptid=d.ptid AND lower(s.sds_term) LIKE '%smok%'"),       # TODO: confirm
      ("Social history","Alcohol (NLP SDS only)","SDS",      f"JOIN {T('nlp_sds')} s ON s.ptid=d.ptid AND lower(s.sds_term) LIKE '%alcohol%'"),
    ]
    parts  = [f"SELECT '{dom}' AS domain, '{var}' AS variable, '{src}' AS source, count(DISTINCT k.ptid) AS n_patients FROM ({keys}) k {j}" for (dom,var,src,j) in keyed]
    parts += [f"SELECT '{dom}' AS domain, '{var}' AS variable, '{src}' AS source, count(DISTINCT d.ptid) AS n_patients FROM ({pts}) d {j}"  for (dom,var,src,j) in ptid]
    df = run_sql("\nUNION ALL\n".join(parts)).sort_values(["domain","variable"]).reset_index(drop=True)
    return _finalize(df, denom_n, "coverage_report", tag)

# COMMAND ----------
# (2) off-the-shelf vs curated lift
def curation_lift(denom, denom_n, tag):
    bmk  = lambda pat: f"SELECT d.ptid FROM ({denom}) d JOIN {T('onc_biomarker')} b ON b.ptid=d.ptid AND ({pat})"
    labs = lambda pat: f"SELECT d.ptid FROM ({denom}) d JOIN {T('lab')} l ON l.ptid=d.ptid AND ({pat})"            # structured lab: cols LOINC + TEST_NAME (claims lab_results uses LOINC_CD)
    dxc  = lambda pat: (f"SELECT d.ptid FROM ({denom}) d JOIN {T('diagnosis')} dx ON dx.ptid=d.ptid "
                        f"AND dx.diagnosis_status='Diagnosis of' AND dx.diagnosis_cd_type LIKE 'ICD%' AND ({pat})")
    # word boundaries so e.g. \balk\b matches ALK but not "alkaline". The LOINC set is the real driver;
    # the lab-name regex is a fallback. Backslashes are doubled so the Spark string literal keeps them.
    bmk_lab = (r"l.loinc IN ('--biomarker/genomic LOINC value-set--') OR lower(l.test_name) RLIKE "
               r"'pd-?l1|pd-?1|microsatellite|mismatch repair|\\bmsi\\b|\\bmmr\\b|\\bmlh1\\b|\\bmsh2\\b|\\bmsh6\\b|\\bpms2\\b|her-?2|erbb2|\\bbrca[12]?\\b|\\bkras\\b|\\bnras\\b|\\bbraf\\b|\\balk\\b|\\bros1\\b|\\bntrk\\b|\\bret\\b|\\bmet\\b|estrogen receptor|progesterone receptor|\\btmb\\b|ki-?67'")  # TODO: LOINC list
    hpv_cur  = " UNION ".join([bmk("upper(b.biomarker) LIKE '%HPV%' OR upper(b.biomarker) LIKE '%P16%' OR upper(b.biomarker) LIKE '%CDKN2A%'"),
                               labs("l.loinc IN ('--HPV/p16 LOINC list--') OR lower(l.test_name) LIKE '%hpv%' OR lower(l.test_name) LIKE '%p16%'"),
                               dxc(r"dx.diagnosis_cd RLIKE '^B97\\.?7'")])
    dmmr_cur = " UNION ".join([bmk("upper(b.biomarker) LIKE '%MSI%' OR upper(b.biomarker) RLIKE 'MLH1|MSH2|MSH6|PMS2'"),
                               labs("lower(l.test_name) LIKE '%microsatellite%' OR upper(l.test_name) RLIKE 'MLH1|MSH2|MSH6|PMS2'")])
    bmk_cur  = " UNION ".join([f"SELECT d.ptid FROM ({denom}) d JOIN {T('onc_biomarker')} b ON b.ptid=d.ptid AND b.biomarker IS NOT NULL", labs(bmk_lab)])
    sets = [
      ("HPV / p16","off-the-shelf (EO)",       bmk("upper(b.biomarker) LIKE '%HPV%' OR upper(b.biomarker) LIKE '%P16%' OR upper(b.biomarker) LIKE '%CDKN2A%'")),
      ("HPV / p16","curated (EO + labs + dx)", hpv_cur),
      ("dMMR / MSI-H","off-the-shelf (EO)",    bmk("upper(b.biomarker) LIKE '%MSI%' OR upper(b.biomarker) RLIKE 'MLH1|MSH2|MSH6|PMS2'")),
      ("dMMR / MSI-H","curated (EO + labs)",   dmmr_cur),
      ("Any biomarker","off-the-shelf (EO)",   f"SELECT d.ptid FROM ({denom}) d JOIN {T('onc_biomarker')} b ON b.ptid=d.ptid AND b.biomarker IS NOT NULL"),
      ("Any biomarker","curated (EO + labs)",  bmk_cur),
      ("Smoking","off-the-shelf (SDS only)",   f"SELECT d.ptid FROM ({denom}) d JOIN {T('nlp_sds')} s ON s.ptid=d.ptid AND lower(s.sds_term) LIKE '%smok%'"),
      ("Smoking","curated (SDS + obs + dx)",   " UNION ".join([f"SELECT d.ptid FROM ({denom}) d JOIN {T('nlp_sds')} s ON s.ptid=d.ptid AND lower(s.sds_term) LIKE '%smok%'",
                                                                f"SELECT d.ptid FROM ({denom}) d JOIN {T('observation')} o ON o.ptid=d.ptid AND (lower(o.observation_type) LIKE '%smok%' OR lower(o.observation_type) LIKE '%tobacco%')",
                                                                dxc(r"dx.diagnosis_cd RLIKE '^Z72\\.?0|^F17|^Z87\\.?891'")])),
      ("Alcohol","off-the-shelf (SDS only)",   f"SELECT d.ptid FROM ({denom}) d JOIN {T('nlp_sds')} s ON s.ptid=d.ptid AND lower(s.sds_term) LIKE '%alcohol%'"),
      ("Alcohol","curated (SDS + obs + dx)",   " UNION ".join([f"SELECT d.ptid FROM ({denom}) d JOIN {T('nlp_sds')} s ON s.ptid=d.ptid AND lower(s.sds_term) LIKE '%alcohol%'",
                                                                f"SELECT d.ptid FROM ({denom}) d JOIN {T('observation')} o ON o.ptid=d.ptid AND lower(o.observation_type) LIKE '%alcohol%'",
                                                                dxc(r"dx.diagnosis_cd RLIKE '^F10|^Z72\\.?1'")])),
    ]
    union = "\nUNION ALL\n".join(
        f"SELECT '{var}' AS variable, '{layer}' AS layer, count(DISTINCT ptid) AS n_patients FROM ({s}) t"
        for (var, layer, s) in sets)
    df = run_sql(union).sort_values(["variable","layer"]).reset_index(drop=True)
    return _finalize(df, denom_n, "curation_lift", tag)

# COMMAND ----------
# (3) staging source coverage + how much the tables overlap on the same patients
def staging_sources_and_overlap(keys, pts, denom_n, tag):
    # EO staging on the target keys; the structured dx signal over the patient denominator
    s_stage = f"SELECT DISTINCT k.ptid FROM ({keys}) k JOIN {T('onc_stage')} s ON s.neoplasm_histology_key=k.neoplasm_histology_key WHERE s.stage IS NOT NULL"
    s_tnm   = f"SELECT DISTINCT k.ptid FROM ({keys}) k JOIN {T('onc_tnm')} t ON t.neoplasm_histology_key=k.neoplasm_histology_key WHERE t.t IS NOT NULL OR t.n IS NOT NULL OR t.m IS NOT NULL"
    s_char  = f"SELECT DISTINCT k.ptid FROM ({keys}) k JOIN {T('onc_characteristic')} c ON c.neoplasm_histology_key=k.neoplasm_histology_key WHERE c.neoplasm_characteristic IS NOT NULL"
    s_icd   = f"SELECT DISTINCT d.ptid FROM ({pts}) d JOIN {T('diagnosis')} dx ON dx.ptid=d.ptid WHERE dx.diagnosis_status='Diagnosis of' AND dx.diagnosis_cd_type LIKE 'ICD%' AND dx.diagnosis_cd RLIKE '^C7[789]'"
    src = "\nUNION ALL\n".join([
        f"SELECT 'onc_stage' src, count(*) n_patients FROM ({s_stage}) t",
        f"SELECT 'onc_tnm (TNM)', count(*) FROM ({s_tnm}) t",
        f"SELECT 'onc_characteristic', count(*) FROM ({s_char}) t",
        f"SELECT 'structured 2ndary-neoplasm dx (C77-C79)', count(*) FROM ({s_icd}) t",
        f"SELECT 'ANY EO staging table', count(*) FROM (({s_stage}) UNION ({s_tnm}) UNION ({s_char})) t",
        f"SELECT 'ANY staging signal incl. structured dx', count(*) FROM (({s_stage}) UNION ({s_tnm}) UNION ({s_char}) UNION ({s_icd})) t",
    ])
    _finalize(run_sql(src).rename(columns={"src":"metric"}), denom_n, "staging_sources", tag)
    # this is who-is-staged-where overlap, not whether the tables agree on the stage value
    ov = "\nUNION ALL\n".join([
        f"SELECT 'Stage & TNM' pair, count(*) n_patients FROM (({s_stage}) INTERSECT ({s_tnm})) t",
        f"SELECT 'Stage only (not in TNM)', count(*) FROM (({s_stage}) EXCEPT ({s_tnm})) t",
        f"SELECT 'TNM only (not in Stage)', count(*) FROM (({s_tnm}) EXCEPT ({s_stage})) t",
        f"SELECT 'Stage & characteristic', count(*) FROM (({s_stage}) INTERSECT ({s_char})) t",
        f"SELECT 'Stage & TNM & characteristic', count(*) FROM ((({s_stage}) INTERSECT ({s_tnm})) INTERSECT ({s_char})) t",
    ])
    return _finalize(run_sql(ov).rename(columns={"pair":"metric"}), denom_n, "staging_overlap", tag)

# COMMAND ----------
# MAGIC %run ./_config_tumours

# COMMAND ----------
# run the all-EO overview, then each tumour
def run_coverage(tag, cfg=None):
    if cfg and not (cfg.get("histology_dependent") or cfg.get("neoplasm") or cfg.get("histology")):
        raise ValueError(f"{tag}: give it 'neoplasm'/'histology' terms in _config_tumours, "
                         f"otherwise keyed coverage picks up other primaries.")
    pts, keys = cohort_pts(cfg), cohort_keys(cfg)
    n = run_sql(f"SELECT count(DISTINCT ptid) n FROM ({pts}) t").iloc[0]["n"]
    print(f"\n######## {tag}: denominator N = {f'<{MIN_CELL}' if 0 < n < MIN_CELL else n} ########")
    if not n: print("  empty denominator - check config / schema names"); return
    coverage_report(pts, keys, n, tag)
    curation_lift(pts, n, tag)
    staging_sources_and_overlap(keys, pts, n, tag)

# preflight: the columns this notebook depends on (so a renamed one is named, not a cryptic union failure)
REQUIRED = [
    (T("onc_neoplasm_histology"), ["ptid","neoplasm_histology_key","neoplasm_type","neoplasm_qualifier","histology","histology_qualifier","note_date"]),
    (T("onc_stage"),     ["neoplasm_histology_key","stage"]),
    (T("onc_tnm"),       ["neoplasm_histology_key","t","n","m"]),
    (T("onc_characteristic"), ["neoplasm_histology_key","neoplasm_characteristic"]),
    (T("onc_grade"),     ["neoplasm_histology_key","grade"]),
    (T("onc_metastatic_location"), ["neoplasm_histology_key","mets_qualifier"]),
    (T("onc_tumor_size"),["neoplasm_histology_key","tumor_size_1"]),
    (T("onc_progression"),["neoplasm_histology_key","progression"]),
    (T("onc_margin"),    ["neoplasm_histology_key","margin_narrative"]),
    (T("onc_biomarker"), ["ptid","biomarker"]),
    (T("onc_lymph_node"),["ptid"]),
    (T("onc_treatment_response"), ["ptid","response"]),
    (T("onc_line_of_therapy"), ["ptid"]),
    (T("onc_medications"),["ptid"]),
    (T("onc_evaluation_system"), ["ptid","system_name"]),
    (T("patient"),       ["ptid","gender","birth_yr","race","ethnicity","date_of_death"]),
    (T("diagnosis"),     ["ptid","diagnosis_status","diagnosis_cd_type","diagnosis_cd","diag_date"]),
    (T("observation"),   ["ptid","observation_type"]),
    (T("visit"),         ["ptid"]),
    (T("clm_derived_enrollment"), ["ptid"]),
    (T("nlp_sds"),       ["ptid","sds_term"]),
    (T("lab"),           ["ptid","loinc","test_name"]),
]
if not preflight(REQUIRED) and STRICT_PREFLIGHT:
    raise RuntimeError("preflight found missing tables/columns (listed above) - fix them, or set "
                       "STRICT_PREFLIGHT=False to run anyway - but note the metrics share one UNION ALL, so a "
                       "still-missing column will fail the whole report, not just that arm (run arms separately to isolate).")

run_coverage("all_eo")
for _name, _cfg in TUMOURS.items():
    run_coverage(_name, _cfg)

# MAGIC   (cut `2025q4`, tables `t_<name>_2025q4`; see `optum data location.pdf` and the data dictionary). Every
# MAGIC   table referenced here exists in that 52-table schema (incl. onc_grade, onc_metastatic_location,
# MAGIC   onc_margin, onc_tumor_size, onc_lymph_node, onc_treatment_response, onc_line_of_therapy,
# MAGIC   onc_evaluation_system, nlp_sds). Demographics + mortality come from `patient` (date_of_death is YYYYMM);
# MAGIC   enrollment from `clm_derived_enrollment` (eligeff/eligend).
# MAGIC - What remains is **column-level**: a few `# TODO`-tagged predicates still need a column-name check on the
# MAGIC   real tables (e.g. onc_grade.grade, onc_tumor_size size column, onc_evaluation_system.system_name,
# MAGIC   nlp_sds.sds_term). The structured `lab` columns are confirmed: `LOINC` + `TEST_NAME` (the *claims*
# MAGIC   lab_results table is the one that uses `LOINC_CD`).
# MAGIC - The biomarker LOINC placeholders must be filled before the curated biomarker rows mean anything.
# MAGIC - Keyed tables (stage/TNM/characteristic/grade/mets/size/progression) go through the concept key;
# MAGIC   biomarker/lymph node/ECOG/response/LoT/meds are per-patient (joined by ptid only). For a multi-primary
# MAGIC   patient those per-patient rows could come from another cancer; the manual suggests attributing them by
# MAGIC   note-date near the cancer's dx - not done here.
# MAGIC - Source layers: this measures EO off-the-shelf/curated plus blended Market Clarity (structured EHR +
# MAGIC   Optum claims). It does NOT split EHR vs Optum-claims vs third-party, so the matrix's third-party
# MAGIC   column and EHR-vs-claims discordance need the per-source switch in `sql/reference_pipeline/`.
# MAGIC - Sense-check on a slice: HPV/p16 under 1%, smoking around 50%, tumour size around 6%.

# Databricks notebook source
# MAGIC %md
# MAGIC # Epi-question counts (Optum MC + Enriched Oncology)
# MAGIC Per cancer: the ID funnel (>=2 ICD dx, then how many have an EO concept) and how many patients
# MAGIC have each variable, as counts + % of cohort. Small cells blanked, written to CSV. Tumours come from
# MAGIC _config_tumours. Schema is set to the deployed catalog; fill in HOST/HTTP_PATH and the per-tumour code
# MAGIC lists first. A preflight reports any missing table/column before the per-question union runs.

# COMMAND ----------
# connection + settings
import os, posixpath, pandas as pd
from databricks import sql

HOST      = "adb-XXXXXXXXXXXX.azuredatabricks.net"      # TODO
HTTP_PATH = "/sql/1.0/warehouses/XXXXXXXXXXXX"          # TODO
TOKEN     = dbutils.notebook.entry_point.getDbutils().notebook().getContext().apiToken().get()  # noqa: F821

CATALOG   = "hive_metastore"
SCHEMA    = "clnprw_optum_mc_full_use"   # one schema: claims/EHR + Enriched Oncology (optum data location.pdf)
CUT       = "2025q4"                      # data cut; tables are t_<name>_<CUT>
def T(n): return f"{CATALOG}.{SCHEMA}.t_{n}_{CUT}"

STUDY_START = None           # cohort dx window (matches coverage_optum_databricks.py); applies to the dx
STUDY_END   = None           #   cohort only, not the joined variable tables. None = all history.
MIN_CELL    = 11             # blank counts below this
STRICT_PREFLIGHT = True      # stop the run if the preflight finds a missing table/column (set False to run anyway)

def run_sql(q):
    with sql.connect(server_hostname=HOST, http_path=HTTP_PATH, access_token=TOKEN) as c, c.cursor() as cur:
        cur.execute(q)
        return pd.DataFrame(cur.fetchall(), columns=[d[0] for d in cur.description])

def preflight(required):
    """Report any missing table/column BEFORE the per-question UNION runs (best-effort; never raises).
    required = list of (table_fqn, [cols])."""
    gaps = []
    for tbl, cols in required:
        try:
            have = set(run_sql(f"SHOW COLUMNS IN {tbl}").iloc[:, 0].str.lower())
        except Exception as e:
            gaps.append(f"{tbl}: TABLE NOT FOUND ({str(e)[:80]})"); continue
        miss = [c for c in cols if c.lower() not in have]
        if miss: gaps.append(f"{tbl}: missing column(s) {miss}")
    if gaps:
        print("PREFLIGHT GAPS - fix these before trusting the counts:")
        for g in gaps: print("  -", g)
    else:
        print("preflight OK: all required tables/columns present")
    return not gaps

def _outdir():
    try:
        nb = dbutils.notebook.entry_point.getDbutils().notebook().getContext().notebookPath().get()  # noqa: F821
        d  = posixpath.dirname(nb)
        return (d if d.startswith("/Workspace") else "/Workspace" + d) + "/epi_outputs"
    except Exception:
        return os.path.join(os.getcwd(), "epi_outputs")

print("schema:", f"{CATALOG}.{SCHEMA}", "| cut:", CUT)

# COMMAND ----------
def _in(xs):   return ", ".join("'" + str(x).replace("'", "''") + "'" for x in xs)
def _code(xs): return ["".join(ch for ch in str(x).upper() if ch.isalnum()) for x in xs]
def _ndc(xs):  return ["".join(ch for ch in str(x) if ch.isdigit()) for x in xs]
def _uin(xs):  return _in([str(x).upper().strip() for x in xs])
def _window(col):
    parts = []
    if STUDY_START: parts.append(f"to_date({col}) >= date'{STUDY_START}'")
    if STUDY_END:   parts.append(f"to_date({col}) <= date'{STUDY_END}'")
    return (" AND " + " AND ".join(parts)) if parts else ""

_norm_dx = "regexp_replace(upper(trim(diagnosis_cd)),'[^0-9A-Z]','')"

def _broad_sql(icd):   # patients with >=2 dated cancer dx
    return (f"SELECT ptid FROM {T('diagnosis')} WHERE diagnosis_status='Diagnosis of' AND diagnosis_cd_type LIKE 'ICD%' "
            f"AND {_norm_dx} IN ({_in(_code(icd))}){_window('diag_date')} "
            f"GROUP BY ptid HAVING count(DISTINCT diag_date) >= 2")

def _keys_sql(icd, histology_dependent, histology, neoplasm):
    # (ptid, neoplasm_histology_key) for the target tumour. Keyed variables join on these keys so a
    # patient's other primary doesn't contribute its stage/TNM/etc. For histology-dependent cancers the
    # histology has to sit on the same key as the target site; site-defined ones just need site or histology.
    broad = _broad_sql(icd)
    base  = f"FROM {T('onc_neoplasm_histology')} h JOIN ({broad}) c ON c.ptid=h.ptid"
    if histology_dependent:
        sel = (f"SELECT DISTINCT h.ptid, h.neoplasm_histology_key {base} "
               f"WHERE h.histology_qualifier='actual' AND upper(trim(h.histology)) IN ({_uin(histology)})")
        if neoplasm:
            sel += (f" AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM {T('onc_neoplasm_histology')} a2 WHERE a2.neoplasm_histology_key=h.neoplasm_histology_key "
                    f"AND a2.neoplasm_qualifier='actual' AND upper(trim(a2.neoplasm_type)) IN ({_uin(neoplasm)}))")
        return sel
    conds = []
    if neoplasm:  conds.append(f"(h.neoplasm_qualifier='actual' AND upper(trim(h.neoplasm_type)) IN ({_uin(neoplasm)}))")
    if histology: conds.append(f"(h.histology_qualifier='actual' AND upper(trim(h.histology)) IN ({_uin(histology)}))")
    return f"SELECT DISTINCT h.ptid, h.neoplasm_histology_key {base}" + ((" WHERE " + " OR ".join(conds)) if conds else "")

def epi_counts(name, icd, histology_dependent=False, histology=None, neoplasm=None, hcpcs=None, ndc=None):
    if not (histology_dependent or neoplasm or histology):
        # bail rather than let keyed counts pick up a patient's other cancer
        raise ValueError(f"{name}: give it 'neoplasm' (and/or 'histology') terms in _config_tumours.")
    broad   = _broad_sql(icd)
    keys    = _keys_sql(icd, histology_dependent, histology, neoplasm)
    n_broad = run_sql(f"SELECT count(*) n FROM ({broad}) t").iloc[0]["n"]
    n_conf  = run_sql(f"SELECT count(DISTINCT ptid) n FROM ({keys}) t").iloc[0]["n"]
    # for NSCLC/SCLC etc. the ICD code is ambiguous, so the confirmed-concept set is the real cohort
    pts     = f"SELECT DISTINCT ptid FROM ({keys}) k" if histology_dependent else broad
    n_denom = n_conf if histology_dependent else n_broad
    _show = lambda n: f"<{MIN_CELL}" if 0 < n < MIN_CELL else str(int(n))
    if not n_denom:
        # empty cohort: still mask small counts, write the same columns, and say why coverage is blank
        print(f"  {name}: nothing in the cohort (dx={_show(n_broad)}, concept={_show(n_conf)}); skipping coverage.")
        f = pd.DataFrame([("Q1 identification", ">=2 confirmed ICD dx dates", n_broad),
                          ("Q1 identification", "+ EO target concept", n_conf)], columns=["epi_q","metric","n_patients"])
        raw = f["n_patients"].fillna(0).astype(int); sup = (raw > 0) & (raw < MIN_CELL)
        f["n_patients"] = raw.where(~sup); f["pct_of_cohort"] = None
        f["n_display"] = [f"<{MIN_CELL}" if s else str(int(v)) for s, v in zip(sup, raw)]
        f["suppressed"] = sup; f.insert(0, "denom_n", 0); f.insert(0, "tumour", name)
        out = _outdir(); os.makedirs(out, exist_ok=True)
        f.to_csv(f"{out}/epi_counts_{name}.csv", index=False); print(f"  wrote {out}/epi_counts_{name}.csv")
        display(f)   # noqa: F821
        return f
    has_eo  = histology_dependent or bool(neoplasm) or bool(histology)
    conf_lbl = "+ EO target-concept confirmed" if has_eo else "+ has any EO concept (NOT target-restricted)"
    print(f"\n== {name} == ICD dx={_show(n_broad)}, EO target concept={_show(n_conf)} "
          f"(denominator={'concept-confirmed' if histology_dependent else 'ICD cohort'}={_show(n_denom)})")
    funnel = pd.DataFrame([("Q1 identification", ">=2 confirmed ICD dx dates", n_broad),
                           ("Q1 identification", conf_lbl, n_conf)], columns=["epi_q","metric","n_patients"])

    # these tables hang off the concept key, so count them through the target keys
    keyed = [
      ("Q2 staging","Stage group (0-IV)", f"JOIN {T('onc_stage')} s ON s.neoplasm_histology_key=k.neoplasm_histology_key AND s.stage IS NOT NULL"),
      ("Q2 staging","TNM",                f"JOIN {T('onc_tnm')} t ON t.neoplasm_histology_key=k.neoplasm_histology_key AND (t.t IS NOT NULL OR t.n IS NOT NULL OR t.m IS NOT NULL)"),
      ("Q5 outcomes","Progression",       f"JOIN {T('onc_progression')} p ON p.neoplasm_histology_key=k.neoplasm_histology_key AND p.progression IS NOT NULL"),
    ]
    # these are per-patient, so just join on ptid
    ptid = [
      ("Q3 biomarkers","Any biomarker",   f"JOIN {T('onc_biomarker')} b ON b.ptid=d.ptid AND b.biomarker IS NOT NULL"),
      ("Q3 biomarkers","HPV / p16",       f"JOIN {T('onc_biomarker')} b ON b.ptid=d.ptid AND (upper(b.biomarker) LIKE '%HPV%' OR upper(b.biomarker) LIKE '%P16%' OR upper(b.biomarker) LIKE '%CDKN2A%')"),
      ("Q3 biomarkers","dMMR / MSI-H",    f"JOIN {T('onc_biomarker')} b ON b.ptid=d.ptid AND (upper(b.biomarker) LIKE '%MSI%' OR upper(b.biomarker) RLIKE 'MLH1|MSH2|MSH6|PMS2')"),
      ("Q3 biomarkers","PD-L1",           f"JOIN {T('onc_biomarker')} b ON b.ptid=d.ptid AND upper(b.biomarker) LIKE '%PD-L1%'"),
      ("Q4 social history","Smoking (NLP SDS)", f"JOIN {T('nlp_sds')} s ON s.ptid=d.ptid AND lower(s.sds_term) LIKE '%smok%'"),         # TODO: confirm
      ("Q4 social history","Alcohol (NLP SDS)", f"JOIN {T('nlp_sds')} s ON s.ptid=d.ptid AND lower(s.sds_term) LIKE '%alcohol%'"),
      ("Q5 outcomes","Treatment response",f"JOIN {T('onc_treatment_response')} r ON r.ptid=d.ptid AND r.response IS NOT NULL"),         # TODO: confirm
      ("Q5 outcomes","Death / mortality (OS)", f"JOIN {T('patient')} pt ON pt.ptid=d.ptid AND pt.date_of_death IS NOT NULL"),           # patient.date_of_death = YYYYMM (data dict)
      ("Q6 treatment","Any oncology med", f"JOIN {T('onc_medications')} mm ON mm.ptid=d.ptid"),
      ("Q6 treatment","Line of Therapy (IV)", f"JOIN {T('onc_line_of_therapy')} lt ON lt.ptid=d.ptid"),                                 # TODO: confirm
      ("Q7 performance","ECOG / perf. status", f"JOIN {T('onc_evaluation_system')} e ON e.ptid=d.ptid AND (upper(e.system_name) LIKE '%ECOG%' OR upper(e.system_name) LIKE '%KARNOFSKY%')"),  # TODO: confirm
      ("Q8 continuum","Has enrollment record", f"JOIN {T('clm_derived_enrollment')} en ON en.ptid=d.ptid"),                                         # presence only, NOT continuous coverage over a window (window measure is a TODO)
      ("Q8 continuum","Any encounter / HCRU",  f"JOIN {T('visit')} v ON v.ptid=d.ptid"),                                                # TODO: confirm
    ]
    if hcpcs:
        ptid.append(("Q6 treatment","Treated w/ study regimen (HCPCS, EHR proc only)",
            f"JOIN {T('procedure')} pr ON pr.ptid=d.ptid AND regexp_replace(upper(trim(pr.proc_cd)),'[^0-9A-Z]','') IN ({_in(_code(hcpcs))})"))  # structured EHR procedure only; claims HCPCS live in clm_med_services - UNION for full HCRU coverage
    if ndc:
        ptid.append(("Q6 treatment","Treated w/ study regimen (NDC)",
            f"JOIN {T('onc_medications')} mm ON mm.ptid=d.ptid AND regexp_replace(mm.ndc,'[^0-9]','') IN ({_in(_ndc(ndc))})"))                    # TODO: ndc column

    parts  = [f"SELECT '{q}' AS epi_q, '{m}' AS metric, count(DISTINCT k.ptid) AS n_patients FROM ({keys}) k {j}" for (q,m,j) in keyed]
    parts += [f"SELECT '{q}' AS epi_q, '{m}' AS metric, count(DISTINCT d.ptid) AS n_patients FROM ({pts}) d {j}" for (q,m,j) in ptid]
    df = pd.concat([funnel, run_sql("\nUNION ALL\n".join(parts))], ignore_index=True)

    raw = df["n_patients"].fillna(0).astype(int)
    sup = (raw > 0) & (raw < MIN_CELL)                                    # hide small counts, keep real zeros
    df["n_patients"]    = raw.where(~sup)
    df["pct_of_cohort"] = (100.0 * raw / n_denom).round(1).where(~sup)
    df.loc[df["epi_q"].eq("Q1 identification"), "pct_of_cohort"] = None    # funnel is counts only
    df["n_display"]     = [f"<{MIN_CELL}" if s else str(int(v)) for s, v in zip(sup, raw)]
    df["suppressed"]    = sup
    denom_disp = f"<{MIN_CELL}" if 0 < n_denom < MIN_CELL else n_denom     # don't leak a tiny cohort size
    df.insert(0, "denom_n", denom_disp); df.insert(0, "tumour", name)
    df = df.sort_values(["epi_q", "metric"]).reset_index(drop=True)

    out = _outdir(); os.makedirs(out, exist_ok=True)
    df.to_csv(f"{out}/epi_counts_{name}.csv", index=False); print(f"  wrote {out}/epi_counts_{name}.csv")
    display(df)   # noqa: F821
    return df

# COMMAND ----------
# MAGIC %run ./_config_tumours

_ok = preflight([
    (T("diagnosis"),     ["ptid","diagnosis_status","diagnosis_cd_type","diagnosis_cd","diag_date"]),
    (T("onc_neoplasm_histology"), ["ptid","neoplasm_histology_key","neoplasm_type","neoplasm_qualifier","histology"]),
    (T("onc_stage"),     ["neoplasm_histology_key","stage"]),
    (T("onc_tnm"),       ["neoplasm_histology_key","t","n","m"]),
    (T("onc_progression"),["neoplasm_histology_key","progression"]),
    (T("onc_biomarker"), ["ptid","biomarker"]),
    (T("nlp_sds"),       ["ptid","sds_term"]),

    (T("patient"),       ["ptid","date_of_death"]),

    (T("procedure"),     ["ptid","proc_cd"]),
])

                       "STRICT_PREFLIGHT=False to run anyway - but note the metrics share one UNION, so a "
                       "still-missing column will fail the whole report, not just that arm (run arms separately to isolate).")

for _name, _cfg in TUMOURS.items():
    epi_counts(_name, **_cfg)

# MAGIC - histology_dependent=True for cancers the ICD code can't pin down (NSCLC/SCLC/HNSCC); give those a

>>>>> OPTUM-MC FILE: sample code.txt
# Databricks notebook source
# MAGIC %md
# MAGIC # BEHOLD site finder — bare core
# MAGIC The minimal version of `behold_sites.py`: **diagnosis ∩ treatment** in one date window, counted by
# MAGIC **treating site** (provider name + address + ZIP5), small-cell suppressed, exported to CSV.
# MAGIC
# MAGIC One provider rule (medical → **billing**, pharmacy → **prescribing**); **no** iterations / ranking /
# MAGIC lineage / heat-map, and **no schema probing** — the provider columns are hardcoded. NDC lists ship
# MAGIC empty; fill them from the **NDC-finder cell at the bottom of this notebook**.

# COMMAND ----------
# ===== connection + settings =====
import os, posixpath, pandas as pd
from databricks import sql

HOST      = "adb-XXXXXXXXXXXX.azuredatabricks.net"
HTTP_PATH = "/sql/1.0/warehouses/XXXXXXXXXXXX"
TOKEN     = dbutils.notebook.entry_point.getDbutils().notebook().getContext().apiToken().get()  # noqa: F821
CATALOG, SCHEMA = "hive_metastore", "your_openclaims_schema"

STUDY_START = "2025-01-01"   # one window for BOTH diagnosis and treatment (None = all history)
STUDY_END   = None           # None = latest
MIN_CELL    = 11             # drop sites/zips with fewer distinct patients

def run_sql(q):
    with sql.connect(server_hostname=HOST, http_path=HTTP_PATH, access_token=TOKEN) as c, c.cursor() as cur:
        cur.execute(q)
        return pd.DataFrame(cur.fetchall(), columns=[d[0] for d in cur.description])

def _latest(prefix):   # newest <prefix><mon><yyyy> table — HV cuts are cumulative, so latest only
    df  = run_sql(f"SHOW TABLES IN {CATALOG}.{SCHEMA}")
    col = "tableName" if "tableName" in df.columns else df.columns[1]
    mon = {"jan":1,"feb":2,"mar":3,"apr":4,"may":5,"jun":6,"jul":7,"aug":8,"sep":9,"oct":10,"nov":11,"dec":12}
    best, key = None, -1
    for t in (str(x) for x in df[col]):
        s = t[len(prefix):]
        if t.startswith(prefix) and s[:3].lower() in mon and s[3:].isdigit():
            k = int(s[3:]) * 100 + mon[s[:3].lower()]
            if k > key: best, key = t, k
    if not best: raise ValueError(f"no {prefix}* tables in {CATALOG}.{SCHEMA}")
    return f"{CATALOG}.{SCHEMA}.{best}"

MED = _latest("v_medical_claims_"); print("medical:", MED)
try:    RX = _latest("v_pharmacy_claims_"); print("pharmacy:", RX)
except Exception as e:  RX = None; print("pharmacy not available:", e)

def _outdir():   # a 'behold_outputs' folder next to this notebook
    try:
        nb = dbutils.notebook.entry_point.getDbutils().notebook().getContext().notebookPath().get()  # noqa: F821
        d  = posixpath.dirname(nb)
        return (d if d.startswith("/Workspace") else "/Workspace" + d) + "/behold_outputs"
    except Exception:
        return os.path.join(os.getcwd(), "behold_outputs")

# COMMAND ----------
# ===== the core =====
LDR = "(logical_delete_reason IS NULL OR trim(logical_delete_reason) = '')"   # keep real-activity rows only

def _in(xs):   return ", ".join("'" + str(x).replace("'", "''") + "'" for x in xs)
def _code(xs): return ["".join(ch for ch in str(x).upper() if ch.isalnum()) for x in xs]   # ICD / HCPCS
def _ndc(xs):  return ["".join(ch for ch in str(x) if ch.isdigit()) for x in xs]           # NDC -> digits
def _window():
    c, parts = "to_date(date_service)", []
    if STUDY_START: parts.append(f"{c} >= date'{STUDY_START}'")
    if STUDY_END:   parts.append(f"{c} <= date'{STUDY_END}'")
    return (" AND " + " AND ".join(parts)) if parts else ""

def _site_cols(block):   # hvid + the provider site (name/address/city/state/ZIP5) from one hardcoded block
    p = f"prov_{block}_"
    return (f"SELECT hvid, upper(trim({p}name_1)) AS site_name, upper(trim({p}address_1)) AS site_address, "
            f"upper(trim({p}city)) AS site_city, upper(trim({p}state)) AS site_state, "
            f"substr(regexp_replace(coalesce({p}zip,''),'[^0-9]',''),1,5) AS site_zip")

def core(study, dx, hcpcs=None, ndc=None):
    norm_dx = "regexp_replace(upper(trim(diagnosis_code)),'[^0-9A-Z]','')"
    cohort  = (f"SELECT DISTINCT hvid FROM {MED} WHERE {norm_dx} IN ({_in(_code(dx))}) "
               f"AND diagnosis_code_qual='02'{_window()} AND {LDR}")
    n_dx = run_sql(f"SELECT count(DISTINCT hvid) n FROM ({cohort}) t").iloc[0]["n"]
    print(f"\n== {study} == {n_dx} diagnosed (window {STUDY_START or 'min'} .. {STUDY_END or 'latest'})")

    parts = []
    if hcpcs or ndc:                                   # medical: HCPCS and/or NDC -> billing provider
        conds = []
        if hcpcs: conds.append(f"(regexp_replace(upper(trim(procedure_code)),'[^0-9A-Z]','') IN ({_in(_code(hcpcs))}) AND procedure_code_qual='HC')")
        if ndc:   conds.append(f"(regexp_replace(ndc_code,'[^0-9]','') IN ({_in(_ndc(ndc))}))")
        parts.append(f"{_site_cols('billing')} FROM {MED} "
                     f"WHERE ({' OR '.join(conds)}){_window()} AND {LDR} AND hvid IN ({cohort})")
    if ndc and RX:                                     # pharmacy: NDC -> prescribing provider
        parts.append(f"{_site_cols('prescribing')} FROM {RX} "
                     f"WHERE regexp_replace(ndc_code,'[^0-9]','') IN ({_in(_ndc(ndc))}){_window()} "
                     f"AND {LDR} AND hvid IN ({cohort})")
    if not parts:
        print("  no treatment codes — set HCPCS and/or NDC"); return

    # pull treatment rows that have a full provider site once, then count distinct patients per site / ZIP
    rows = run_sql("WITH rows AS (\n" + "\nUNION ALL\n".join(parts) + "\n)\n"
                   "SELECT hvid, site_name, site_address, site_city, site_state, site_zip FROM rows\n"
                   "WHERE site_name<>'' AND site_address<>'' AND site_state<>'' AND length(site_zip)=5 "
                   "AND site_zip<>'00000'")
    n_tx  = rows["hvid"].nunique()
    keys  = ["site_name", "site_address", "site_city", "site_state", "site_zip"]
    sites = rows.groupby(keys)["hvid"].nunique().reset_index(name="n_patients")
    sites = sites[sites["n_patients"] >= MIN_CELL].sort_values("n_patients", ascending=False)
    zips  = rows.groupby(["site_state", "site_zip"])["hvid"].nunique().reset_index(name="n_patients")
    zips  = zips[zips["n_patients"] >= MIN_CELL].sort_values("n_patients", ascending=False)
    print(f"  {n_tx} treated at a mappable site; {len(sites)} sites, {len(zips)} ZIP5s with >= {MIN_CELL} patients")

    out = _outdir(); os.makedirs(out, exist_ok=True)
    tag = f"{study}__{STUDY_START or 'min'}_to_{STUDY_END or 'latest'}__{MED.split('.')[-1]}"
    sites.to_csv(f"{out}/site_counts_{tag}.csv", index=False)
    zips.to_csv(f"{out}/zip_counts_{tag}.csv", index=False)
    print(f"  exported -> {out}/site_counts_{tag}.csv")
    if len(sites): display(sites)   # noqa: F821  (display() can't infer a schema from an empty DataFrame)
    return sites, zips

# COMMAND ----------
# ===== studies — ICD-10 dx + HCPCS; paste 11-digit NDCs (see the NDC-finder cell at the bottom) =====
OVARIAN     = ["C56.1","C56.2","C56.3","C56.9","C57.00","C57.01","C57.02","C48.1","C48.2","C48.8"]
ENDOMETRIAL = ["C54.0","C54.1","C54.2","C54.3","C54.8","C54.9","C55"]

core("224038", ENDOMETRIAL, hcpcs=["J9271","J9272","J9045","J9267"], ndc=[])   # + lenvatinib, megestrol
core("224031", OVARIAN,     hcpcs=["J8705","J9001","J9201","J9267","J9351"], ndc=[])
core("224037", OVARIAN,     hcpcs=["J9035","Q5107","Q5118","Q5126"], ndc=[])   # + PARP (niraparib/olaparib/rucaparib)
core("224039", ENDOMETRIAL, hcpcs=["J9272"], ndc=[])
core("224040", OVARIAN,     hcpcs=["J9045","J9060","J9263","J9001","J9171","J9201","J9267","J9035","Q5107","Q5118","Q5126"], ndc=[])

# COMMAND ----------
# ===== (reference) find real NDCs for a drug — paste results into the ndc=[...] lists above =====
# Pulls actual ndc_code values for a drug from YOUR pharmacy feed (also confirms the pharmacy arm works).
# Set DRUG, run; if the feed has no drug-name column, NDCs must come from an external RxNorm/FDA crosswalk.
DRUG = "lenvatinib"   # generic or brand, e.g. "lenvatinib" / "megestrol" / "niraparib"
if not RX:
    print("pharmacy table not resolved (see the connection cell).")
else:
    _cols  = set(run_sql(f"SHOW COLUMNS IN {RX}").iloc[:, 0].str.lower())
    _names = [c for c in ("ndc_desc", "ndc_description", "drug_name", "label_name", "brand_name",
                          "generic_name", "product_name") if c in _cols]
    if _names:
        _w = " OR ".join(f"upper(cast({c} AS string)) LIKE '%{DRUG.upper()}%'" for c in _names)
        print(f"NDCs in {RX.split('.')[-1]} matching '{DRUG}' — paste the 11-digit ndc_code into ndc=[...]:")
        display(run_sql(f"SELECT {', '.join(_names)}, ndc_code, count(*) n FROM {RX} WHERE {_w} "  # noqa: F821
                        f"GROUP BY {', '.join(_names)}, ndc_code ORDER BY n DESC LIMIT 50"))
    else:
        print(f"No drug-name column in {RX.split('.')[-1]} — NDCs must come from an external RxNorm/FDA "
              f"crosswalk. Columns: {sorted(_cols)}")
        display(run_sql(f"SELECT ndc_code, count(*) n FROM {RX} GROUP BY 1 ORDER BY n DESC LIMIT 20"))  # noqa: F821
