# Reference pipeline (optional appendix)

These are the **full, parameterized** SQL scripts — the end-to-end *design* of the two case studies — but they
are **NOT runnable as-is** (see Status). They are **not** the primary deliverable: the case studies are
presented as **illustrative worked examples** in `docs/case_studies/`. Keep these only if/when someone wants to
actually *run* the analysis end-to-end across all six data-source cohorts.

Status: **design-complete; execution scaffolded — not runnable as-is.** Before use, wire the per-cohort
source placeholders to real tables (incl. the Optum-claims union for cohorts 2a/2b; cohorts 3a/3b are
not-assessable without a third-party-claims asset), validate the SQL dialect (recursion + array functions), and
resolve the open items in `docs/optum-gsk-questions.md`. See `docs/review-notes.md` for the full hardening history.

Run order (per cohort): `00_reset.sql` → `code_lists.sql` → `lib_cancer_cohort.sql` → `caseA_colon.sql`
or `caseB_ovarian.sql` → `priority_tumour_counts.sql`.

| File | Role |
|---|---|
| `code_lists.sql` | DRAFT value-set tables (ICD/CPT/HCPCS, drug classes, biosimilars, biomarker labs, neoplasm/histology terms) |
| `lib_cancer_cohort.sql` | shared building blocks: six-cohort source switch, cohort entry, EO concept hub, reusable flags |
| `caseA_colon.sql` | colon dMMR/MSI-H 6-step attrition |
| `caseB_ovarian.sql` | ovarian total / custom LoT / platinum sensitivity |
| `priority_tumour_counts.sql` | per-tumour identified + histology-confirmed counts |
| `00_reset.sql` | teardown for re-running per cohort |
>>>>> OPTUM-MC FILE: sql/reference_pipeline/caseA_colon.sql
-- ============================================================================
-- caseA_colon.sql — dMMR/MSI-H locally advanced colon cancer (6-step attrition)
-- Run order: code_lists.sql -> lib_cancer_cohort.sql (with params.tumour='colon'
--   and params.cohort set to one of 1/2a/2b/3a/3b/4) -> this file.
-- Tumour ICD/surgery/drug sets come from the code_lists.sql value sets.
-- STATUS: executable-LATER; value sets are DRAFT pending clinical sign-off.
--
-- Metastatic exclusion = DISTANT disease only (flag_mets uses C78-C79 + keyed
-- Metastasis_Location, and EXCLUDES C77 regional nodes) so that N+ locally
-- advanced patients are retained. Steps depending on onc_* (3,5) only populate
-- for use_onc cohorts -> the attrition gap IS the feasibility/enrichment finding.
-- ============================================================================

-- STEP 1 — colon-cancer surgery: ALL qualifying colectomy dates (not just min) --
-- (keeping every candidate lets STEP 2 pick the earliest *qualifying* surgery.)
create table a_step1_surgery as
select distinct p.ptid, p.proc_date as surgery_date
from src_proc p
inner join cohort_dx d on p.ptid = d.ptid
join vs_surgery v
     on v.tumour = 'colon'
    and ( (v.proc_cd_type = p.proc_cd_type and v.proc_cd = p.proc_cd)
       or (v.proc_cd_type = 'ICD10PCS' and regexp_like(p.proc_cd, '^0DT[E-Q]')) );

-- STEP 2 — earliest surgery with newly-diagnosed colon cancer <=183 d prior -----
-- Selects the earliest QUALIFYING surgery (an older non-qualifying colectomy no
-- longer disqualifies a patient whose later cancer-directed surgery fits the window).
create table a_step2_newdx as
select q.ptid, min(q.surgery_date) as surgery_date, q.first_dx_date
from (
    select s.ptid, s.surgery_date, d.first_dx_date
    from a_step1_surgery s
    join cohort_dx d on s.ptid = d.ptid
    where d.first_dx_date between date_add('day', -183, s.surgery_date) and s.surgery_date
      and not exists (                                   -- washout: no colon dx before window
          select 1 from src_diag x
          where x.ptid = s.ptid
            and x.diagnosis_status = 'Diagnosis of'
            and regexp_like(x.diagnosis_cd,'^C1[89]|^C20')
            and x.diag_date < date_add('day', -183, s.surgery_date) )
) q
group by q.ptid, q.first_dx_date;

-- STEP 3 — stage T1-4N+ or T4N0 (else lymph nodes > 0), INITIAL / PERI-OPERATIVE
-- Window = [first_dx - 30d, surgery_date + 90d] so it captures BOTH pre-op clinical
-- staging AND the post-op PATHOLOGICAL stage from the surgical specimen (the gold
-- standard for a surgical cohort). Later notes are restaging and are excluded.
-- has_pathologic is REPORTED (not used to select) so analysts can prefer path stage.
-- OPEN (GSK): confirm the peri-operative window (optum-gsk-questions.md #17).
create table a_step3_stage as
select n.ptid, n.surgery_date,
       max(case when lower(coalesce(st.t_prefix,st.stage_prefix)) like 'p%' then 1 else 0 end) as has_pathologic
from a_step2_newdx n
join cohort_stage st on n.ptid = st.ptid
-- onc_lymph_node is STAND-ALONE (no neoplasm_histology_key, manual 2.1) -> join on
-- ptid + note_date proximity to the index window, NOT on the key.
left join {{schema_onc}}.onc_lymph_node ln
       on st.ptid = ln.ptid
      and ln.note_date between date_add('day', -30, n.first_dx_date) and date_add('day', 90, n.surgery_date)
where st.stage_note_date <= date_add('day', 90, n.surgery_date)     -- incl. post-op path stage
  and st.stage_note_date >= date_add('day', -30, n.first_dx_date)   -- near index dx
  and (
        try_cast(regexp_extract(st.t,'[1-4]') as integer) between 1 and 4
        and (
              -- parse the numeric out of N (handles 'N1','1a','N1a','x'); 'x'->null
              try_cast(regexp_extract(st.n,'[0-9]') as integer) > 0                  -- N+
           or (try_cast(regexp_extract(st.t,'[1-4]') as integer) = 4
               and try_cast(regexp_extract(st.n,'[0-9]') as integer) = 0)            -- T4N0
           or (regexp_extract(st.n,'[0-9]') is null and ln.lymph_numeric > 0)        -- N missing/x -> nodes>0
            )
      )
group by n.ptid, n.surgery_date;
-- NOTE: cohorts 1-3 (use_onc=false) have no onc_tnm/onc_lymph_node and will
--       largely fail this step from claims/EHR alone -> document as the headline
--       feasibility result (staging requires Enriched Oncology).

-- STEP 4 — no DISTANT metastatic diagnosis prior to surgery ------------------
create table a_step4_nomets as
select s3.ptid, s3.surgery_date
from a_step3_stage s3
left join flag_mets m on s3.ptid = m.ptid          -- flag_mets = C78-C79 + keyed mets (distant)
where m.first_mets_date is null
   or m.first_mets_date >= s3.surgery_date;

-- STEP 5 — dMMR+ or MSI-H (cancer-windowed in flag_dmmr_msi) ------------------
create table a_step5_dmmr as
select s4.ptid, s4.surgery_date
from a_step4_nomets s4
join flag_dmmr_msi f on s4.ptid = f.ptid;

-- STEP 6 — IO use after diagnosis and before (distant) metastatic diagnosis ---
create table a_step6_io as
select s5.ptid, s5.surgery_date
from a_step5_dmmr s5
join cohort_dx dx on s5.ptid = dx.ptid
left join flag_mets m on s5.ptid = m.ptid
join flag_drug io on s5.ptid = io.ptid and io.exposure_strength >= 3   -- confirmed/likely
join vs_drug vd on vd.generic = io.generic_desc and vd.is_io = true    -- IO agents from value set
where io.first_confirmed_date > dx.first_dx_date                       -- confirmed exposure date
  and (m.first_mets_date is null or io.first_confirmed_date < m.first_mets_date);

-- ATTRITION SUMMARY (tag with the cohort id when you re-run per definition) ----
select 'step1_surgery' as step, count(distinct ptid) as n from a_step1_surgery
union all select 'step2_newdx',  count(distinct ptid) from a_step2_newdx
union all select 'step3_stage',  count(distinct ptid) from a_step3_stage
union all select 'step4_nomets', count(distinct ptid) from a_step4_nomets
union all select 'step5_dmmr',   count(distinct ptid) from a_step5_dmmr
union all select 'step6_io',     count(distinct ptid) from a_step6_io;
>>>>> OPTUM-MC FILE: sql/reference_pipeline/caseB_ovarian.sql
-- ============================================================================
-- caseB_ovarian.sql — Ovarian cancer: total, custom LoT, platinum sensitivity
-- Run order: code_lists.sql -> lib_cancer_cohort.sql (params.tumour='ovarian',
--   params.cohort set) -> this file. STATUS: design-complete; value sets DRAFT.
--
-- !!! SQL-ENGINE NOTE: the LoT scan uses WITH RECURSIVE + array functions.
--   Trino/Athena recursion is EXPERIMENTAL with a default depth (~10) that WILL
--   TRUNCATE long histories -> raise max_recursion_depth or implement the LoT
--   procedurally (Python/stored proc). Treat as validated-PENDING, not engine-final.
-- ============================================================================

-- TOTAL OC — ICD-identified for ALL cohorts (NOT gated on EO histology), with a
-- histology_confirmed flag that is only set for use_onc cohorts. This lets the
-- six-cohort comparison count ovarian patients in EHR/claims-only cohorts, with
-- NLP histology as an enrichment metric (see Case B spec / review-notes R-B1).
create table b_total_oc as
select d.ptid, d.first_dx_date,
       case when (select use_onc from cohort_meta) = false then null   -- non-EO: histology NOT assessable
            when nh.ptid is not null then 1 else 0 end as histology_confirmed
from cohort_dx d                                  -- cohort_dx already = ovarian ICD, all cohorts
-- histology_confirmed requires an ACTUAL ovarian HISTOLOGY (not a neoplasm-only match),
-- so the field name doesn't overstate the evidence.
left join (select distinct ptid from cohort_neo_hist
           where histology_qualifier = 'actual'
             and histology in (select histology from vs_histology where tumour='ovarian')) nh
       on d.ptid = nh.ptid;

-- PRIMARY CYTOREDUCTIVE SURGERY date (from vs_surgery) -----------------------
create table b_surgery as
select oc.ptid, min(p.proc_date) as surgery_date
from b_total_oc oc
join src_proc p on oc.ptid = p.ptid
join vs_surgery v on v.tumour='ovarian' and v.proc_cd_type=p.proc_cd_type and v.proc_cd=p.proc_cd
where p.proc_date between date_add('day', -30, oc.first_dx_date)      -- PRIMARY window: near/after dx ...
                     and date_add('day', 365, oc.first_dx_date)      -- ... and <=1y (provisional; GSK)
  and v.descr not like '%recurrent%'                                 -- exclude recurrent-resection codes (58957/58958)
group by oc.ptid;

-- FIGO STAGE (extent I-IV) from onc_stage near dx — EO-only, portable ---------
create table b_figo_stage as
select ptid, figo_stage, stage_prefix from (
    select s.ptid, s.stage as figo_stage, s.stage_prefix,
           row_number() over (partition by s.ptid
               order by case when s.stage_note_date <= date_add('day',30,oc.first_dx_date) then 0 else 1 end,
                        s.stage_note_date) as rn
    from cohort_stage s
    join b_total_oc oc on s.ptid = oc.ptid
    where s.stage_note_date between date_add('day',-30,oc.first_dx_date)    -- bound to the initial-staging
                               and date_add('day', 90,oc.first_dx_date)    -- window (avoid stale/old rows)
) z where rn = 1;

-- DRUG EXPOSURE: canonicalize FIRST, then classify; window to post-diagnosis --
create table b_drug as
select x.ptid, x.action_date, x.drug, x.exposure_strength, vd.is_platinum, vd.is_maintenance_candidate
from (
    select m.ptid, m.action_date,
           coalesce(bs.canonical_generic, upper(trim(m.generic_desc))) as drug,
           case m.action when 'administered' then 4 when 'filled' then 4
                         when 'started' then 3 when 'is on' then 3
                         when 'prescribed' then 2 else 1 end as exposure_strength
    from src_med m
    join b_total_oc oc on m.ptid = oc.ptid
    left join vs_biosimilar bs on upper(trim(m.generic_desc)) = bs.source_name
    where m.action not in ('stopped','suspended')
      and m.action_date >= date_add('day', -30, oc.first_dx_date)
) x
join vs_drug vd on vd.generic = x.drug
     and vd.drug_class in ('platinum','taxane','parp','anti_vegf',
                           'antimetabolite','anthracycline','topo1_inhibitor');

-- CUSTOM LoT — recursive line scan (90-day gap + 30-day regimen + switch) -----
create table b_lines as
with recursive
days as (
    select ptid, action_date, array_agg(distinct drug) as drugs
    from b_drug where exposure_strength >= 3
    group by ptid, action_date
),
ordered as (
    select ptid, action_date, drugs,
           row_number() over (partition by ptid order by action_date) as rn from days
),
walk as (
    select ptid, action_date, drugs, rn, 1 as line_no, action_date as line_start, drugs as regimen
    from ordered where rn = 1
    union all
    select o.ptid, o.action_date, o.drugs, o.rn,
           case when date_diff('day', w.action_date, o.action_date) > 90
                  or ( o.action_date > date_add('day',30,w.line_start)
                       and cardinality(array_except(o.drugs, w.regimen)) > 0 )
                then w.line_no + 1 else w.line_no end,
           case when date_diff('day', w.action_date, o.action_date) > 90
                  or ( o.action_date > date_add('day',30,w.line_start)
                       and cardinality(array_except(o.drugs, w.regimen)) > 0 )
                then o.action_date else w.line_start end,
           case when date_diff('day', w.action_date, o.action_date) > 90
                  or ( o.action_date > date_add('day',30,w.line_start)
                       and cardinality(array_except(o.drugs, w.regimen)) > 0 )
                then o.drugs
                when o.action_date <= date_add('day',30,w.line_start)
                then array_distinct(w.regimen || o.drugs)
                else w.regimen end
    from walk w join ordered o on o.ptid = w.ptid and o.rn = w.rn + 1
)
select ptid, action_date, line_no from walk;

-- LINE SUMMARY: regimen, line end, LAST PLATINUM date, platinum + maintenance --
create table b_line_summary as
with base as (
    select l.ptid, l.line_no,
           min(l.action_date)                                   as line_start,
           max(l.action_date)                                   as line_end,
           max(case when d.is_platinum then l.action_date end)  as last_platinum_date,
           max(case when d.is_platinum then 1 else 0 end) = 1   as is_platinum_line,
           array_distinct(array_agg(d.drug))                    as line_regimen
    from b_lines l
    join b_drug d on l.ptid = d.ptid and l.action_date = d.action_date
                 and d.exposure_strength >= 3          -- confirmed/likely only; exclude intent (strength 2)
    group by l.ptid, l.line_no
)
select b.*,
       ( b.line_no > 1
         and cardinality(array_except(b.line_regimen,
               (select array_agg(generic) from vs_drug where is_maintenance_candidate))) = 0
       ) as is_maintenance
from base b;

-- TRUE TREATMENT LINES (exclude maintenance), renumbered ---------------------
create table b_tx_lines as
select ptid, line_no,
       row_number() over (partition by ptid order by line_no) as tx_line_no
from b_line_summary
where is_maintenance = false;

-- NEOADJUVANT / ADJUVANT — NON-EXCLUSIVE flags (a patient with confirmed therapy
-- both BEFORE and AFTER surgery is counted in BOTH; clinically valid).
-- ADJUVANT is bounded to <=180 d post-surgery so late recurrence treatment isn't
-- mislabeled adjuvant (provisional window; confirm — optum-gsk-questions.md #18).
create table b_neoadj_adj as
select oc.ptid,
       max(case when s.surgery_date is not null and d.action_date <  s.surgery_date then 1 else 0 end) as neoadjuvant,
       max(case when s.surgery_date is not null and d.action_date >= s.surgery_date
                 and d.action_date <= date_add('day',180,s.surgery_date) then 1 else 0 end)            as adjuvant,
       max(case when s.surgery_date is null then 1 else 0 end)                                          as no_surgery
from b_total_oc oc
left join b_surgery s on oc.ptid = s.ptid
left join b_drug d   on oc.ptid = d.ptid and d.exposure_strength >= 3
group by oc.ptid;

-- OVARIAN progression events (EO-gated; tied to ovarian via key) -------------
create table b_progression_evt as
select b.ptid, a.note_date as progression_date
from {{schema_onc}}.onc_progression a   -- deployed table name (manual labels it onc_tumor_progression); date col = note_date
join {{schema_onc}}.onc_neoplasm_histology b on a.neoplasm_histology_key = b.neoplasm_histology_key
where (select use_onc from cohort_meta) = true
  and lower(a.progression) in ('progression','recurrence')
  and a.progression_temporal_status = 'current'
  and b.neoplasm_qualifier = 'actual' and b.neoplasm_temporal_status = 'current'
  and b.neoplasm_type in (select neoplasm_type from vs_neoplasm where tumour='ovarian');

-- PLATINUM SENSITIVITY — patients with a true 2L+ (>=2 NON-maintenance lines).
-- Anchor = last platinum administration in the platinum line PRECEDING the 2nd
-- treatment line (clinically standard). Threshold >183 days = sensitive.
-- OPEN (GSK): confirm anchor = preceding-line vs latest platinum exposure
-- (optum-gsk-questions.md #11/#12).
create table b_platinum_status as
with second_line as (                       -- original line_no of each patient's 2nd treatment line
    select ptid, line_no as second_orig from b_tx_lines where tx_line_no = 2
),
plat_ref as (                               -- platinum line before the 2nd treatment line
    select sl.ptid, max(ls.last_platinum_date) as plat_last_date
    from second_line sl
    join b_line_summary ls
      on ls.ptid = sl.ptid and ls.is_platinum_line = true and ls.line_no < sl.second_orig
    group by sl.ptid
),
firstprog as (                              -- first progression AFTER that platinum
    select p.ptid, p.plat_last_date, min(e.progression_date) as progression_after_plat
    from plat_ref p
    left join b_progression_evt e on e.ptid = p.ptid and e.progression_date > p.plat_last_date
    group by p.ptid, p.plat_last_date
)
select ptid, plat_last_date, progression_after_plat,
       date_diff('day', plat_last_date, progression_after_plat) as days_to_prog,
       case when progression_after_plat is null then 'no_progression_after_platinum'
            when date_diff('day', plat_last_date, progression_after_plat) > 183
            then 'platinum_sensitive' else 'platinum_resistant' end as platinum_status
from firstprog;
-- NOTE: progression_date is a NOTE-date proxy for the event (manual: note date != event date).

-- COUNT OUTPUT (tag with cohort id when re-run per definition) ----------------
select 'total_oc'                  as stratum, count(distinct ptid) as n from b_total_oc
union all select 'total_oc_histology_confirmed',
   case when (select use_onc from cohort_meta) = false then null    -- NULL = not assessable (non-EO)
        else count(distinct case when histology_confirmed=1 then ptid end) end
   from b_total_oc
union all select 'neoadjuvant (non-excl)', count(distinct ptid) from b_neoadj_adj where neoadjuvant=1
union all select 'adjuvant (non-excl)',    count(distinct ptid) from b_neoadj_adj where adjuvant=1
union all select '1L (>=1 line, cumulative)',  count(distinct ptid) from b_tx_lines
union all select '2L_plus (>=2 lines, cumulative)', count(distinct ptid) from (select ptid from b_tx_lines group by ptid having max(tx_line_no)>=2) z
union all select 'maintenance',     count(distinct ptid) from b_line_summary where is_maintenance=true
union all select 'platinum_sensitive', count(distinct ptid) from b_platinum_status where platinum_status='platinum_sensitive'
union all select 'platinum_resistant', count(distinct ptid) from b_platinum_status where platinum_status='platinum_resistant'
-- not-assessable 2L+ groups, so sensitive+resistant+no_progression+no_prior_platinum reconciles to total 2L+
union all select 'platinum_no_progression_observed', count(distinct ptid) from b_platinum_status where platinum_status='no_progression_after_platinum'
union all select 'platinum_no_prior_line (2L+)',
   count(distinct z.ptid) from (select ptid from b_tx_lines group by ptid having max(tx_line_no)>=2) z
   where z.ptid not in (select ptid from b_platinum_status);
>>>>> OPTUM-MC FILE: sql/reference_pipeline/code_lists.sql
-- ============================================================================
-- code_lists.sql — value-set lookup tables referenced by lib/cases
-- Run this FIRST. These replace the previously-inline illustrative IN-lists.
--
-- ⚠️ DRAFT value sets — require oncology/clinical sign-off before execution.
--    They are intentionally explicit so a clinician can review/extend them in
--    one place. ICD anchors are ICD-10-CM regex (confirm ICD-9 crosswalk via
--    manual appendix §4.8.4). HCPCS J-codes/CPT confirm against the licensed
--    procedure dictionary. Histology-dependent tumours (NSCLC/SCLC/HNSCC) need
--    the histology filter noted in vs_cancer_icd.histology_note.
-- ============================================================================

-- Idempotency: drop value sets first so a full top-to-bottom re-run doesn't fail
-- on "table already exists" (00_reset.sql intentionally leaves vs_* in place).
drop table if exists vs_cancer_icd;
drop table if exists vs_surgery;
drop table if exists vs_drug;
drop table if exists vs_biosimilar;
drop table if exists vs_biomarker_lab;
drop table if exists vs_neoplasm;
drop table if exists vs_histology;

-- 1) Cancer ICD anchors (one or more regex per tumour) -----------------------
-- The optional dot `\.?` matches BOTH dotted (C57.0) and undotted (C570) storage
-- formats — confirm diagnosis_cd format and adjust if needed.
create table vs_cancer_icd (tumour varchar, icd_regex varchar, histology_note varchar);
insert into vs_cancer_icd values
  ('colon',            '^C18|^C19|^C20', null),                       -- colon + rectosigmoid + rectum
  ('ovarian',          '^C56|^C57\.?0|^C48', null),                   -- ovary + fallopian + peritoneum
  ('endometrial',      '^C54|^C55', null),
  ('prostate',         '^C61', null),
  ('multiple_myeloma', '^C90\.?0', null),
  ('nsclc',            '^C34', 'restrict histology to non-small-cell'),
  ('sclc',             '^C34', 'restrict histology to small-cell'),
  ('hnscc',            '^C0[0-9]|^C1[0-4]|^C32', 'restrict histology to squamous cell'),
  ('sarcoma',          '^C4[01]|^C49', null),                          -- bone + soft tissue
  ('bladder',          '^C67', null),
  ('cervical',         '^C53', null),
  ('gastric',          '^C16', null),
  ('esophageal',       '^C15', 'ESCC = squamous histology'),
  ('pancreatic',       '^C25', 'PDAC = ductal adenocarcinoma'),
  ('dlbcl',            '^C83\.?3', null),
  ('glioblastoma',     '^C71', 'unmethylated MGMT = biomarker filter');

-- ============================================================================
-- lib_cancer_cohort.sql  —  Shared, parameterized building blocks
-- Run AFTER code_lists.sql. Adapted from Enriched Oncology User Guide §4.8.2.
--
-- STATUS: design-complete; execution SCAFFOLDED. On data access the analyst must
-- (a) wire the §0 per-cohort source placeholders to real tables (names unknown ->
-- optum-gsk-questions.md); (b) validate SQL dialect (caseB header); (c) confirm
-- onc_medications.source / action value spellings and whether onc_medications is
-- licensable for non-EO cohorts (else source structured meds from raw EHR/claims).
-- Dialect target: Trino/Athena-v3 (regexp_*, try_cast, date_diff, array_*).
-- ============================================================================

-- TUMOUR + COHORT parameters (set both per run) ------------------------------
create table params as select 'colon' as tumour, '4' as cohort;   -- e.g. 'ovarian','2b'

create table cohort_meta as
select (select cohort from params) in ('4')        as use_onc,     -- Enriched Oncology only in cohort 4
       (select cohort from params) in ('2b','3b')  as enrol_gate;  -- continuous-enrollment gate

-- ============================================================================
-- 0) COHORT SOURCE-LAYER SWITCH — REAL per-cohort selection (placeholders).
--    Each src_* UNIONs EHR with the claims layer THIS cohort sees, filtered by
--    params.cohort, then applies the enrollment gate (2b/3b) consistently.
--    Cohorts: 1 EHR-only · 2a/2b EHR+Optum · 3a/3b EHR+3P · 4 EHR+Optum+EO.
--    ⚠️ The UNIONs reference ALL placeholder tables STATICALLY (the cohort filter
--    only zeroes the rows, not the reference). So unused layers must still resolve:
--    provide empty stand-in tables for the claims layers a given cohort doesn't use,
--    OR split into per-cohort scripts. (Parse-time, not row-level, requirement.)
-- ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Enrollment source for the gate: Optum enrol for 2b, THIRD-PARTY enrol for 3b.
-- ⚠️ This gate is EVENT-LEVEL (keeps a row if its date is inside an enrollment span).
-- It does NOT yet enforce PATIENT-LEVEL continuous-observable eligibility across the
-- analytic window (washout -> follow-up). Define that window with GSK before using
-- 2b/3b as truly "strict" (optum-gsk-questions.md #16).
create table src_enrol as
select ptid, enrol_start, enrol_end from {{optum_clm_enrol}} where (select cohort from params) = '2b'
union all
select ptid, enrol_start, enrol_end from {{tp_clm_enrol}}    where (select cohort from params) = '3b';

create table src_diag as
with base as (
    select ptid, diagnosis_cd, diagnosis_cd_type, diagnosis_status, diag_date, 'ehr' src
    from {{ehr_diag}}
    union all
    select ptid, diagnosis_cd, diagnosis_cd_type, diagnosis_status, diag_date, 'optum_clm'
    from {{optum_clm_diag}} where (select cohort from params) in ('2a','2b','4')
    union all
    select ptid, diagnosis_cd, diagnosis_cd_type, diagnosis_status, diag_date, 'tp_clm'
    from {{tp_clm_diag}}    where (select cohort from params) in ('3a','3b')
)
select b.* from base b
where (select enrol_gate from cohort_meta) = false
   or exists (select 1 from src_enrol e where e.ptid=b.ptid and b.diag_date between e.enrol_start and e.enrol_end);

create table src_proc as
with base as (
    select ptid, proc_cd, proc_cd_type, proc_date from {{ehr_proc}}
    union all
    select ptid, proc_cd, proc_cd_type, proc_date from {{optum_clm_proc}} where (select cohort from params) in ('2a','2b','4')
    union all
    select ptid, proc_cd, proc_cd_type, proc_date from {{tp_clm_proc}}    where (select cohort from params) in ('3a','3b')
)
select b.* from base b
where (select enrol_gate from cohort_meta) = false
   or exists (select 1 from src_enrol e where e.ptid=b.ptid and b.proc_date between e.enrol_start and e.enrol_end);

create table src_lab as
with base as (
    select ptid, loinc, lab_name, result, result_date, collect_date, order_date from {{ehr_labs}}
    union all
    select ptid, loinc, lab_name, result, result_date, collect_date, order_date from {{optum_clm_labs}} where (select cohort from params) in ('2a','2b','4')
    union all
    select ptid, loinc, lab_name, result, result_date, collect_date, order_date from {{tp_clm_labs}}    where (select cohort from params) in ('3a','3b')
)
select b.* from base b
where (select enrol_gate from cohort_meta) = false
   or exists (select 1 from src_enrol e where e.ptid=b.ptid
              and coalesce(b.result_date,b.collect_date,b.order_date) between e.enrol_start and e.enrol_end);

-- Medications via the unified onc_medications.source tag (emulates the layers).
-- action normalized to lower-case. CONFIRM source/action spellings; and whether
-- onc_medications is available for non-EO cohorts (else use raw EHR/claims meds).
create table src_med as
with base as (
    select ptid, generic_desc, drug_name, trim(lower(action)) as action, action_date, date_from_note, route, trim(lower(source)) as source
    from {{schema_onc}}.onc_medications
    where trim(lower(source)) in ('proc','rx_admin','written prescriptions','medication administration')   -- EHR
       or (trim(lower(source)) in ('clm_rx','clm_med_serv')       and (select cohort from params) in ('2a','2b','4'))
       or (trim(lower(source)) in ('tp_clm_rx','tp_clm_med_serv') and (select cohort from params) in ('3a','3b'))
       or (trim(lower(source)) = 'provider notes'                 and (select use_onc from cohort_meta))     -- NLP -> cohort 4
)
select b.* from base b
where (select enrol_gate from cohort_meta) = false
   or exists (select 1 from src_enrol e where e.ptid=b.ptid and b.action_date between e.enrol_start and e.enrol_end);

-- ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- 1) Cancer cohort by ICD inclusion list (ONE tumour, from vs_cancer_icd)
-- ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
create table cohort_dx as
select s.ptid, min(s.diag_date) as first_dx_date, count(distinct s.diag_date) as n_dx_dates
from src_diag s
join vs_cancer_icd v on v.tumour = (select tumour from params)
where s.diagnosis_cd_type like 'ICD%'
  and s.diagnosis_status = 'Diagnosis of'
  and regexp_like(s.diagnosis_cd, v.icd_regex)
group by s.ptid
having count(distinct s.diag_date) >= 2;

-- ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- 2) Histology confirmation + concept hub — GATED by use_onc. EMPTY for cohorts
--    1-3 (no NLP histology/stage) — that empty propagation IS a feasibility finding.
--    Enforce *_qualifier='actual' on BOTH the neoplasm and histology match arms.
-- ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
create table cohort_neo_hist as
select distinct
    a.ptid, a.note_date, a.neoplasm_histology_key,
    a.neoplasm_type, a.neoplasm_qualifier, a.neoplasm_temporal_status,
    a.histology, a.histology_qualifier, a.neoplasm_dx_date
from {{schema_onc}}.onc_neoplasm_histology a
inner join cohort_dx d on a.ptid = d.ptid
left join (select histology_note from vs_cancer_icd where tumour=(select tumour from params)) hd on true
where (select use_onc from cohort_meta) = true
  and (
        -- histology-DEPENDENT target (NSCLC/SCLC/HNSCC/...): REQUIRE actual histology AND an
        -- actual target-SITE neoplasm anchored to the SAME EO concept (neoplasm_histology_key),
        -- not merely the same patient — otherwise a multi-primary patient could supply the
        -- target-site neoplasm from one primary and the matching histology from another.
        ( hd.histology_note is not null
          and a.histology_qualifier = 'actual'
          and a.histology in (select histology from vs_histology where tumour=(select tumour from params))
          and exists (select 1 from {{schema_onc}}.onc_neoplasm_histology a2
                      where a2.neoplasm_histology_key = a.neoplasm_histology_key
                        and a2.neoplasm_qualifier = 'actual'
                        and a2.neoplasm_type in (select neoplasm_type from vs_neoplasm where tumour=(select tumour from params))) )
        -- histology-INDEPENDENT target (colon/ovarian/...): neoplasm OR histology
     or ( hd.histology_note is null
          and ( ( a.neoplasm_qualifier = 'actual'
                  and a.neoplasm_type in (select neoplasm_type from vs_neoplasm where tumour=(select tumour from params)) )
             or ( a.histology_qualifier = 'actual'
                  and a.histology      in (select histology     from vs_histology where tumour=(select tumour from params)) ) ) )
      );

-- multi-primary detector (EO only) — flags patients with an actual neoplasm OUTSIDE
-- the target tumour's neoplasm set (i.e., a different-organ cancer). This is the
-- single-primary-OF-INTEREST test: same-organ sites (colon/rectum/rectosigmoid;
-- ovary/fallopian/peritoneum) are IN-set and do NOT trigger it, avoiding the
-- over-exclusion of `count(distinct neoplasm_type)>1`. Requires the target tumour's
-- vs_neoplasm list to be reasonably complete.
create table flag_multiprimary as
select distinct a.ptid
from {{schema_onc}}.onc_neoplasm_histology a
inner join cohort_dx d on a.ptid = d.ptid
where (select use_onc from cohort_meta) = true
  and a.neoplasm_qualifier = 'actual'
  and a.neoplasm_type not in (select neoplasm_type from vs_neoplasm where tumour = (select tumour from params));

-- ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- 3) Staging bundle keyed on neoplasm_histology_key (empty for non-EO cohorts)
-- ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
create table cohort_stage as
select
    h.ptid, h.neoplasm_histology_key, h.neoplasm_dx_date, h.note_date as stage_note_date,
    c.neoplasm_characteristic,
    s.stage_prefix, s.stage,
    t.t_prefix, t.t, t.t_suffix, t.n_prefix, t.n, t.n_suffix, t.m_prefix, t.m, t.m_suffix
from cohort_neo_hist h
left join {{schema_onc}}.onc_characteristic c on h.neoplasm_histology_key = c.neoplasm_histology_key
left join {{schema_onc}}.onc_stage         s on h.neoplasm_histology_key = s.neoplasm_histology_key
left join {{schema_onc}}.onc_tnm          t on h.neoplasm_histology_key = t.neoplasm_histology_key;

-- ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- 4) Reusable flags
-- ----------------------------------------------------------------------------

-- 4a) dMMR / MSI-H. Biomarker table is STAND-ALONE -> attribute by PTID + date-
--     after-dx + SINGLE-PRIMARY restriction. The restriction is applied to BOTH
--     the EO-biomarker and the structured-Labs arms. NOTE: flag_multiprimary is
--     EO-derived, so for non-EO cohorts multi-primary cannot be detected -> Labs
--     attribution there is best-effort; flag such patients for manual review.
create table flag_dmmr_msi as
select ptid, min(evt_date) as dmmr_first_date from (
    select b.ptid, b.note_date as evt_date
    from {{schema_onc}}.onc_biomarker b
    join cohort_dx d on b.ptid = d.ptid
    left join flag_multiprimary mp on b.ptid = mp.ptid
    where (select use_onc from cohort_meta) = true
      and mp.ptid is null
      and b.note_date >= d.first_dx_date
      and ( ( upper(b.biomarker) like '%MSI%' and lower(b.narrative_result) like '%high%' )
         or ( regexp_like(upper(b.biomarker),'MLH1|MSH2|MSH6|PMS2')
              and lower(b.narrative_result) in ('mutation','loss','deficient','positive') ) )
    union all
    select l.ptid, coalesce(l.result_date, l.collect_date, l.order_date) as evt_date
    from src_lab l
    join cohort_dx d on l.ptid = d.ptid
    left join flag_multiprimary mp on l.ptid = mp.ptid          -- same single-primary restriction
    join vs_biomarker_lab vb on (l.loinc = vb.loinc or lower(l.lab_name) like lower(vb.lab_name_pattern))
    where mp.ptid is null
      and vb.concept in ('MSI','MMR_MLH1','MMR_MSH2','MMR_MSH6','MMR_PMS2')
      and ( lower(l.result) like '%high%' or lower(l.result) in ('deficient','loss','mutation','positive') )
      and coalesce(l.result_date, l.collect_date, l.order_date) >= d.first_dx_date
) group by ptid;

-- 4b) DISTANT metastasis only: C78-C79 (EXCLUDE C77 regional) + keyed mets (EO-gated)
create table flag_mets as
select ptid, min(mets_date) as first_mets_date from (
    select ptid, diag_date as mets_date
    from src_diag
    where diagnosis_cd_type like 'ICD%' and diagnosis_status = 'Diagnosis of'
      and regexp_like(diagnosis_cd, '^C7[89]')
    union all
    select ml.ptid, ml.metastasis_dx_date as mets_date
    from {{schema_onc}}.onc_metastasis_location ml
    inner join cohort_neo_hist h on ml.neoplasm_histology_key = h.neoplasm_histology_key  -- empty if non-EO
    where ml.mets_qualifier = 'actual' and ml.metastasis_dx_date is not null
) group by ptid;

-- 4c) Drug exposure: action-strength hierarchy + biosimilar canonicalization.
--     first_confirmed_date = first date at strength>=3 (NOT first intent date).
--     src_med.action is already lower-cased. Optum drops implied/relative dates,
--     so all retained rows are usable (manual §4.7.5) — no date_from_note filter.
create table flag_drug as
select ptid, drug as generic_desc,
       min(action_date)                                   as first_action_date,
       min(case when strength >= 3 then action_date end)  as first_confirmed_date,
       max(strength)                                      as exposure_strength
from (
    select coalesce(bs.canonical_generic, upper(trim(m.generic_desc))) as drug,
           m.ptid, m.action_date,
           case m.action when 'administered' then 4 when 'filled' then 4
                         when 'started' then 3 when 'is on' then 3
                         when 'prescribed' then 2 else 1 end as strength
    from src_med m
    left join vs_biosimilar bs on upper(trim(m.generic_desc)) = bs.source_name   -- canonicalize FIRST
    where m.action not in ('stopped','suspended')
) x
group by ptid, drug;
>>>>> OPTUM-MC FILE: sql/reference_pipeline/priority_tumour_counts.sql
-- ============================================================================
-- priority_tumour_counts.sql — patient counts per GSK priority tumour
-- Run after code_lists.sql + lib (section 0 src_diag for the chosen cohort).
-- Re-run per cohort definition (params.cohort) to fill the matrix columns.
--   n_identified            = >=2 ICD diagnosis dates (all cohorts)
--   n_histology_confirmed   = identified AND NLP neoplasm/histology match
--                             (only meaningful for use_onc cohort 4)
-- Tumours with NO Enriched-Oncology term mapping show histology_confirmed = NULL
-- (not 0) and a note, so they are not misread as true zeros.
-- ============================================================================

create table priority_tumour_counts as
with dx as (
    select v.tumour, s.ptid, count(distinct s.diag_date) as n_dates
    from src_diag s
    join vs_cancer_icd v on regexp_like(s.diagnosis_cd, v.icd_regex)
    where s.diagnosis_cd_type like 'ICD%' and s.diagnosis_status = 'Diagnosis of'
    group by v.tumour, s.ptid
),
identified as (
    select tumour, count(distinct ptid) as n_identified
    from dx where n_dates >= 2 group by tumour
),
mapped as (                         -- tumours that HAVE EO neoplasm/histology terms
    select distinct tumour from vs_neoplasm
    union select distinct tumour from vs_histology
),
confirmed as (                      -- histology-confirmed (EO-gated)
    select dx.tumour, count(distinct h.ptid) as n
    from dx
    join {{schema_onc}}.onc_neoplasm_histology h on h.ptid = dx.ptid and dx.n_dates >= 2
    left join (select distinct tumour, histology_note from vs_cancer_icd) hd on hd.tumour = dx.tumour
    where (select use_onc from cohort_meta) = true        -- only meaningful for cohort 4
      and (
            -- histology-DEPENDENT tumours (NSCLC/SCLC/HNSCC/...): REQUIRE an actual histology
            -- match AND an actual target-SITE neoplasm on the SAME EO concept
            -- (neoplasm_histology_key), not merely the same patient — so squamous histology
            -- from a DIFFERENT primary can't confirm, and 'lung' can't confirm both NSCLC and
            -- SCLC. (Tumours lacking vs_neoplasm site terms stay not-confirmable.)
            ( hd.histology_note is not null
              and h.histology_qualifier = 'actual'
              and exists (select 1 from vs_histology vh where vh.tumour = dx.tumour and vh.histology = h.histology)
              and exists (select 1 from {{schema_onc}}.onc_neoplasm_histology h2
                          join vs_neoplasm vn on vn.tumour = dx.tumour and vn.neoplasm_type = h2.neoplasm_type
                          where h2.neoplasm_histology_key = h.neoplasm_histology_key and h2.neoplasm_qualifier = 'actual') )
            -- histology-INDEPENDENT tumours: neoplasm OR histology match is sufficient
         or ( hd.histology_note is null
              and ( ( h.neoplasm_qualifier  = 'actual'
                      and exists (select 1 from vs_neoplasm vn where vn.tumour = dx.tumour and vn.neoplasm_type = h.neoplasm_type) )
                 or ( h.histology_qualifier = 'actual'
                      and exists (select 1 from vs_histology vh where vh.tumour = dx.tumour and vh.histology = h.histology) ) ) )
          )
    group by dx.tumour
)
select i.tumour, i.n_identified,
       -- WARNING: for histology_dependent tumours, n_identified is a BROAD ICD count
       -- (e.g. NSCLC & SCLC are both ^C34 lung; HNSCC is a site range) — the true
       -- histology-specific count needs EO histology (n_histology_confirmed, cohort 4).
       (v.histology_note is not null) as histology_dependent,
       case when (select use_onc from cohort_meta) = false then null   -- non-EO: not assessable
            when m.tumour is null then null
            else coalesce(c.n, 0) end as n_histology_confirmed,
       case when (select use_onc from cohort_meta) = false then 'non-EO cohort: histology-confirmed not assessable'
            when m.tumour is null then 'no EO term mapping — extend vs_neoplasm/vs_histology'
            when v.histology_note is not null then v.histology_note end as note
from identified i
left join mapped m    on i.tumour = m.tumour
left join confirmed c on i.tumour = c.tumour
left join (select distinct tumour, histology_note from vs_cancer_icd) v on i.tumour = v.tumour
order by i.n_identified desc;

select * from priority_tumour_counts;
>>>>> OPTUM-MC END

"""
Unpack optum_mc_bundle.txt (PLAIN TEXT - no base64) back into files.

Usage:
    python unpack.py [bundle.txt] [output_dir]

Defaults: bundle = optum_mc_bundle.txt, output_dir = current directory.

The bundle is human-readable: each file's content sits between a
'>>>>> OPTUM-MC FILE: <path>' marker line and the next marker. This unpacker:
  * reads the bundle as UTF-8, tolerating a leading BOM (utf-8-sig) and LF, CRLF or
    CR line endings - so a file that's been through a Windows editor still unpacks;
  * writes every file with LF line endings, UTF-8 (so a CRLF-converted bundle still
    produces clean files - nothing is doubled or mangled), preserving non-ASCII text;
  * rejects absolute paths and '..' segments (writes only under output_dir).

Pure standard library; works on Python 3.x. No base64, no binary blobs.
"""
import sys, os

MARK = ">>>>> OPTUM-MC FILE: "
END  = ">>>>> OPTUM-MC END"


def main():
    bundle  = sys.argv[1] if len(sys.argv) > 1 else "optum_mc_bundle.txt"
    out_dir = sys.argv[2] if len(sys.argv) > 2 else "."

    if not os.path.isfile(bundle):
        print("ERROR: bundle not found:", bundle)
        sys.exit(1)

    with open(bundle, "r", encoding="utf-8-sig") as fh:   # utf-8-sig strips a BOM if Windows added one
        lines = fh.read().splitlines()      # handles \n, \r\n and \r

    path, buf, count = None, [], 0

    def flush():
        nonlocal count
        if path is None:
            return
        parts = [p for p in path.replace("\\", "/").split("/") if p not in ("", ".")]
        if os.path.isabs(path) or any(p == ".." for p in parts) or not parts:
            print("  SKIP unsafe path:", path)
            return
        target = os.path.join(out_dir, *parts)
        parent = os.path.dirname(target)
        if parent:
            os.makedirs(parent, exist_ok=True)
        content = "\n".join(buf) + "\n"                 # LF endings + trailing newline
        with open(target, "wb") as out:                # binary -> no platform translation
            out.write(content.encode("utf-8"))
        print("  wrote", target, "(%d lines)" % len(buf))
        count += 1

    for line in lines:
        if line.startswith(MARK):
            flush()
            path = line[len(MARK):].strip()
            buf = []
        elif line.strip() == END:
            flush()
            path = None
        elif path is not None:
            buf.append(line)

    print("done: %d files into %s" % (count, os.path.abspath(out_dir)))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
